# Gavelling UI audit

1089 findings across 12 areas. Flat list, no severity tiers. Each entry: what is wrong, then the concrete fix.


---

# Delegate, advisor and voting views  
_85 findings_


## Delegate — Session tab

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1382`** — The single fact a nervous delegate opens the app for — "how close am I to speaking?" — is a `text-xs font-bold` span tucked into the right side of the speakers-list card header (`✓ After {n} speakers`). Meanwhile their own flag at 120×86px and their own country name at `text-3xl` (line 1281-1282) dominate the top of the screen, and they already know both. AGENTS.md describes a permanent 4-state colour-coded floor card (grey/amber/yellow/green); no such card exists in the current code. → _Promote queue position to the top of the Session tab as a full-width card with the number at ~48–56px: "3 SPEAKERS UNTIL YOU" / "YOU'RE UP NEXT" / "YOU HAVE THE FLOOR", tinted forest→amber→gold as the delegate approaches. Demote the identity flag to a 40px chip beside the committee name._  `[hierarchy]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1280`** — The delegate's own country is rendered three times on one 820px-tall screen: the 120×86 flag + `text-3xl` name (1281-1282), the highlighted `bg-[#1B3828]/20` row inside the speakers list (1393), and again in the "YOUR DELEGATION" card with a 36px flag + name + status (1408-1419). Roughly 220px of vertical space says "you are France" three times. → _Keep one identity surface. Fold status (Present / P+V) and the status toggle into the same block as the flag chip, and delete the separate "YOUR DELEGATION" card._  `[density]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1311`** — The "SESSION STATUS" card renders `phaseDisplay` at `text-2xl font-black` — during the GSL that reads "General Speakers' List", which is immediately repeated 40px lower as the `SPEAKERS LIST` card header (line 1362). Two headings for the same state stacked on top of each other. → _Drop the phase text when `phase === 'speakers-list'` (the queue card itself is the label), or merge the status card and speakers-list card into one panel with a single heading._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1316`** — During the GSL the delegate sees "NOW SPEAKING · France" with no clock at all, while the moderated-caucus view shows `formatTime(remainingTime)` at `text-3xl` (1225) and the unmoderated view at `text-5xl` (1264). A delegate two places back has no way to judge whether they have 20 seconds or 3 minutes before they need to stand up. → _Show the current speaker's remaining time on the GSL card too — even a coarse read-only bar or a `mm:ss` at `text-2xl` — so the same information exists in all three phases at a consistent size._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1375`** — "+ REQUEST TO SPEAK" — the primary delegate action of the whole app — is a `text-xs px-3 py-1.5` chip in a card header, roughly 26px tall. Below the 44px minimum tap target, and visually subordinate to the card's own label. → _Make it a full-width button under the queue, min-height 44px, `text-sm font-black` on `NEU.forest` with the gold text treatment used by the primary CTA elsewhere._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1425`** — The Present / Present & Voting toggle buttons are `py-2 text-xs` ≈ 32px tall — under the 44px touch minimum on the screen 30+ phone users tap most, and the same size as the tertiary "Request again" chip. → _Raise to `py-3` (44px) and use the same two-state pill treatment as the roll-call slider in RollCallPanel so the pattern is learned once._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1359`** — The whole speakers-list card, including the Request to Speak button, is gated on `committee.phase === 'speakers-list'`. During `pre-session` / `roll-call` — exactly when everyone wants to claim an early GSL slot — the delegate sees no queue and has no way to request one. They must watch for the phase to flip and race. → _Render the queue (read-only) and the Request to Speak button during pre-session/roll-call too, queuing the request for the chair; or show "Requests open when the session begins" instead of hiding the card entirely._  `[wasted-effort]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1368`** — The GSL-denied message uses Tailwind `text-red-400` (#f87171) on the `#EDE7D8` card — about 2.6:1, below AA for 12px text — while every other error in the file uses `#8B2020`. Three different reds are in play on this page (#8B2020, #f87171, `hover:text-red-400`). → _Standardise on #8B2020 for all delegate-facing error text and remove `text-red-400` / `hover:text-red-400`._  `[contrast]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1452`** — "{n} status change{s} remaining (resets after 3 hrs)" is always visible, including at 3/3 when it is pure noise. When it reaches 0 the two buttons go grey and unclickable with no explanation of *when* the allowance returns — the delegate reads it as broken. → _Hide the line at 3/3, show it in amber at 1, and at 0 replace the buttons with "You've used your 3 status changes. Ask a chair, or try again after {time}." using a real timestamp rather than a rolling window._  `[copy]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1208`** — Inside a moderated caucus the delegate's own flag renders as `<FlagImg size={88} className="rounded-full shadow-lg" />`. FlagImg forces `width:88;height:88;objectFit:contain`, so a 3:2 flag is letterboxed into a square and then clipped to a circle — the flag's left and right edges are cut off and there are transparent crescents top and bottom inside the drop shadow. Outside a caucus the very same flag renders as a proper `<img>` at 120×86, `objectFit:cover`, `borderRadius:10px` (line 1281). → _Use one flag component with a 3:2 aspect box and `objectFit:cover` everywhere. Never circle-clip a rectangular flag._  `[consistency]`

## Delegate — Session tab (voting phase)

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1297`** — `<p className="text-base font-bold text-white">{activeDoc.title}</p>` sits inside a card whose `backgroundColor` is `#FAF8F3`. The resolution title being voted on is white-on-near-white — completely invisible. The sibling lines also use `text-[#7B4A1E]` and `text-[#7A5A38]`, two browns that exist nowhere in the NEU palette. → _Set the title to `NEU.ink` (#1C1410), the doc code to `NEU.forest` (#1B3828) and the helper line to `NEU.muted` (#9A8A78). Delete #7B4A1E and #7A5A38._  `[contrast]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1301`** — `delegate_vote_desc` reads "Your chair is conducting the vote. Check the voting screen for results." Delegates have no voting screen — /voting/[code] is chair-gated. The copy sends 30 people looking for a page they cannot open. → _Rewrite to describe what actually happens: "Your chairs are running the vote at the dais. The result will appear here when it's called."_  `[copy]`

## Delegate — absent state

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1278`** — An absent delegate gets two completely different products depending on a chair setting they cannot see: with `requireChairApproval` on, a full-screen forest Waiting Room with a 104×74 flag and a gold CTA (1058-1113); with it off, the ordinary session dimmed to `opacity-60 pointer-events-none select-none` with a small ivory banner. The dimmed variant reads as a broken page, not a locked one. → _Use one absent state. Keep the Waiting Room design in both cases and just swap the copy/CTA ("Mark yourself present" vs "Request a seat"), rather than dimming a live screen._  `[consistency]`

## Delegate / advisor / voting — all flag lists

- **`src/components/FlagImg.tsx:16`** — FlagImg hardcodes a square box (`width: size, height: size`) with `objectFit: 'contain'`. Every 3:2 flag therefore fills only the middle ~67% of its box, so a `size={20}` flag paints 20×13 with 3.5px of dead space above and below, and sits visually high against the text baseline in every list row. → _Give FlagImg a `width = size, height = Math.round(size / 1.5)` 3:2 box with `objectFit:'cover'` and a 3px radius, matching the raw `<img>` treatment already used on the same screens._  `[polish]`

## Delegate — moderated vs unmoderated caucus

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1225`** — The identical piece of information — caucus time remaining — is `text-3xl` in the moderated view (1225) and `text-5xl` in the unmoderated view (1264). The two cards also differ in padding (`p-5` vs `p-6`) and one has `space-y-3` while the other doesn't. → _Extract a single `<CaucusClockCard>` used by both phases; one size, one padding, one progress-bar treatment._  `[consistency]`

## Delegate — moderated caucus

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1237`** — `committee.caucusQueue.slice(0, 8)` silently truncates the upcoming-speakers list with no "+N more" indicator. A delegate at position 9 sees the queue and concludes they are not on it. → _Append a muted row "+{caucusQueue.length - 8} more" whenever the list is truncated, and always show the reader's own row even if it falls past index 8._  `[missing-state]`

## Delegate — caucus progress bars

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1229`** — `className="h-full rounded-full transition-all"` — `transition-all` on an element whose width changes every second animates every animatable property, including layout-affecting ones, and fights the 1s tick. Three instances (1229, 1268, 1341). → _Replace with `transition: width 1000ms linear` so the bar drains smoothly and nothing else animates._  `[polish]`

## Delegate — header

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1134`** — The tab buttons carry `transition-all duration-200` and their hover handlers imperatively set `el.style.transform = 'translateY(-1px)'`, so the browser transitions colour, background *and* transform together. A tab that lifts 1px on hover in a 44px bar also nudges the 2px gold underline out of alignment. → _Use `transition: background-color 200ms, color 200ms` and drop the translateY entirely; the background tint is already the hover signal._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1135`** — The active tab is signalled four ways at once: colour change (#1C1410→#1B3828), weight 700→900, a `rgba(27,56,40,0.07)` background block, and a 2px #B6871F gold underline. Three of the four are nearly invisible; the gold underline alone would do it. → _Keep the gold underline + weight 900 and drop the background tint and colour shift, so the underline reads instantly across a room._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1140`** — The chat unread badge is a fixed `w-4 h-4` (16px) circle with `text-[10px]`. A count of 10 or more overflows the circle; there is no `99+` cap. → _Use a pill (`min-w-[18px] h-[18px] px-1 rounded-full`) and clamp the label to `99+`._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1149`** — Tapping the committee-code chip calls `navigator.clipboard.writeText` with no visual acknowledgement at all — no "Copied", no icon swap, no colour flash. Users tap it repeatedly assuming it failed. (It is also unclear why a delegate needs to copy the code they already used to join.) → _Swap the label to "COPIED" for 1.2s on success, or drop the copy affordance from the delegate header entirely and keep the code as plain text._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1011`** — Two parallel translation-key sets exist for the same four tab labels: the (unused) `tabs` array at 1011 uses `delegate_tab_session/documents/chat/stats`, while the header that actually renders (line 1124) uses `delegate_session_tab / delegate_documents_tab / tab_chat / delegate_stats_tab`. A translator who fixes one set changes nothing on screen. → _Delete the dead `tabs` array and the orphaned `delegate_tab_*` keys, keeping one set._  `[consistency]`

## Delegate — Documents tab

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:649`** — `docsSection` defaults to `'submit'`, so opening Documents lands on a blank submission form. Reading what is on the floor is a far more common action than submitting (every delegate reads, a handful submit), and the reader has to make an extra tap every time. → _Default to `'view'` and put "View" first in the sub-tab bar; make "Submit" the second tab._  `[wasted-effort]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:247`** — `DelegateDocCard` — the only component that renders `documents_status_approved` / `documents_status_rejected` badges — is defined but never rendered anywhere in `src/`. With `requireDocApproval` on, a delegate whose working paper the chair rejected sees the card sitting at "Submitted" forever, with no notification path. → _Either render `DelegateDocCard` in the View sub-tab in place of the two hand-rolled card blocks at lines 1522 and 1554, or delete it and add the approved/rejected badge to those blocks. Right now the approval feature is invisible to the people it is about._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1560`** — Working-paper cards render a badge only when `doc.status === 'submitted'`; a WP that is on-floor, introduced, passed or failed shows no badge at all. Draft-resolution cards in the section directly above render four distinct badges for exactly those states. Same object, two lifecycles. → _Use one status-badge function for both types (the one already written inside `DelegateDocCard`), so WPs and DRs read identically._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:379`** — The attach button doubles as the filename display: `{uploading ? '⏳ …' : fileName ? `📎 ${fileName}` : t(...)}`. A 60-character PDF name stretches the button across the card with no truncation, and the upload state is a bare hourglass emoji with no progress or file-size feedback. → _Keep the button label fixed ("Attach PDF"), render the chosen file as a separate truncating chip underneath with a × remove control, and show a determinate or at least labelled progress state ("Uploading… " + spinner) instead of ⏳._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:404`** — The submit button's disabled style (`#DDD4C0` on `#9A8A78`) is applied identically for four different reasons — empty title, sending, uploading, limit reached — so "I haven't typed a title" and "the server is working" look the same. There is no focus ring (`focus:outline-none` with no replacement). → _Keep the disabled fill only for "nothing to submit"; for sending/uploading keep the forest fill with a spinner and the label "Submitting…". Add `:focus-visible { box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(182,135,31,0.45) }`._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:339`** — The sub-tab already says "SUBMIT DOCUMENT" and the panel immediately repeats it as an `<h2 className="text-lg font-black">` (`delegate_doc_submit_heading`). Two identical headings 40px apart at the top of a short form. → _Drop the h2 and start the panel at the type selector; the sub-tab is the heading._  `[density]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:321`** — `autoDocCode` assigns the document code ("WP 1.3") only at submit time; the delegate never sees what their paper will be called, so they cannot reference it in a speech they are about to give. → _Show the code that will be assigned as a read-only chip beside the Title field ("Will be filed as WP 1.3"), recomputed as the type toggles._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Delegate — Documents / co-sponsors

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:213`** — The co-sponsor typeahead renders as `absolute top-full left-0 right-0 … z-20` inside the form card, which lives inside the page's `flex-1 overflow-y-auto` column (line 1190). When the input sits low in the scroll viewport the suggestion list is clipped by the scroll container, and there is no upward flip. AGENTS.md UI RULE requires every floating layer to go through `@/components/Portal` at fixed coordinates with edge-flipping, per the applications `PaymentMenu`. → _Port the dropdown to `@/components/Portal`, position from the input's `getBoundingClientRect()` (via the same `anchorBox`/`place` helpers VotingRulesPanel already has for the FitToScreen transform), reposition on capture-phase scroll + resize, and flip upward near the bottom edge._  `[rule-violation]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:213`** — The dropdown carries both `overflow-hidden` and `overflow-y-auto` on the same element, and the list is double-truncated: `matches.slice(0, 8)` *and* `max-h-40` (160px ≈ 4.5 rows). A delegate typing "United" sees at most 8 matches, half of them behind a scroll they cannot see. → _Remove `overflow-hidden`, drop the `slice(0, 8)` cap, and let `max-h-64` + a visible scrollbar handle the length — matching the chat DM picker described in AGENTS.md._  `[polish]`

## Delegate — Stats tab

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:544`** — The leaderboard is headed `COMMITTEE SPEAKING TIME`, is sorted purely by seconds spoken, and truncated to the top 10 by time — yet it carries a "Pts" column, and `pointsByCountry` is only computed for those same 10. A delegation with the highest score but modest speaking time never appears in the points column at all, and a delegate reading row 1 concludes that delegation is "winning". → _Either drop the Pts column and keep it an honest speaking-time board, or add a Time/Points toggle that re-sorts and recomputes points across all delegations._  `[copy]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:518`** — The three-up stat row shows a big `#{myRank}` under the label "Rank by time", sitting immediately beneath a `text-6xl` score number. Two different rankings (points and seconds) are presented as one status, and "Rank by time" is easily misread as overall rank. → _Label it "Rank by speaking time" in full, or replace it with rank by score so the headline number and the rank agree._  `[copy]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:482`** — `<span className="text-6xl font-black">{total}</span>` gives a raw number with no context — no scale, no committee average, no delta since last check. "47 pts" tells a nervous delegate nothing about whether they are doing well. → _Pair the number with a peer comparison the page already has the data for: "47 pts · committee average 31" or a thin bar against the top score, using `NEU.green` for above-average and `NEU.muted` below._  `[hierarchy]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:528`** — Speaking-history rows print the raw enum with `l.context.replace(/-/g, ' ')` + `capitalize`, so delegates read "Speakers list" / "Moderated caucus" / "Unmoderated caucus" — untranslated in every locale, and inconsistent with the chair's renamed motion labels that `motionNames()` already resolves on this page. → _Map `context` through the existing `mn` motion names / `PHASE_LABEL` table so the row says whatever the chairs call it, in the delegate's language._  `[copy]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:529`** — Individual speech durations are printed as raw seconds (`{l.seconds}s` → "217s") while the total two rows above uses `formatTime()` ("3:37") and the leaderboard uses `formatTime()` again. Same quantity, three formats on one screen. → _Run every duration through `formatTime`, and add `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` so the column aligns._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:562`** — The leaderboard bar width is `(r.seconds / ranking[0].seconds) * 100%`. If the top speaker has 0 seconds (possible when the ledger contains only non-speech events), every bar computes `NaN%` and the row collapses. → _Guard with `ranking[0].seconds || 1`, and render an explicit "No speeches recorded yet" state for the whole card rather than a row of empty tracks._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:475`** — All five Stats cards (Score, Speaking history, Leaderboard, Chair recap, Tips) use exactly the same chrome: `rounded-2xl` + `1px #DDD4C0` border + a `#1B3828` header bar with a 4px gold tick and `text-xs font-mono tracking-widest` gold label. Nothing signals that the score is the headline and the tips are secondary — the page reads as five equal slabs. → _Give the score card a larger, distinct treatment (forest gradient fill, `NEU.out` shadow, `borderRadius: 22`) and demote the rest to plain `NEU.surface` cards with a small `NEU.ink` heading and no forest bar._  `[hierarchy]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:611`** — Coaching tips render as plain `→` arrows + 14px `#6A5A4A` text with no ordering signal, no icon and no indication that they are ranked by the gap in the delegate's own ledger (which `selectDelegateTips` works hard to compute). → _Number them 1–3, lead each with the category it addresses as a small gold caps label ("SPEAKING", "DOCUMENTS"), and show the points at stake — the selector already knows `s.value`._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:444`** — `getDelegateFeedback(...).then(setRecap)` has no loading or empty state — the Chair Recap card simply pops into existence mid-scroll once the promise resolves, shifting everything below it. `src/components/Skeleton.tsx` exists precisely for this and is used nowhere in the delegate, advisor or voting trees. → _Render `SectionCardSkeleton` in the recap card's slot while the fetch is in flight so the layout height is stable._  `[missing-state]`

## Delegate — Waiting Room / Adjourned

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1108`** — Both the Waiting Room (1108) and the suspended screen (1052) print `delegate_adjourned_esc` = "Press ESC to return to main menu", but the ESC handler at line 844 early-returns unless `sessionEnded || sessionSuspended`. In the Waiting Room neither is true, so pressing ESC does nothing. The instruction is dead in the state where it is most prominent. → _Either register the ESC handler for the waiting-room state as well, or replace the hint with a real "Leave session" link on that screen._  `[copy]`

## Delegate — Session ended

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:865`** — `setHoursRemaining(Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 3600000)))`. With the 1-hour deletion window this reads "0 hours until committee is deleted" within seconds of the gavel. The chair page computes the same value as `Math.max(1, Math.ceil(...))` (chair/[code]/page.tsx:2097) and says "1 hour". Two people on the same committee see contradictory countdowns. → _Use `Math.max(1, Math.ceil(...))` here to match the chair, or better, render minutes under an hour ("48 minutes until this committee is deleted")._  `[copy]`

## Delegate — access gates

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:907`** — The sign-in gate (907-916), the allocation-denied gate (925-927) and the not-found screen (937-939) are entirely hardcoded English on a product with four locales including RTL Arabic. They also disagree stylistically: the not-found heading uses `t()` but its body does not, the CTA labels are "SIGN IN" / "BACK TO HOME" / "TRY AGAIN", and the body copy uses a raw `"` around the code. → _Move all six strings into translations.ts, standardise the CTA to one label per destination, and use typographic quotes (or drop the quotes and render the code as a mono chip)._  `[copy]`

## Delegate — absent banner

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1019`** — `const AbsentBanner = () => (...)` is declared inside the render body, so React sees a brand-new component type on every render and remounts the banner. The `animate-spin` waiting indicator inside it restarts from 0° on every realtime tick — the spinner visibly stutters while the delegate waits for approval. → _Hoist `AbsentBanner` to module scope and pass what it needs as props (the same fix already applied to `RollCallModal` and `VotingHeader` in the voting page, for the same reason)._  `[polish]`

## Delegate — first render

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:639`** — `const country = searchParams.get('country') || ''`. If the query param is missing or names a delegation not on the roster, the page renders normally with a blank country name, no flag, `myDelegate === undefined`, and drops the user into a Waiting Room for a seat that does not exist. No error is ever shown. → _Add an early guard: if `!country` or `!committee.delegates.some(d => d.country === country)`, render a "That delegation isn't in this committee" screen with a link back to /join._  `[missing-state]`

## Advisor — delegate grid

- **`src/app/advisor/[code]/page.tsx:621`** — The same delegate is drawn two completely different ways depending on whether anything is selected: `NormalDelegateCard` is a full-width forest row with flag + name + queue position + status letter, and `CollapsedDelegateCard` is an 80×80 forest square with a 36×26 flag and a 9px name. Selecting one delegate re-renders all the others as a different object. → _Keep one card shape and just shrink the grid columns when the detail pane opens (e.g. from 4 columns to 2), so the advisor never has to re-find a delegation they were tracking._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/advisor/[code]/page.tsx:621`** — An advisor typically watches one or two delegations from their own school, but the grid is a flat alphabetical list of 20–40 with no search, no filter and no way to pin. Finding "their" delegation means scanning every render. → _Add a search input above the grid and a pin/star that hoists chosen delegations into a "Watching" row at the top — persisted in localStorage per committee code._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Advisor — collapsed delegate tile

- **`src/app/advisor/[code]/page.tsx:210`** — `text-[9px]` country label, `truncate`, inside an 80px tile. "Democratic Republic of the Congo" becomes about four legible characters, and 9px on `#1B3828` is under any reasonable legibility floor. → _Raise to 10.5–11px, allow two lines with `line-clamp-2`, and add a `title` attribute; or drop the label and rely on the flag plus a tooltip._  `[contrast]`

## Advisor — expanded delegate card

- **`src/app/advisor/[code]/page.tsx:121`** — The expanded card is ivory (`bg-[#EDE7D8]` with a `#1B3828`/60 border) while every other delegate representation on the page — the collapsed tiles, the normal rows, the entire left rail — is forest `#1B3828` with ivory text. The selected item is the one thing that inverts, so selection reads as "this one broke" rather than "this one is focused". → _Keep the expanded card forest with the ivory/gold text system and signal focus with a gold ring (`0 0 0 2px #B6871F`) plus elevation, matching the `active` NeuStatTile pattern in neu.tsx._  `[consistency]`

## Advisor — flags

- **`src/app/advisor/[code]/page.tsx:82`** — Five hand-rolled flag `<img>` treatments in one file: 96×68 r10 with a `rgba(28,20,16,0.10)` border (82), 36×26 r4 with a `rgba(255,255,255,0.1)` border (197), 32×22 r4 with `rgba(28,20,16,0.15)` (244), 80×58 r8 with `rgba(238,217,138,0.2)` (530/576), 24×17 r3 with `rgba(255,255,255,0.1)` (604). The two white-alpha borders are invisible on their backgrounds. `FlagImg` is never used on this page. → _Replace all five with one shared flag component taking a `size` prop and deriving radius/border from it, so the border colour is chosen once per surface tone rather than per call site._  `[consistency]`

## Advisor — header

- **`src/app/advisor/[code]/page.tsx:489`** — The advisor header renders a raw `<img src="/GavellingLogo.png" className="w-[150px] h-auto max-h-9">` while the delegate and voting headers both use `<SessionsHeaderLogo />` (128×32 derived from the 4:1 natural ratio). The advisor logo is 150px wide against 128px elsewhere, and the wrapping `<Link>` has no `aria-label` and no `shrink-0`. → _Swap in `<SessionsHeaderLogo />`, which already handles ratio, priority loading, the error fallback and the aria-label._  `[consistency]`

## Advisor — delegate row

- **`src/app/advisor/[code]/page.tsx:252`** — `advisor_queue_position_display` is `'{n}.° up'` in the English table (translations.ts:741), so an advisor reads "3.° up" — a Spanish ordinal indicator leaked into the English string. The expanded card's equivalent (`advisor_next_up`) says "Next up: #{n} in queue", a third phrasing for the same fact. → _Set the English key to `'#{n} in queue'` and use the same phrasing in `advisor_next_up` so the row and the detail card agree._  `[copy]`

## Advisor — adjourned screen

- **`src/app/advisor/[code]/page.tsx:441`** — The adjourned screen prints "Press ESC to return to main menu", but the advisor page registers no keydown handler anywhere (`grep keydown` returns nothing). The only instruction on an otherwise empty screen does nothing. → _Add the ESC handler, or replace the line with a visible "Back to sessions" button._  `[copy]`

## Advisor — left rail

- **`src/app/advisor/[code]/page.tsx:512`** — Three `MajorityPie` donuts render side by side as 32px rings with bare numbers ("24", "16", "13") and no legend, label, or `title` attribute anywhere. Nothing tells the advisor these are total present / two-thirds majority / simple majority — the same unlabelled trio appears in the voting roll-call modal. → _Add a 8.5px caps label under each ring (PRESENT / 2-3 / MAJORITY) using the `Stat` pattern from VotingRulesPanel, or at minimum a `title` on each._  `[missing-state]`

## Advisor — nudges

- **`src/app/advisor/[code]/page.tsx:165`** — Nudges are one-way by construction: `sendMessageDB(..., 'Faculty Advisor', ...)` opens a DM thread the delegate can reply to, but the advisor page renders no chat surface at all, so replies are unreachable. The send confirmation (`advisor_nudge_sent`) also disappears after 1500ms with no history, so an advisor cannot tell whether they already encouraged a delegate. → _Either mark the nudge thread explicitly one-way in the delegate's chat ("Advisor nudge — no reply"), or give the advisor a read-only thread view. Persist a small "last nudged 4m ago" line on each delegate card instead of a 1.5s toast._  `[missing-state]`

## Advisor — left rail phase chip

- **`src/app/advisor/[code]/page.tsx:459`** — `return committee.phase.replace(/-/g, ' ')` is the fallback for every non-caucus phase, so the gold chip shows the raw enum: "pre session", "speakers list", "roll call", "adjourned" — untranslated and inconsistent with the delegate page, which maps the same phases through `PHASE_LABEL` to "General Speakers' List" etc. → _Import/share the delegate page's `PHASE_LABEL` map so both surfaces name the phase the same way in every language._  `[copy]`

## Advisor / delegate / voting — loader

- **`src/app/advisor/[code]/page.tsx:400`** — The gavel loader is copy-pasted three times (delegate:65, voting:288, advisor:399), each injecting the same global `@keyframes gavel-strike` and `.gavel-anim` class into a `<style>` tag. Three components racing to define the same global class names, and any tweak has to be made in three places. → _Extract a single `<GavelLoader />` component and move the keyframes into globals.css._  `[consistency]`

## Voting — active vote

- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:1051`** — AGAINST is `bg-[#8B2020]` and AGAINST-WITH-RIGHTS is `bg-[#7A2020]` — a ~7% luminance difference, effectively the same colour from three metres away. IN FAVOUR (#2A7A3C) and IN FAVOUR WITH RIGHTS (#1B5C2E) are only marginally better. A chair under time pressure at the front of a room cannot reliably hit the right one of six large blocks. → _Keep the plain For/Against buttons solid and render the two "with rights" variants as outlined/ghost versions of the same hue with a gold rights icon, so the distinction is shape-and-icon, not a near-identical fill._  `[contrast]`
- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:1009`** — The progress state — "which delegation number is voting, how many left" — is `<p className="text-[#9A8A78] text-sm">{currentVoterIndex + 1} / {presentDelegates.length}</p>`: 14px muted grey under a 40px country name. On a projected screen the room cannot tell how far through the vote they are. The header's `{votes.length}/{presentDelegates.length} voted` is `text-xs` muted and hardcoded English ("voted", line 955). → _Make it a full-width progress bar under the flag with `24 OF 41 · 17 REMAINING` at ~20px in `NEU.ink`, and translate the header string._  `[hierarchy]`
- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:785`** — There is no way to go back. `castVoteAndAdvance` increments `currentVoterIndex` and nothing decrements it, and no "previous delegate" control exists in the voting phase. A chair who mis-clicks on delegate 12 of 41 has no recovery path short of restarting the whole vote from the doc list. → _Add a "◀ Previous" control beside the counter that decrements `currentVoterIndex` (the vote map is already keyed by `delegateId`, so re-casting overwrites cleanly), plus a small "last vote: France — In Favour · undo" line._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:1016`** — A chair clicks 41 times with a mouse in front of a room, one click per delegate, with no keyboard bindings at all. The task is inherently a rapid-fire keyed entry. → _Bind F / A / B (abstain) / P (pass) / R+F / R+A and Backspace-for-undo, and print the key on each button as a small gold badge so the shortcut teaches itself._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Voting — vote scale

- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:1070`** — The hide/show-votes eye button is `absolute right-0 top-0 h-7 w-9` — exactly the height of the 28px scale bar — so it physically sits on top of the right end of the bar, covering the last 36px of the green "for" fill. At a high for-count the bar visually stops short of its true value. → _Move the toggle outside the bar (to the right of it, or beside the "{n} voted" caption) so nothing occludes the data._  `[polish]`

## Voting — vote scale / pie / results

- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:244`** — The voting screen introduces ten colours that exist nowhere in the NEU palette: `bg-red-500/70` and `bg-green-500/70` on the scale (244/248), `text-red-400` / `text-green-400` labels (254/256), `text-amber-400` (971, 1143, 1170, 1199), `#4ade80` / `#f87171` pie slices (281-283), and `#6EE7A0` / `#FCA5A5` / `#FCD34D` result numbers (1297-1312). The 400-weight Tailwind colours on the `#F6F1E9` background sit around 2–3:1 contrast. → _Map the whole screen onto the palette: for = `NEU.green` #3D7A52, against = #8B2020, abstain = `NEU.muted` #9A8A78, rights = `NEU.deepGold` #B6871F, and keep the pastel tints (#6EE7A0/#FCA5A5) only inside the dark result bubble where they actually have contrast._  `[consistency]`

## Voting — result screen

- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:1355`** — `<PieChart forVotes against abstain />` renders three unlabelled coloured wedges at 120px with no legend, no percentages and no `<title>`. On a projected result screen it conveys nothing that the numbers above it don't already say better. → _Either add an inline legend (colour swatch + label + count + %) or delete the pie and give the VoteScale the freed width._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:1379`** — Three buttons sit in one flex row at identical size: "VOTE AGAIN" (tan), "NEXT DRAFT RESOLUTION" (forest, primary) and "END DEBATE" (red, irreversible — it sets `phase='adjourned'` and routes away). The destructive action is directly adjacent to the primary one at the same visual weight, and END DEBATE is *also* permanently present in the header (line 211). → _Keep only the primary and secondary actions in the row; move End Debate to a text link below, or remove the header copy so there is exactly one entry point to the irreversible action._  `[polish]`

## Voting — result / all-voted screens

- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:1339`** — Five result-critical strings are hardcoded English while their siblings are translated: `Simple majority · {n}/{n} in favour, {n} needed` (1339), `Quorum not met · {n} present, {n} required` (1344), `Abstentions counted toward the total` (1349), and on the all-voted screen `{n} counted · {n} needed to pass` / `no votes against allowed` / `quorum {n}/{n}` (1150-1153). The 2/3 and consensus variants immediately above use `t()`. → _Move all six into translations.ts alongside `voting_supermajority` / `voting_consensus`, which already exist for exactly this purpose._  `[copy]`

## Voting — rules console

- **`src/components/VotingRulesPanel.tsx:650`** — The veto-seat warning block is hardcoded English inside an otherwise fully translated component: `'1 veto seat is not in this committee'`, `'… veto seats are not in this committee'`, `'— no delegation on the roster matches, so … can never block a vote.'`, `'{n} veto seat(s) seated: …'`. It is also the panel's most consequential message. → _Key all four strings (`voting_rules_veto_missing_one/other`, `voting_rules_veto_missing_body`, `voting_rules_veto_seated`) with `{n}` interpolation, matching the pattern used two lines above for `voting_rules_veto_custom_note`._  `[copy]`
- **`src/components/VotingRulesPanel.tsx:447`** — The three live `Stat` tiles render values at 16px and labels at `text-[8.5px]` in `rgba(255,255,255,0.42)`. On the console a chair consults mid-vote — and which is also shown beside the projected roll call — 8.5px at 42% opacity is unreadable at any distance, and the numbers that matter ("counted", "to pass") are smaller than the surrounding body copy. → _Raise values to 22px and labels to 10px at 0.6 opacity; the panel is 336px wide and has the room._  `[contrast]`
- **`src/components/VotingRulesPanel.tsx:307`** — `InfoDot` is a 15×15px button — well under any tap target minimum, and its `cursor: 'help'` plus hover-only reveal means it is effectively unusable on the touch devices chairs increasingly use. (The hover-not-click behaviour itself correctly follows the AGENTS.md rule.) → _Keep the 15px visual dot but wrap it in a 28×28 transparent hit area, and treat a tap as a reveal (`onPointerDown` → open, outside-tap → close) so touch users get the same explanation._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/VotingRulesPanel.tsx:380`** — Every interactive control in the console — `Seg` radio buttons (380), `RuleToggle` switches (418), the popover trigger (760) — carries `focus:outline-none` with no replacement focus style. A chair tabbing through the rules mid-vote has no idea where focus is. The same pattern appears 20× in the delegate page, 4× in advisor and 5× in the voting page. → _Add a shared `:focus-visible` ring (`box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(238,217,138,0.7)` on dark surfaces, `0 0 0 3px rgba(182,135,31,0.4)` on ivory) and apply it everywhere `focus:outline-none` currently sits alone._  `[polish]`

## Voting — roll call modal

- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:150`** — The A / P / PV slider's only instruction is `title="Tap to cycle: Absent → Present → PV"` — a hardcoded English tooltip that never appears on the touch devices where tapping is the interaction. Nothing on screen says the control cycles, so chairs tap repeatedly and overshoot. → _Replace the cycling toggle with three discrete tappable segments (A / P / PV) that set state directly, or add a one-line translated caption under the modal subtitle._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:109`** — Thumb positions are hardcoded pixel offsets (`left-[2px]` / `left-[32px]` / `left-[62px]`) against a `w-[90px]` track and a `w-[26px]` thumb. Any change to the track width silently desynchronises the thumb from the three `grid-cols-3` label centres. → _Drive the thumb with `transform: translateX(calc(var(--i) * 30px))` or percentage positions derived from the same 3-column grid, so track width and thumb stay locked._  `[polish]`

## Voting — document selection

- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:857`** — The document-selection screen returns a plain `min-h-screen bg-[#F6F1E9]` tree while the actual voting screen (line 947) is wrapped in `<FitToScreen>`. Picking a document therefore jumps the entire page geometry — header height, type size and spacing all change scale in one step. `#F6F1E9` also differs from `NEU.base` #EDE7D8 used everywhere else in the app. → _Wrap the selection screen in `FitToScreen` too and set its background to #EDE7D8 so the transition is seamless._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:901`** — The PASSED / FAILED badges use `text-green-400 bg-green-950/40 border-green-800/40` and `text-red-400 bg-red-950/40 border-red-800/40` — dark-theme utilities left over from an earlier palette, rendered on a cream `#F6F1E9` card. `green-400` on a 40%-alpha `green-950` wash over cream is barely legible, and neither badge matches the passed/failed treatment used on the same objects in the delegate Documents tab (`#1B3828` on `#EED98A`). → _Use the delegate page's badge styling: passed = `#EED98A` text on `#1B3828`, failed = `#8B2020` text on a `rgba(139,32,32,0.1)` fill with a `rgba(139,32,32,0.3)` border._  `[contrast]`

## Voting — roll call + rules console

- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:868`** — The rules console is `hidden md:flex w-[336px]` beside a `max-w-md` (448px) modal with `gap-4` — 800px of demanded width. Media queries resolve against the *physical* viewport, but the layout width is `#fit-root`'s (`window.innerWidth / scale`). On a 1024×1366 iPad portrait the scale is 1.67, so the layout is only ~615px wide while `md:` still fires — the console and modal collide. The same mismatch affects the delegate tab labels (`sm:text-[18px]`, delegate:1134), the vote-button grid (`sm:grid-cols-3 lg:flex`, voting:1016) and the advisor grid (`sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4`, advisor:621). → _Inside FitToScreen trees, stop using viewport media queries for layout. Either publish the fit-root width as a CSS custom property and switch on container queries (`@container`), or key the breakpoints off the computed `dims.width` FitToScreen already calculates._  `[responsive]`

## Voting — rights speakers

- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:1249`** — The reorderable rights list uses a bare `⠿` (braille pattern dots-123456) as the drag handle, has no drop indicator during a drag, and relies on `cursor-grab` alone to signal draggability. On touch the list is not reorderable at all (`draggable` + HTML5 DnD only). → _Use a proper 6-dot grip glyph in `NEU.muted`, show a 2px `NEU.deepGold` insertion line at the drop index during `dragOver`, and add ▲/▼ buttons as the touch fallback._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:1206`** — Start/Pause (the action) sits in the same flex row, at the same height, as five duration presets (30s/45s/60s/90s/120s). Six buttons of equal weight, and the timer length is something a chair sets once per committee, not per speaker. → _Make Start/Pause a large primary button on its own line and collapse the presets into a small secondary row or a single "60s ▾" menu._  `[hierarchy]`
- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:1203`** — The 60px countdown switches to `text-yellow-500` (#eab308) at ≤20s on the `#F6F1E9` background — roughly 2.2:1, the least legible state at the exact moment it matters most. The warning tier elsewhere in the app is `NEU.amber` #B8844A (see the advisor caucus clock, advisor:552). → _Use #B8844A at ≤20s and #8B2020 at ≤10s, both of which clear 4.5:1 on cream._  `[contrast]`

## Voting — header

- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:949`** — The 44px header carries nine elements: logo, committee name, BACK TO SESSION, END DEBATE, the rules popover trigger, a `⚙` settings glyph, the doc code chip, the doc title and the votes-cast count, plus a "back to documents" link. Everything is 12px, and the destructive END DEBATE is indistinguishable in size from the rest. → _Reduce to logo + committee/doc identity + progress on the left and a single overflow menu (Rules, Settings, Back to documents, End debate) on the right, using the portaled menu pattern from the applications `PaymentMenu`._  `[density]`
- **`src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx:221`** — The settings control is a bare `⚙` gear character at `text-2xl` with `hover:text-[#1C1410]`, no label, no aria-label and no hit-area padding. Every other icon in the polished parts of the app is a lucide glyph at a controlled stroke weight. → _Replace with `<Settings size={16} strokeWidth={2.4} />` in a 32px button with `aria-label`, matching the applications toolbar._  `[consistency]`

## All three session views

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1308`** — None of the three screens uses the NEU design system at all — zero imports from `@/components/neu`. Everything is a flat `1px solid #DDD4C0` border on `#EDE7D8`/`#FAF8F3`, against the benchmark applications page which builds every card from `NEU.surface` + `NEU.out`/`NEU.outSm` + `borderRadius: 22`. Radii on these three files alone run 8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 20, 24 and 999px with no rule about which applies where. → _Adopt `NeuCard` / `NeuInset` / `NeuPill` / `NeuButton` for the delegate session cards, the advisor delegate cards and the voting result surfaces, and fix the radius scale to three steps (chips 999, cards 16, hero cards 22)._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/globals.css:51`** — `body { font-family: 'Outfit', system-ui, sans-serif }` — but Outfit is never loaded. `src/app/layout.tsx:102` only links Playfair Display (italic) and Noto Sans Arabic, there is no `next/font` usage anywhere, and no `@font-face` in the repo. The entire design system's specified typeface silently falls through to system-ui, so the `fontFamily: "'Outfit', sans-serif"` set eleven times in the delegate page is a no-op. → _Load Outfit via `next/font/google` with the weights actually used (400/600/700/800/900) and expose it as `--font-sans`, or change the token to name a font that is actually shipped._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:348`** — Three different mono/heading stacks are in play on one screen: `fontFamily: "'DM Mono', monospace"` (6 occurrences — DM Mono is never loaded either, so it resolves to the OS default mono), Tailwind's `font-mono` class (28 occurrences → the `ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo…` stack), and `'Outfit', sans-serif` (11 occurrences). Adjacent labels in the same card therefore render in different typefaces. `ChatDisabledNotice.tsx:11` adds a fourth with `'Poppins', sans-serif`, also unloaded. → _Pick one mono face, load it, and expose it as a `--font-mono` token; replace all `'DM Mono'`, `font-mono` and `'Poppins'` literals with it._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1118`** — The film-grain overlay is `position: fixed; inset: 0` — but inside `FitToScreen` the `#fit-root` element carries a `transform: scale(...)`, which makes it the containing block for fixed descendants. The grain therefore covers the *fit-root* box (which can be much wider than the viewport when scaled down), not the viewport, so at small scales the grain texture is scaled with everything else and its density differs between the delegate, advisor and adjourned screens. → _Move the grain to an absolutely-positioned layer sized to the fit-root, or render it once at the `FitToScreen` outer wrapper (which is not transformed) so it is viewport-locked at a constant density._  `[consistency]`

## Delegate / voting — committee name

- **`src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx:45`** — AGENTS.md requires long committee names to render as the ACRONYM with the full name small beneath, via `committeeDisplayName()` from `src/lib/presetNames.ts`. None of the three screens does this. Instead there is a bespoke hardcoded regex `abbreviateCommitteeName()` duplicated verbatim in delegate:45 and voting:64 that only knows UNSC/UNGA/UNHRC/HRC and is used for `document.title` (delegate) and the visible header (voting:200). "Disarmament and International Security Committee" renders in full at `text-base` on the delegate strip (1198) and `truncate`d in the advisor rail (503). → _Delete both copies of `abbreviateCommitteeName` and render `committeeDisplayName(name, abbreviation)` as the primary label with the full name as a smaller secondary line beneath, on the delegate committee strip, the advisor rail header and the voting header._  `[rule-violation]`

> **Method / limits:** METHOD: pure source read — no dev server, no browser, no screenshots (per the brief, another agent holds the browser). I read AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md in full, src/components/neu.tsx, and then every file in my scope end to end: src/app/delegate/[code]/page.tsx (1623 lines), src/app/advisor/[code]/page.tsx (665), src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx (1446), src/components/VotingRulesPanel.tsx (791), src/lib/delegateTips.ts, plus FlagImg, Emoji, FitToScreen, MobileGate, Skeleton, SessionsHeaderLogo, CowDelegationBoard, ChatDisabledNotice and the MajorityPie export in RollCallPanel.tsx. I cross-checked the applications benchmark page for its NEU/OUTFIT/portal conventions, grepped translations.ts for every user-facing string I cite, and grepped layout.tsx/globals.css/public for font loading. All colour, px and class values quoted are verbatim from the files at the line numbers given.

COVERAGE LIMITS: (1) Everything is judged from code, so anything that depends on real rendering — actual line-wrap of long country names, whether a given flag PNG 404s, real contrast after the film-grain multiply overlay — is inferred, not measured. (2) I did not audit ChatPanel/src/components/chat/** (out of scope, another agent has it) even though the delegate Chat tab renders it. (3) I did not audit SettingsPanel, which the voting page mounts. (4) I did not evaluate RTL (Arabic) layout beyond noting untranslated strings.

INCIDENTAL NON-UI OBSERVATIONS (bugs / dead code, deliberately NOT in findings):
• delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1011 — the `tabs` array is built and never used; :654 `showChat`/`setShowChat` is dead state ("kept for chatReadCounts compat only" but nothing reads it). AGENTS.md also flags a dead `getSettings`/`enabledMotionTypes` pair on this page; I confirmed both are now gone from the file, so that AGENTS.md note is stale.
• delegate/[code]/page.tsx:247 — `DelegateDocCard` is fully implemented and never rendered (reported as a missing-state finding because it makes the doc-approval feature invisible, but the underlying cause is dead code).
• advisor/[code]/page.tsx:35 `ordinal()` and :26 the `Emoji` import are both unused.
• delegate/[code]/page.tsx:97 `autoDocCode` derives the code from the current document count, so two delegates submitting simultaneously both get "WP 1.4".
• delegate/[code]/page.tsx:562 leaderboard bar divides by `ranking[0].seconds`, which is NaN-producing if the top entry is 0.
• delegate/[code]/page.tsx:865 vs chair/[code]/page.tsx:2097 — genuinely divergent expiry maths (floor vs ceil), reported as a copy finding because the visible symptom is contradictory countdowns.
• voting/[code]/page.tsx:923 `passVoterIndex` counts passed delegates that already have a vote recorded; combined with the absence of any back control this makes a mis-click in the pass round unrecoverable too.
• AGENTS.md's "FEATURE: DELEGATE VIEW" section is stale in two places: it describes a 4-colour floor card that does not exist in the current file, and it lists a "Motions — Coming Soon" tab that has been removed (the header renders Session / Documents / Chat / Stats).

# Conferences — organiser dashboard  
_95 findings_


## Manage shell — desktop rail vs mobile drawer

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/layout.tsx:194`** — Two completely different navs coexist. SideRail (:194) is a floating neumorphic pill: radius 26, NEU.out shadow, active item = solid #1B3828 fill + #EED98A text + `0 4px 14px` glow, 13px/600 labels, 17px icons. SidebarContent (:416) — the mobile drawer — is a flat list: active = `3px solid #1B3828` left border + `rgba(27,56,40,0.08)` wash, forest text, `text-sm` (14px) labels, 16px icons, inactive #9A8A78. A phone user and a desktop user see two different products. → _Delete SidebarContent and render SideRail in the drawer with `expanded` forced true. One nav component, one active treatment (forest fill + gold text), one type scale._  `[consistency]`

## Manage shell — desktop rail

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/layout.tsx:196`** — The rail expands 68px→256px purely on `onMouseEnter`, and it is `position: fixed` while content only reserves `md:ml-[96px]` — so expanding overlays ~160px of the page. Reading a nav label while scanning the applications list means covering the list. There is no way to pin it open. → _Add a pin toggle persisted to localStorage (same idea as `committees-view` at committees/page.tsx:602). When pinned, push content with `md:ml-[270px]` instead of overlaying. Keep hover-peek for the unpinned state._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Manage shell — access denied / section restricted

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/layout.tsx:743`** — Both gate screens render `backgroundColor: '#FAF8F3'` + `border: 1px solid #DDD4C0` + `rounded-2xl`, and a rectangular `rounded-xl` forest button. That is the pre-neumorphic card treatment; every dashboard page behind it is NEU.surface #F0EBDD + NEU.out, radius 22. → _Swap to `<NeuCard style={{padding:32, borderRadius:22}}>` + `<NeuIconDisc gradient={NEU_GRADIENTS.amber} icon={Lock} size={48}>` + `<NeuButton>`. Same for the sectionBlocked card at :780._  `[consistency]`

## Manage shell — sidebar nav labels

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/layout.tsx:123`** — The nav item is labelled "Email Builder" under a "COMMUNICATE" header, but the page it opens is titled "Communications" (communications/page.tsx:1841) and its badge counts unread *participant inbox messages*, not emails you are building. A dot on "Email Builder" reads as "a draft needs finishing", not "3 delegates wrote to you". → _Rename the nav item to "Communications" to match the h1, and give the badge a tooltip/aria-label of "{n} unread messages" rather than the generic `${item.badge} needing attention` at :332._  `[copy]`

## Manage shell — nav badge dot

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/layout.tsx:334`** — The unread dot uses `border: '1.5px solid #FAF8F3'` as a knock-out ring, but the rail it sits on is `#F0EBDD`. The ring is a visibly lighter, slightly pinker halo around the gold dot. → _Use `border: 1.5px solid ${NEU.surface}` so the knock-out matches the actual surface._  `[polish]`

## Manage shell — top bar on mobile

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/layout.tsx:851`** — Both "← BACK" (to /my-conferences) and "VIEW PAGE →" are `hidden sm:inline-flex` / `hidden sm:inline-block`. On a phone the only escape from a conference dashboard is the avatar dropdown, and there is no way at all to open the public page. → _Keep them below sm as icon-only buttons (ArrowLeft, ExternalLink) at 36×36, or move both into the mobile drawer footer where the status pill already lives._  `[responsive]`

## Manage shell — top bar

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/layout.tsx:844`** — The conference acronym is rendered twice within ~80px of each other: once in the header link (:844) and again in the rail identity block (:249, as "ACRONYM YEAR"). Neither is the page title — every page then prints the acronym a third time in its eyebrow (e.g. applications/page.tsx:2381 "{acronym} · Applications"). → _Drop the header acronym link and keep the logo → home breadcrumb only. The rail identity block plus the page eyebrow are already two mentions; three is noise._  `[density]`

## Manage shell — mobile drawer

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/layout.tsx:894`** — The hamburger button has no `aria-label` and no `aria-expanded`, and the open drawer has no close control inside it — dismissal depends on hitting the 40px strip of scrim to the right of a 280px panel on a 375px screen. → _Add `aria-label="Menu"` + `aria-expanded={mobileMenuOpen}`, and put an X button in the drawer's identity row._  `[polish]`

## Applications — review modal

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/page.tsx:3201`** — The most polished page in the app opens a modal styled in the old flat language: `maxHeight:'85vh', backgroundColor:'#FAF8F3', border:'1px solid #DDD4C0'`, with tinted-outline action buttons (`rgba(139,32,32,0.08)` + `1px solid rgba(139,32,32,0.2)`, `rounded-lg py-1.5 px-4`). The page behind it is neumorphic NeuCards and gradient pills. The transition from row → modal reads as two different apps. → _Move the modal card to `backgroundColor: NEU.surface, borderRadius: 22, boxShadow: NEU.out` and replace the tinted-outline buttons with the same gradient pills used in the row (StatusPill/PaymentMenu treatment). LiveModals.tsx:130 already does this correctly — copy that._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/page.tsx:3049`** — The review modal has no previous/next navigation and no keyboard shortcuts. Reviewing 200 applications costs 200 open-read-decide-close cycles, each returning you to a scroll position you then have to re-find. `ChevronLeft` is imported but used only inside QuickAllocate (:673). → _Add ‹ › buttons in the modal header that step through `filtered`, bound to ArrowLeft/ArrowRight, plus A = accept, R = reject, Esc = close. Show "12 of 187" beside them so the organiser knows how much is left._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Applications — list

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/page.tsx:2231`** — Sort order is hardcoded `submitted_at` descending with no UI to change it. An organiser chasing payments wants unpaid-first; one allocating wants top-preference grouped; one checking in wants alphabetical. The sibling committees page does have a sort bar (committees/page.tsx:1394), so the capability exists — just not on the surface that needs it most. → _Add a SortButton row (reuse committees/page.tsx:69) for Name / Submitted / Status / Payment / Experience, defaulting to Submitted desc._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Applications — filters

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/page.tsx:1156`** — `filters`, `search` and `selectedIds` are plain useState. Opening one application, jumping to Assignment to allocate them, and coming back resets the whole view to unfiltered newest-first. Committees persists its view mode to localStorage but nothing persists filters. → _Mirror the filter state into the URL query string (`?status=submitted&role=delegate&q=ana`) so back/forward and a shared link both restore it. localStorage is a weaker fallback._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Applications — FILTERS popover

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/page.tsx:1054`** — FilterPanel renders its panel as `className="absolute z-40"` with `top:'calc(100% + 10px)'` and `maxHeight:'calc(100vh - 150px)'` — not portaled. AGENTS.md's popover rule requires floating layers to be portaled at fixed viewport coordinates, and this very page's PaymentMenu (:522) and QuickAllocate (:657) are the cited reference implementations. A shared, portaled version already exists at src/components/FilterPopover.tsx:119 and is used by communications and the participant RequestsPanel — but not by the page it was extracted from. → _Replace the inline FilterPanel with `<FilterPopoverShell>` from FilterPopover.tsx and delete the local copies of CheckChip/FilterHeading/FilterGroup (:918, :958, :970), which are now byte-for-byte duplicates._  `[rule-violation]`
- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/page.tsx:1018`** — The panel opens on `onMouseEnter` and is pinned by clicking the same button that also toggles the pin — so on a touch device the first tap both hover-opens and pins, and the second tap closes-then-reopens. There is no keyboard path to open it at all (no onFocus, no Enter handling). → _Make click the primary open (as FilterPopoverShell does), keep hover as a desktop-only preview, and add `onFocus`/`onKeyDown` so Tab+Enter opens it._  `[polish]`

## Applications — Export CSV

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/page.tsx:2146`** — `handleExportCSV` maps over `applications` — the raw unfiltered array — so an organiser who has filtered to "unpaid delegates" and clicks EXPORT CSV silently gets all 400 rows including withdrawn and not-attending. The button gives no indication of scope. → _Export `filtered` (or `selectedApps` when a selection exists) and label the button dynamically: "EXPORT 42" / "EXPORT ALL 400"._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Applications — bulk action bar

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/page.tsx:2929`** — `@keyframes bulkPulse` runs `infinite` on the suggested action, scaling it 1→1.06 and swelling its shadow forever while any selection exists. A permanently animating button in the corner of a page you are working in for an hour is fatiguing and undermines the same device being used for genuinely urgent states. → _Run the pulse 3 times (`animation: bulkPulse 1.5s ease-in-out 3`) then settle, or drop it and rely on the gradient fill alone to mark the suggested action._  `[polish]`

## Applications — select checkboxes

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/page.tsx:771`** — SelectBox is a 20×20 button with a 7px radius — the primary control for bulk work, at less than half the 44px minimum touch target, with no padded hit area. → _Keep the 20px visual but wrap it in a 44×44 button with `padding: 12` and a transparent background, so the visible box stays small and the target is thumb-sized._  `[responsive]`

## Applications — row card

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/page.tsx:2645`** — Inside one row, the assigned committee acronym renders at `fontSize: 27, fontWeight: 900` and the country at `fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 800`, while the applicant's name — the thing you scan the list by — is `fontSize: 19.5, fontWeight: 800`. The allocation, which is derived data, out-shouts the person. → _Drop the committee acronym to 20/900 and the country to 15/700, and lift the name to 21/800. The 92px LogoDisc beside them already carries the committee identity._  `[hierarchy]`
- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/page.tsx:2540`** — A single row carries: SelectBox, 62px avatar with an overlapping 24px flag, name, NOT-REGISTERED chip, HEAD DEL. chip, email, society button, age, nationality + flag, pledge chip, RolePill, INVITED chip, 66px LevelBadge (insignia + tier + "9 confs"), 92px committee LogoDisc, acronym, full name, country flag + country, StatusPill, NOT ATTENDING badge, PaymentMenu, and up to four action buttons. That is ~20 elements competing inside roughly 200px of height. → _Demote the secondary facts (age, nationality, conference count, pledge line) into the review modal or behind a hover card, and keep the row to: select, avatar+flag, name+email+society, role, allocation, status, payment, primary action._  `[density]`

## Applications — filters-active notice

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/page.tsx:2437`** — "Showing 42 of 187 — filters active" is inert 12px muted text. The user's next move is almost always "show me everything again", and the only way to do that is to reopen the FILTERS popover and hit CLEAR ALL, or find the Total stat tile. → _Make the notice a row with an inline `CLEAR FILTERS` text button (same treatment as the popover's CLEAR ALL at :1071)._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Applications — focus states

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/page.tsx:503`** — 42 occurrences of `focus:outline-none` on this page alone (and 34 in settings, 41 in communications, 28 in assignment) with no replacement focus ring anywhere in /manage. Keyboard focus is completely invisible across the entire organiser dashboard. Only four files in the repo use `focus-visible`, all under /auth. → _Add a single global rule in globals.css: `:where(button, a, [role="button"], input, select):focus-visible { outline: 2px solid #B6871F; outline-offset: 2px; border-radius: inherit; }` and stop stripping outlines per-element._  `[contrast]`

## Assignment — header actions

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/assignment/page.tsx:3803`** — Two adjacent CTAs read "SEND ALLOCATION EMAILS" (gold gradient) and "SEND ALL ALLOCATIONS" (forest gradient). The second one sends nothing: `handleSendAllAllocations` (:3237) only flips `allocation_sent = true` on the allocation rows. The label promises delivery and delivers a flag flip. → _Rename to "RELEASE ALLOCATIONS" (or "MARK ALL AS RELEASED") and add a one-line caption under the pair: "Release makes allocations visible; Email notifies delegates." Consider merging into one action with a checkbox._  `[copy]`
- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/assignment/page.tsx:3810`** — `handleSendAllocationEmails` opens a confirm dialog spelling out the recipient count (:3350). `handleSendAllAllocations` — which flips every unsent allocation in the conference in one click and is what delegates actually see — has no confirm at all. → _Give it the same `confirm({ title: 'Release N allocations?' })` gate with the count spelled out, matching the sibling button._  `[polish]`

## Assignment — page header

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/assignment/page.tsx:3793`** — Page headers differ on every sibling. Live (live/page.tsx:685): muted Eyebrow, h1 28. Documents (documents/page.tsx:713): muted eyebrow, h1 24. ## Assignment — suggested assignments

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/assignment/page.tsx:3910`** — Each suggestion card is a `<NeuInset small>` — a pressed-in well. neu.tsx documents inset as the "done / inactive / track" state (NeuChecklistRow: `done ? NEU.inSm : NEU.outSm`, :646). These are the most actionable cards on the page and they read as already-handled. → _Render suggestions as extruded NeuCards (NEU.outSm, lifting to outSmHover on hover) inside the gold-washed strip. ## Assignment — applicant cards

## Assignment — feedback

## Committees — session release times

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/committees/page.tsx:274`** — ReleaseTimePicker pairs the shared `<DatePicker>` with a native `<input type="time">` sitting directly beside it. The DatePicker is a friendly neumorphic popover; the native time input renders OS chrome (spinners in Chrome, a stepper in Safari, a full-width native sheet on iOS) with a different height, a different focus ring, and no NEU shadow. The same pattern is in documents/page.tsx:281. → _Add a matching `<TimePicker>` beside DatePicker in src/components/ (a 15-minute-step neumorphic dropdown), or extend DatePicker with an optional `withTime` prop. AGENTS.md bans native date pickers; a native time picker glued to the shared date picker is the same problem._  `[rule-violation]`
