## Live Status — committee grid

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/live/page.tsx:812`** — The grid renders every committee with no search, no filter and no sort — ordered by `name` from the query. During a live conference with 20+ committees, finding the one that is suspended means visually scanning 20 cards, even though `cardStatus(r)` already classifies each one. → _Make the five StatusGlyphs clickable filters (they already hold the counts) and sort so live/suspended committees float to the top._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Live Status — refresh

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/live/page.tsx:757`** — The page polls every 10s (:660) *and* offers a manual Refresh button plus a "Refreshed Xs ago" counter that re-renders every second. The counter therefore never exceeds ~10, making both it and the button near-pointless, while the 1s tick re-renders the whole page. → _Drop the manual button and the seconds counter; show a small pulsing "Live" dot instead, and keep a pause control for organisers who want to freeze the view while reading._  `[polish]`

## Documents — cards

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/documents/page.tsx:765`** — Section cards are `backgroundColor:'#FAF8F3', border:'1.5px solid #D8CDB6', borderRadius:16` + a local CARD_SHADOW — yet the tab switcher directly above them (:749) is `NeuPill`. The page is half-migrated: neumorphic pills floating over flat bordered panels. → _Convert the three section panels to NeuCard (radius 22, NEU.out) and the row buttons to NeuPill/NeuButton, finishing the migration the tab switcher started._  `[consistency]`

## Documents — study guide status

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/documents/page.tsx:800`** — Three statuses render: PUBLISHED (green), RELEASED (green, identical tint `rgba(61,122,82,0.12)`), DRAFT (amber). PUBLISHED and RELEASED look the same, mean nearly the same thing to a reader, and both offer an UNPUBLISH button — except one of them opens a tooltip explaining that unpublishing won't actually hide it. → _Collapse to two visible states: VISIBLE (with a sub-line "published manually" or "released on 3 Mar") and HIDDEN. Move the scheduling nuance into the sub-line rather than a second green chip._  `[copy]`

## Documents — delete guide

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/documents/page.tsx:855`** — The delete control is bare text at `fontSize: 11` in `#9A8A78` on `#FAF8F3` — roughly 2.4:1 contrast, below the 4.5:1 minimum, and it only turns red-with-underline once hovered. A destructive action is the least visible thing in the row. → _Give it an icon (Trash2 at 14) plus `color: '#8B2020'` at rest, or move it into a portaled RowMenu (assignment/page.tsx:2322 already has one)._  `[contrast]`

## Job Board — whole page

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/jobs/page.tsx:37`** — The page declares its own palette — `FOREST/GOLD/INK/MUTED/CARD_BG '#FAF8F3'/BORDER '#DDD4C0'/DIVIDER '#F0EDE6'/AMBER_TEXT` — and imports nothing from neu.tsx. Everything is flat: bordered cards, rectangular `rounded-xl` forest buttons, `transition-all` on nine elements. It is the least-migrated route in /manage. → _Rebuild on NEU tokens: stats → NeuStatTile, posting cards → NeuCard, POST A POSITION → NeuButton, category tabs → NeuPill. Delete the local palette constants._  `[consistency]`

## Job Board — loading

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/jobs/page.tsx:872`** — The skeleton renders three `animate-pulse` blocks with `backgroundColor: BORDER` (#DDD4C0) — a distinctly darker grey than the #EDE7D8 page, so loading reads as three heavy grey slabs. Every other skeleton in the app uses `backgroundColor: NEU.surface, boxShadow: NEU.out` (dashboard page.tsx:912, invoices :618, live :802). → _Match the others: `NEU.surface` + `NEU.out` + `rounded-[22px]`, so the skeleton is the shape of the thing that's coming._  `[polish]`

## Job Board — empty state

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/jobs/page.tsx:969`** — The empty state says `Click "Post a Position" to start recruiting` while the actual button reads POST A POSITION in uppercase — and the empty state provides no button of its own, so a first-time organiser has to hunt for the one in the header. Committees' empty state (committees/page.tsx:1225) does include the CTA. → _Put a POST A POSITION button in the empty state and quote the label exactly (or drop the quote: "Post your first position to start recruiting chairs and staff.")._  `[copy]`

## Job Board — stats

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/jobs/page.tsx:924`** — The three stats (Open positions / Total applications / Categories active) are inert flat boxes. On Applications the equivalent tiles are clickable filters (NeuStatTile with `active` + `onClick`, :2426) — clicking "Unpaid" filters the list. Here, clicking "Total applications" does nothing, and there is no way to see all applications across postings. → _Convert to NeuStatTile and wire Open positions → filter to open, Total applications → an all-applications view._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Communications — stat medallions

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/communications/page.tsx:1948`** — Three hand-rolled stat cards: `#FAF8F3` background, `1.5px solid #D8CDB6`, plus a `borderTop: '2.5px solid #1B3828'` accent on the first one only, a 44px tinted-square icon and a 26px number. NeuStatTile exists and is used on the dashboard, applications and financials with a 42px gradient disc, 29px number and NEU.out. → _Replace with `<NeuStatTile emoji="Envelope" gradient={NEU_GRADIENTS.gold} .../>`. The top-border accent trick appears nowhere else and should go._  `[consistency]`

## Communications — tab switcher

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/communications/page.tsx:1979`** — EMAILS / NOTIFICATIONS / INBOX use a local `TabPill` (:357) — `borderRadius: 8`, solid forest fill, no shadow — inside a `rounded-xl` bordered container. On the same page, the audience filter uses the neumorphic `FilterPopoverShell` (:2379) with gradient CheckChips. Two design systems, one viewport. → _Swap TabPill for NeuPill and delete the local component; the FilterPopoverShell import already commits this page to the neumorphic language._  `[consistency]`

## Communications — inbox badge

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/communications/page.tsx:1993`** — The unread count renders as a parenthetical inside the tab label — `INBOX (3)` — while the exact same number renders as a gold pill badge in the sidebar (layout.tsx:352) and as a dot when collapsed (:331). Three presentations of one count. → _Use the pill badge inside the tab too (the `minWidth:18, height:18, borderRadius:999` treatment from layout.tsx:352)._  `[consistency]`

## Communications — audience typeahead

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/communications/page.tsx:2822`** — `className="absolute left-0 right-0 rounded-xl shadow-lg overflow-y-auto"` — a non-portaled dropdown, in a page whose surrounding regions set `maxHeight: 280/440/140` with `overflowY: 'auto'` (:2205, :2617, :2765). It will be clipped by its own scroll container. AGENTS.md requires portaling at fixed viewport coordinates. → _Wrap in `<Portal>` and compute `top/left` from the trigger's `getBoundingClientRect()`, flipping upward near the bottom edge — the PaymentMenu pattern (applications/page.tsx:439-465)._  `[rule-violation]`

## Communications — colour fields

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/communications/page.tsx:413`** — ColorField's free-text hex input accepts anything — no validation, no error, and no swatch showing what the typed value actually is. Typing `forest` or `#1B382` silently produces a broken email theme with no feedback until you open the preview. → _Show a live 26px swatch of the current value beside the input (greyed with a small warning glyph when the value isn't a valid hex), and normalise on blur._  `[missing-state]`

## Communications — toggles

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/communications/page.tsx:336`** — A local `PillToggle` at 34×19 with a 15px thumb. settings/page.tsx:200 defines a different `PillToggle` at 40×22 (md) / 32×18 (sm) with an 18px/14px thumb. accountUi.tsx exports a third, imported by committees/page.tsx:12. All three use `#1B3828` on / `#DDD4C0` off with a white thumb and `transition-all`. → _Keep the accountUi export as canonical, delete the other two definitions, and give it the neumorphic treatment (NEU.inSm track, NEU.outSm thumb) rather than a flat #DDD4C0 track._  `[consistency]`

## Settings — application windows

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/settings/page.tsx:1871`** — `<input type="datetime-local">` for the Opens and Closes fields (:1871, :1885), and again at jobs/page.tsx:599 for the posting deadline. AGENTS.md: "The native `<input type=\"date\">` (and any other old or native date picker) must NEVER be used anywhere in the app. EVERY date input must use the shared friendly picker @/components/DatePicker." These are the most consequential dates in the product — the window delegates can apply in. → _Use `<DatePicker>` for the date half plus a matching neumorphic time control, exactly as committees/page.tsx:262 attempts (and fix that one's native time half at the same time)._  `[rule-violation]`

## Settings — role config fields

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/settings/page.tsx:1868`** — Opens / Closes / Max Accepted / Fee are uncontrolled `defaultValue` inputs that save on blur via `saveRoleConfig`. There is no saving indicator, no saved confirmation, no error surface if the write fails, and nothing validates that Closes is after Opens. An organiser can set applications to close before they open and get no feedback at all. → _Add a per-field save state (a small spinner → green tick that fades), surface write errors inline, and pass `min={openIso}` to the Closes picker so the invalid range is unreachable._  `[missing-state]`

## Settings — panel surfaces

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/settings/page.tsx:1626`** — `cardStyle` is `backgroundColor: '#FFFDF9'` — a near-white card — with `1.5px solid #D8CDB6` and a `0 1px 2px` shadow that is effectively flat. neu.tsx's header states the system is "never white cards". These sit inside a glass `<section>` (:1739, `backdropFilter: blur(14px)`), which itself sits on the ivory page: three nested surface treatments, none of them the system's. → _Panel → NeuCard (NEU.surface, radius 22, NEU.out). Inner groups → NeuInset (NEU.base, NEU.in). That is exactly two surfaces and both are in the system._  `[consistency]`

## Settings — section rail

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/settings/page.tsx:1666`** — A 244px vertical rail (Applications / Conference / Organizers / Privacy, each with an icon tile and a hint line) sits immediately to the right of the layout's own vertical rail. On a 1440px screen with the layout rail expanded you have two vertical navs occupying 500px before any content, and both use forest-fill active states — so it's momentarily ambiguous which one you're navigating. → _Make the settings sections a horizontal NeuPill row above the panel (matching committees/documents/financials, which all use horizontal pills), and reclaim the 244px for the forms._  `[hierarchy]`

## Settings — inputs

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/settings/page.tsx:179`** — `inputStyle` here is `backgroundColor:'#FAF8F3', border:'1.5px solid #DDD4C0', borderRadius:10` — a bordered flat field. financials/shared.tsx:175 exports an `inputStyle` with the *same name* that is `backgroundColor: NEU.base, boxShadow: NEU.inSm, border: 'none', borderRadius: 12`. Two identically-named tokens producing opposite treatments, applied to the same kind of form. → _Move the financials (neumorphic) `inputStyle` into neu.tsx as the single export and delete the settings copy._  `[consistency]`

## Import — imported delegates table

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/import/page.tsx:1112`** — The page surfaces an "Unclaimed" count (:1102) and then makes you click RESEND INVITE once per row, each with its own confirm dialog (:1060). For a 120-person roster import where 40 haven't claimed, that is 40 confirms. There is no "resend to all unclaimed", even though `queueImportJoinInviteEmails` already takes an array. → _Add a "Resend to all N unclaimed" button beside the Unclaimed SummaryStat, with one confirm and one batched call._  `[wasted-effort]`
- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/import/page.tsx:1123`** — The only real `<table>` in /manage lives inside `maxHeight: 560, overflowY: 'auto'` but the `<thead>` is not sticky, so Name/Email/Role/Status/Invite scroll out of view after ~14 rows. The container is also a flat `1px solid #DDD4C0` box with `#F0EDE6` header and `#FAF8F3` rows, unrelated to the neumorphic cards elsewhere on the same page (which does use NeuCard/NeuIconDisc at :1092). → _Add `position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 1` to the `<th>` cells with the header background, and wrap the table in a NeuCard instead of the bordered box._  `[polish]`

## Import — wizard headings

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/import/page.tsx:652`** — The page has two h1s at two sizes: `text-2xl` "Import Applicants" (:579) and `text-3xl` "Import your roster" (:652), in different steps of the same flow — and the two titles say the same thing differently ("Applicants" vs "roster"). → _One h1 at 26/900, "Import roster", persistent across steps, with the step name as the gold eyebrow._  `[consistency]`

## Modals — shared overlay

- **`src/components/CommitteeEditorModal.tsx:105`** — ModalOverlay has no Escape-to-close (only ConfirmModal adds its own keydown at :62), no focus trap, no focus restoration on close, and no body scroll lock — so the page scrolls behind an open dialog. The scrim is `rgba(0,0,0,0.4)`, neutral black, in a system whose shadows are deliberately forest-tinted (`rgba(27,56,40,...)`) and "never neutral black". → _Add Escape handling, `overflow:hidden` on body while mounted, initial focus on the dialog and restore on unmount, and change the scrim to `rgba(27,56,40,0.45)`._  `[polish]`

## Modals — ConfirmModal

- **`src/components/ConfirmModal.tsx:62`** — `if (e.key === 'Enter') { onConfirm(); }` fires regardless of `danger`. Every destructive dialog in the dashboard — delete committee, reject N applications, remove from conference — is one stray Enter away from executing, and the confirm button is not focused, so the user has no visual cue that Enter is armed. → _Only bind Enter when `!danger`. For danger dialogs, focus the Cancel button on mount and require an explicit click (the `confirmDisabled` type-to-confirm gate already exists for the worst cases)._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/ConfirmModal.tsx:117`** — Cancel and Confirm are both `flex-1` with identical `rounded-xl py-2.5 font-bold text-sm` — equal width, equal weight. In a danger dialog the destructive action (solid `#8B2020`) is the visually heaviest thing on screen and sits where muscle memory expects OK. → _For `danger`, make Cancel the filled/prominent button and the destructive one a text-weight red button, or at minimum drop the destructive button to `flex-none` with less width than Cancel._  `[hierarchy]`
- **`src/components/ConfirmModal.tsx:100`** — The optional checkbox is a native `<input type="checkbox">` styled only with `accentColor: '#1B3828'` at 15×15 — an OS control inside a design system that has two purpose-built checkboxes (SelectBox at applications/page.tsx:771 and CheckChip at FilterPopover.tsx:26). → _Use SelectBox with a label, and give the whole row a 44px-tall hit area._  `[consistency]`

## Toasts

- **`src/components/Toast.tsx:23`** — `DISMISS_MS = 2200` applies to error toasts as well as success. 2.2 seconds is not enough to read "Could not delete Conference T-Shirt, it was restored. Please try again." — and once it's gone there is no log or history, so the user knows only that something flashed red. → _Keep 2.2s for `kind: 'ok'` and make `kind: 'err'` persist until dismissed (or 8s minimum). The X button already exists._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/components/Toast.tsx:121`** — `backgroundColor: 'rgba(240,235,221,0.72)'` + `backdropFilter: 'blur(10px) saturate(1.35)'` + `inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,0.5)` — matte glass, in the system that documents itself as having no glass. It also mixes `NEU.out` with a second custom drop shadow. → _Use opaque `NEU.surface` with `NEU.out` and keep the 3px accent border-left. The toast already floats above everything; the blur adds nothing but a third surface language._  `[consistency]`

## Organiser edit affordances

- **`src/components/OrganizerPencil.tsx:30`** — The corner variant is a 28×28 button with a 13px pencil in `#9A8A78` on ivory — roughly 2.4:1, below the 4.5:1 minimum for a UI glyph — and it only reaches full contrast (`#1B3828`) on hover. → _Rest at `#6B5F52` (≈4.6:1), size the button 34×34, and keep the forest hover._  `[contrast]`
- **`src/components/OrganizerPencil.tsx:44`** — The cover variant renders at `opacity: 0` and only appears on `onMouseEnter`/`onFocus`. On any touch device — an organiser fixing their banner on an iPad at the conference — the edit affordance is completely invisible and the first tap both reveals and fires it. → _Add a persistent low-opacity (0.5) pencil disc in the corner on `(hover: none)` pointers via a media query, so touch users can see there is something to tap._  `[polish]`

## Email composer — Test as / preview pickers

- **`src/components/EmailComposer.tsx:524`** — Both dropdowns are `className="absolute right-0 ..."` (:524) and `className="absolute left-0 right-0 ... overflow-y-auto"` (:663), not portaled. They live inside the communications builder, which nests scrollable `maxHeight` regions — so they can be clipped, which is precisely what the AGENTS.md popover rule exists to prevent. → _Portal both at fixed coordinates with edge flipping, following applications/page.tsx PaymentMenu (:439) / QuickAllocate (:568)._  `[rule-violation]`

## Email composer — errors

- **`src/components/EmailComposer.tsx:475`** — `setTestSentMessage(\`Failed to send test: ${error.message}\`)` puts a raw Postgres/Supabase error string in front of an organiser (e.g. 'new row violates row-level security policy for table "email_outbox"'). auth/signin/page.tsx:50 does the same with `setError(error.message)`. → _Map to human copy with a next step: "Couldn't send the test email. Check that your account email is verified, then try again." Log the raw message to the console instead._  `[copy]`

## Email composer — block editing

- **`src/components/EmailComposer.tsx:410`** — Blocks reorder only via `moveBlock(id, ±1)` chevrons — one click per position. Moving a paragraph from the bottom of an 8-block email to the top is 7 clicks. The assignment board already ships drag-and-drop in this codebase. → _Add drag handles (GripVertical, same as assignment/page.tsx:4106) with the chevrons kept as the keyboard-accessible fallback._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Financial Aid — request list

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/AidRequestsSection.tsx:320`** — Aid requests open one modal at a time with no prev/next and no bulk actions, and the modal's only exit is a CLOSE button at the bottom of a `maxHeight:'85vh'` scroll. Approving 40 aid requests means 40 open/scroll/decide/close cycles, and each approval needs the grant amount retyped even though `requested_amount` is known. → _Prefill the grant amount with `requested_amount` (or the full fee), add ‹ › stepping through `filtered`, and add a bulk "approve N at requested amount" for the common case._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Financial Aid — review modal

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/AidRequestsSection.tsx:430`** — The grant-amount input is `backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF'` with `1px solid #DDD4C0` — pure white, inside a `#FAF8F3` card, on an `#EDE7D8` page. neu.tsx explicitly rules out white surfaces; every other input in financials is `NEU.base` + `NEU.inSm`. → _Use the shared neumorphic `inputStyle` from financials/shared.tsx:175._  `[consistency]`

## Financial Aid — Approve / Deny

- **`src/app/manage/[slug]/applications/AidRequestsSection.tsx:450`** — APPROVE AID and DENY AID are both `flex-1` tinted-outline buttons at identical size and weight (`rgba(61,122,82,0.12)` vs `rgba(139,32,32,0.1)`), distinguished only by hue. For a red/green-blind organiser they are two identical grey buttons with a tick and a cross at 14px. → _Make Approve the filled primary (forest→green gradient, white text) and Deny a text-weight red button, so the difference survives without colour._  `[hierarchy]`

## My Conferences

- **`src/app/my-conferences/MyConferencesClient.tsx:933`** — The continent filter is a native `<select>` — correctly styled with `appearance-none` + NEU.base + NEU.inSm + an overlaid ChevronDown, which is the *right* way to do it. But it sits beside two `NeuSegmented` controls, and every other native select in the dashboard (invoices, history, settings, addons) skipped this treatment entirely. → _Promote this pattern to a shared `<NeuSelect>` in neu.tsx and use it for all remaining native selects, or replace it with NeuSegmented for consistency within this one toolbar._  `[consistency]`

## Account shell

- **`src/app/account/layout.tsx:76`** — Account uses `max-w-[1000px]`, a full-page GRAIN noise overlay at `opacity: 0.18` with `mixBlendMode: 'multiply'` (:60), SiteNav instead of the manage top bar, and a mobile tab bar of underline tabs (:80) — a sixth tab style. Moving from /manage to /account feels like leaving the product. → _Either bring the grain into /manage as well, or drop it here. Replace the underline tabs with NeuSegmented (already used at my-conferences:920) so the two authenticated shells share a vocabulary._  `[consistency]`

## Account — CV share

- **`src/app/account/cv/page.tsx:95`** — When `navigator.clipboard` fails, the fallback is `window.prompt('Copy your public CV link:', url)` — a raw OS dialog (also ShareAchievementModal.tsx:119, :124). → _Render the URL in a selectable inset field inside the existing card with a "select all" behaviour on focus, instead of a browser prompt._  `[polish]`

## Staff admin — whole page

- **`src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:19`** — A fourth local palette (`INK/MUTED/FOREST/GOLD/PANEL '#FAF8F3'/LINE '#DDD4C0'`) plus a genuinely monospace `MONO = 'ui-monospace, monospace'` used for chips and the SET-UP label — the only real monospace type in the product. Cards are flat `1px solid LINE` boxes. → _If staff tooling is intentionally utilitarian, say so with a deliberate style; otherwise move it onto NEU tokens and drop the monospace, which currently reads as an unfinished admin panel._  `[consistency]`

## Staff admin — loading

- **`src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:100`** — The loading state is the string "Loading…" centred at `py-24`. Every other list surface in the product shows either a spinner (applications :2444) or shaped skeletons (dashboard :912, invoices :618, live :802, my-conferences :966). → _Render 5 skeleton rows at the real row height so the page doesn't collapse then jump._  `[missing-state]`

## Staff admin — stalled conferences

- **`src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:186`** — The row prints `nudged {n}d ago` from `last_nudge_at` and the stats bar counts "Stalled 14d+" and "Under capacity", but there is no nudge action, and none of the six stats are clickable filters. Staff can see exactly who needs chasing and then has to leave the page to do anything about it. → _Make the six stat boxes filter the list (as the Applications stat tiles do), and add a "Nudge organiser" button on each row that queues the existing reminder email and stamps `last_nudge_at`._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Staff admin — list

- **`src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:198`** — No sort control. Rows arrive in whatever order the RPC returns, so "which drafts have been stalled longest" — the page's whole purpose — cannot be answered without reading every `touched Nd ago`. → _Add sort by Last touched / Set-up progress / Applications, defaulting to last-touched ascending on the Drafts tab._  `[wasted-effort]`

> **Method / limits:** METHOD — findings come entirely from reading source at /Users/peterzakhar/debate.com on branch feature/conferences-auth (working tree, uncommitted changes included). I did not start a dev server and did not use browser tools, per the brief. Nothing was edited. Line numbers were verified by direct read or grep at the time of the audit; a few (applications/page.tsx:3049, :3201, documents/page.tsx:855) are ±3 because they were located via range scan rather than a direct anchor.

COVERAGE — read in full or in substantial part: manage/[slug]/layout.tsx, page.tsx (dashboard), applications/page.tsx + AidRequestsSection.tsx, assignment/page.tsx, committees/page.tsx, financials/{layout,shared,invoices,history,AddonsSection}, live/page.tsx, documents/page.tsx, jobs/page.tsx, import/page.tsx, communications/page.tsx, settings/page.tsx, financial-aid/page.tsx, account/layout.tsx + cv, admin/AdminClient.tsx, auth/authUi.tsx + signin, my-conferences/MyConferencesClient.tsx, and components neu.tsx, FilterPopover.tsx, Toast.tsx, ConfirmModal.tsx, Portal.tsx, OrganizerPencil.tsx, EmailComposer.tsx, DefaultEmailPreviewModal.tsx, CommitteeEditorModal.tsx (ModalOverlay + card styles). Sampled rather than read line-by-line: assignment/{DelegationsView,IndependentsView,delegationShared}.tsx, financials/{VouchersSection,ApplicationFeeSection,settings}, financial-aid/AidFormEditor.tsx, live/LiveModals.tsx, CVEntryModal.tsx (1374 lines — only its one `absolute` layer and modal shell were checked), account/{profile,unlimited,calendar,points,conferences}. Deeper passes on those five could add findings of the same kinds already listed (flat #FAF8F3 cards, native selects, focus:outline-none).

THE BENCHMARK, STATED PLAINLY — applications/page.tsx leads its siblings on: portaled edge-flipping popovers (PaymentMenu :417, QuickAllocate :547); clickable stat tiles that ARE the filters (:2426); a single hover FILTERS panel instead of a row of dropdowns; a sticky bulk-action bar with a computed suggested next action (:2926); optimistic writes with exact per-row rollback and a busy guard; a "Showing X of Y — filters active" honesty line (:2437); genuinely distinct empty vs no-match copy (:2455); the acronym-with-full-name-beneath committee rule implemented properly (`committeeDisplay`, :806); and never-broken flags for crisis/character seats (`CountryFlag`, :837). Where siblings fall short I named the exact benchmark line. The two places the benchmark itself falls short of its own standard: its FilterPanel is not portaled even though the extracted, portaled FilterPopoverShell exists, and its review modal reverts to the pre-neumorphic flat card.

THE SINGLE BIGGEST CROSS-CUTTING ISSUE — the dashboard is mid-migration. Roughly half the routes (dashboard, applications, assignment, financials, live, my-conferences, financial-aid) are on neu.tsx; the other half (jobs, documents, communications, settings, admin, and every modal except LiveModals) still use the legacy flat language: `#FAF8F3`/`#FFFDF9` cards, `1px–1.5px solid #DDD4C0`/`#D8CDB6` borders, rectangular forest buttons, local FOREST/GOLD/INK/MUTED constants. Finishing that migration would resolve about a third of the findings above on its own, and the modal layer is the highest-leverage place to start because every route opens into it.

INCIDENTAL NON-UI OBSERVATIONS (not findings, for whoever owns these):
1. manage/[slug]/layout.tsx:767 — `SECTION_PERMS` has no key for `live`, so a co-organiser with no permissions can open /manage/[slug]/live. Probably intentional (read-only), but it is the only manage route with no permission mapping.
2. assignment/page.tsx:517 and delegationShared.tsx:19 — `const MONO = "'Outfit', sans-serif"`. The constant is named MONO but resolves to the proportional system font; it is used ~75 times across four files. Purely a naming lie, no visual effect, but it will mislead the next person who tries to change "the monospace font".
3. applications/page.tsx duplicates CheckChip / FilterHeading / FilterGroup / toggleIn (:911-998) which are already exported verbatim from src/components/FilterPopover.tsx. Two copies that can drift.
4. jobs/page.tsx:50-51 — category/compensation values round-trip through `toDbEnum`/`toUiEnum` (lowercase-hyphen ⇄ UPPERCASE-SPACED) on every read and write. Works, but it means the UI's tab labels and the DB CHECK constraint are coupled through a string transform rather than a shared constant.
5. live/page.tsx:470 carries an explicit `TODO(merge): wire to chair award allocation from production branch` — flagged above as a copy problem, but it is also a genuine unfinished merge.
6. Toast.tsx:52 collapses an exact-duplicate message only against the last item currently in the stack, so the same error firing twice 3s apart shows twice. Minor, and arguably correct.


---

# Conferences — public and participant side  
_95 findings_


## Explore — filter bar

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:1035`** — The "APPLICATIONS OPEN" pill is a dead control. `applicationsOpen` is read only by `hasActiveFilters` (:848) and reset by `clearFilters` (:839) — the `filtered` memo (:801-826) never references it. Clicking it turns the pill forest-green, lights the gold "active filters" dot, and changes nothing in the grid. → _Either wire it into `filtered` (needs `application_role_configs.applications_open_at/close_at` in the explore fetch, which currently only pulls fee columns) or delete the pill. A filter that visibly activates and does nothing is worse than no filter._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Explore — region dropdown

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:331`** — The RegionControl listbox renders as `className="absolute right-0 z-50 mt-2"` inside the sticky glass bar. AGENTS.md UI RULE requires every floating layer to be portaled through @/components/Portal at fixed viewport coordinates with edge-flip. It also sits inside a `position:sticky; z-30` ancestor, which creates a stacking context that traps its z-50. → _Port it to the PaymentMenu pattern: `getBoundingClientRect()` on the trigger, render through Portal at `position:fixed`, reposition on capture-phase scroll + resize, clamp `left` at the viewport edge, flip upward when there is no room below._  `[rule-violation]`

## Explore — filter pills

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:115`** — `className="… transition-all focus:outline-none"` on FilterPill, and the same `transition-all` on the ORGANISE A CONFERENCE button (:931). `transition: all` animates layout properties too and is the one transition the house style bans. Both also kill the focus outline with no replacement. → _Replace with `transition: background-color 200ms EASE, color 200ms EASE, border-color 200ms EASE` and add a visible focus ring (e.g. `box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px #B6871F` on `:focus-visible`)._  `[polish]`

## Explore — loading state

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:1079`** — The loading skeleton is always six card-shaped blocks in `grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3`, even when `view === 'list'`. A returning visitor whose stored view is 'list' sees a grid of cards flash and then reflow into a completely different row layout. → _Branch the skeleton on `view`: render six 108px-tall row skeletons (circle + two text bars + right-rail columns) when `view === 'list'`._  `[missing-state]`

## Explore — zero results

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:1119`** — The generic empty state reads "No conferences listed yet" / "Gavelling Conferences is launching soon. Be the first to list your conference." but it also fires when a search or format/level filter simply matched nothing. Searching "asdf" tells the user the product is pre-launch, and the only button offered is ORGANISE A CONFERENCE. → _Split the branch: when `searchQuery || formatFilter || levelFilter || region`, show "No conferences match your filters" with a primary "Clear all filters" button (reuse `clearFilters`). Keep the launching-soon copy only for `conferences.length === 0`._  `[copy]`

## Explore — list row (mobile)

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:522`** — The whole right rail is `hidden sm:flex`, delegates is `hidden lg:flex` (:535), fee is `hidden md:flex` (:543), and the CTA is `hidden sm:flex` (:571). On a 375px phone a list row shows only logo, name, city, two chips and an inline date — no fee, no delegate count, no apply affordance. Fee is one of the top two comparison facts for a student. → _Keep the fee on mobile: move it into the same wrap row as the format/level chips (next to the existing `sm:hidden` date), rendered as the compact `£450` / `FREE` pill. Drop the delegate count on mobile instead — it is the least decision-relevant number._  `[responsive]`

## Explore — list row logo

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:470`** — The list row hand-rolls its own 64px logo disc with a raw `<img>` and no `onError`, while the grid card uses the shared `LogoDisc` (which owns the near-white #FDFCF9 disc, the 7% inner margin, the hairline rim and the forest+gold monogram fallback). A dead logo URL leaves a blank white circle in the list and a proper monogram in the grid. → _Replace lines 459-481 with `<LogoDisc src={conf.logo_url} alt={conf.acronym} size={64} fallbackText={initials} />`, keeping only the hover box-shadow via the `style` prop._  `[consistency]`

## Explore — list row CTA

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:595`** — The gold pill says "APPLY →" but `onCta` (:432) does `router.push('/conferences/' + slug)` — exactly what clicking anywhere else on the row does. The button promises an application flow and delivers the same conference page. → _Either relabel to "VIEW →" (matching the member state's label), or point it at `/conferences/${slug}/apply` so the label is true. Same applies to ConferenceCard's ApplyButton._  `[copy]`

## Explore — grid card

- **`src/app/conferences/ConferenceCard.tsx:296`** — The grid card is `<article onClick={onClick} className="cursor-pointer">` with no `href`, no `role`, no `tabIndex`. The list row is a real `<Link>`. So in grid view a student cannot cmd-click to open three conferences in tabs to compare them, cannot tab to a card, and crawlers see no link — while in list view all of that works. → _Wrap the card in `<Link href={`/conferences/${conf.slug}`}>` (as ConferenceListRow already does) and drop the `onClick`/`router.push` from the explore call site at :1163. That also fixes keyboard access for free._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/conferences/ConferenceCard.tsx:168`** — `<style>{PHOTO_REDUCED_MOTION_CSS}</style>` is emitted inside every `heroCompact` card instance, so a rail of N cards injects N identical `<style>` tags into the DOM. → _Hoist the rule into globals.css (or emit it once from the rail's parent), keeping the `.gv-photo-card` class hooks as they are._  `[polish]`

## Explore — grid card apply pill

- **`src/app/conferences/ConferenceCard.tsx:566`** — `ApplyButton` renders a `<button type="button">` with hover handlers but **no onClick** — it relies on the click bubbling to the parent article. It is keyboard-focusable, and pressing Enter on it does nothing. There is also no `:focus-visible` style anywhere on it. → _Make it a non-interactive `<span>` styled as a pill (the whole card is the target anyway), or give it a real onClick + focus ring. Also unify the element type with the APPLIED state, which is already a `<span>` (:545) — right now the three states are span/button/button with different font sizes (10.5 / 10.5 / 11px)._  `[polish]`

## Explore — grid card format chip

- **`src/app/conferences/ConferenceCard.tsx:369`** — The format chip is `fontSize: '9px'` with `letterSpacing: '0.12em'` in `#FAF8F3` over `rgba(20,36,27,0.45)` + backdrop-blur, sitting on an arbitrary user-uploaded banner photo. On a bright banner the 45% scrim is not enough for 9px type. → _Raise to 10.5px, drop letter-spacing to 0.08em, and deepen the chip fill to `rgba(20,36,27,0.62)`. Match the date chip beside it, which is already 11.5px._  `[contrast]`

## Explore — grid card fee bubble

- **`src/app/conferences/ConferenceCard.tsx:387`** — Inside one ~30px-tall pill there are three type treatments: currency symbol 14.5px/800 in #B6871F, amount 16.5px/800 in #1C1410, and (in the free case) "FREE" at 14px/800 in #2A5A3C with a different border colour. Three sizes and three colours for one number. → _Set the symbol and amount to the same 16px/800 and colour the symbol #B6871F only (drop the size delta), and make FREE 16px too so the bubble is one optical height regardless of state._  `[hierarchy]`

## Explore — grid card name

- **`src/app/conferences/ConferenceCard.tsx:428`** — The card heading is `appendEditionYear(conf.acronym, year)` and the full conference name is never rendered — not as a second line, not even as a `title` attribute. A student who does not already know "TEIMUN" has no idea what the conference is. This inverts the AGENTS.md rule (acronym big, full name small beneath), which PersonalConferenceCard already implements via `acronymForward`. → _Add the full name as an 11px `NEU.muted` truncated line directly under the h3 (mirroring `acronymForward`'s `secondary`), or at minimum `title={conf.full_name}` on the h3._  `[rule-violation]`

## Explore — grid card delegate count

- **`src/app/conferences/ConferenceCard.tsx:452`** — The foot row shows a Users icon plus a bare number ("1,200"). Nothing says whether that is delegates, committees, spots left or attendees. The detail page labels the same field "Delegates". → _Render `{n} delegates` (or `{n} expected`) at the same 13px — the pill has room, and the label is what makes the number comparable across cards._  `[copy]`

## Explore — comparison facts

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:801`** — Neither card variant nor the filter bar exposes the application deadline or spots remaining, and the only sort is by date. A student choosing between conferences has to open every conference page one at a time to find out which ones are still open and by when. → _Fetch `applications_close_at` alongside the delegate fee configs already fetched at :687-694, render a "Apply by 12 Mar" line on both card variants (amber when ≤14 days), and add a fee sort next to `DateSortChip`._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Explore — country mode

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:837`** — `const displayed = countryMode ? sorted.slice(0, 4) : sorted` silently drops results. The only signal is a small " · 4 OF 12" fragment appended inside the uppercase 11.5px rule label at :1066 — easy to miss, and there is no "show all 12" control, only "Explore all conferences" which resets the region to worldwide. → _Either drop the slice, or add an explicit "Show all 12 in {country}" button under the grid that lifts the cap without clearing the region._  `[hierarchy]`

## Explore — date formats

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:381`** — `splitDateRange` renders "Jul 13 – Jul 17" over "2026" in the list, while `formatDateRange` in ConferenceCard.tsx:90 renders "13–17 Jul 2026" in the grid. Same field, same page, two formats depending on a view toggle. The detail hero uses a third variant ("13 – 17 Jul 2026", ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:196). → _Move one `formatConferenceDates(start, end, {twoLine?})` helper into a shared lib and call it from all three._  `[consistency]`

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:504`** — The list row shows a level chip ("High School" / "University") and derives the format label from `FORMAT_LABELS` ("In person"); the grid card shows no level at all and derives its own label with `conf.format.toUpperCase().replace('-',' ')` ("IN PERSON"). Flipping the view toggle silently changes which facts you can compare and how they are spelled. → _Export `FORMAT_LABELS`/`LEVEL_LABELS` from one module, use them in both, and add the level chip to the grid card's foot row._  `[consistency]`

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:962`** — The primary search input is `className="… focus:outline-none"` with `border: 'none'` and a transparent background — when focused there is no visual change whatsoever, so keyboard users cannot tell where focus is inside the glass bar. → _On focus, tint the input's own wrapper (e.g. `boxShadow: NEU.inSm` on the flex container) or add a 1.5px `#1B3828` underline. Also add a clear-"×" button once `searchQuery` is non-empty._  `[polish]`

## Explore — filter bar shape

- **`src/app/conferences/explore/ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:946`** — The glass bar is `borderRadius: 9999px` with an inner `flex-wrap md:flex-nowrap` row. Below `md` the search box (`minWidth:190px`), region control, date chip, view toggle and FILTERS button wrap to 2–3 rows inside a fully-rounded pill, producing a tall stadium blob with large dead corners. → _Switch `borderRadius` to `24px` whenever the row wraps (below `md`), or collapse region/date/view into a single "Filters" sheet on mobile so the bar stays one row._  `[responsive]`

## Conference page — deadline

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:1063`** — `applications_close_at` is selected (:712) and used only inside `getRoleWindowStatus` to compute an open/closed boolean. The actual closing date is never printed anywhere on the page. Only the *opening* date is ever shown ("Opens 12 Mar", :2082), and only for roles that are not yet open. The single question this page exists to answer — "by when do I have to apply?" — has no answer on it. → _Print "Apply by {fmtWindowDate(r.applications_close_at)}" on each open role row in the picker, and surface the soonest close date as a line in the dark Apply card itself (amber when ≤14 days, e.g. "Applications close in 6 days · 12 Mar")._  `[wasted-effort]`

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:2007`** — The state most first-time visitors land in shows only "Ready to take the floor? / Sign in with a free account to start your application. / APPLY NOW →". No fee, no role list, no deadline, no open-or-closed signal — all of which the page has already loaded in `roleConfigs`. The signed-in card at :2039 shows strictly more. The least-informed viewer gets the least information. → _Render the same `enabledRoles` list (role name + today's fee + open/closed) in the signed-out branch, read-only, with the sign-in CTA beneath it. Nothing in that list is private._  `[hierarchy]`

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:2045`** — When `hasOpenRoles` is false the card says "Applications are currently closed." and then still renders a full-brightness gold APPLY NOW button that expands to a list where every row is `disabled`, `opacity:0.55`, and says "Applications closed". Two clicks to learn what the line above already said. → _When `!hasOpenRoles`, replace the gold CTA with a muted, non-expanding panel that lists each role and when it opens/closed, plus a "Notify me when applications open" affordance if you have one._  `[wasted-effort]`

The fee, deadline and Apply CTA are all below the fold on the page whose job is "should I apply, and by when". → _Make the floor viewport-relative on small screens, e.g. `clamp(240px, 44vw, 512px)`, so the stat strip and the top of the dark Apply card land above the fold on a 667px viewport._  `[responsive]`

## Conference page — stat strip

Format is already printed as a pill in the hero (:1188), level is rarely a tie-breaker, and the expected-delegate count is a vanity number. Fee and application deadline, the two facts that decide the click, are not there. → _Swap the strip to Fee (today's active phase) · Applications close · Delegates, and leave Format/Level as small chips in the hero where format already lives._  `[hierarchy]`

## Conference page — tabs

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:1428`** — The sticky tab pill is three 72×46 icon-only buttons — a Landmark, a UserRound and a Star — with labels only in `aria-label`/`title`. "Landmark = Overview" and "UserRound = your application" are not guessable, and `title` never appears on touch. The active tab is 72px wide with a 21px icon centred in it: a lot of chrome carrying no words. → _Show the text label inside the active pill (icon + "Overview"), keep inactive tabs icon-only, and animate the width — the transition list at :1436 already animates `width`, so the machinery exists._  `[copy]`

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:2125`** — A 150px circle with a gold radial wash and an 8px outer ring holds one 38px number and an 8.5px "PER DELEGATE" caption — roughly 190px of sidebar height for a value already shown on the delegate row inside the APPLY NOW picker. On mobile the sidebar is `order-first`, so this decorative medallion sits between the Apply CTA and all page content. → _Shrink to a plain fee row inside the dark Apply card ("Delegate · £450 today"), and keep the medallion for desktop only — or drop it and promote the per-role fee table (currently hidden behind PRICING DETAILS) into its place._  `[density]`

## Conference page — pricing details

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:2156`** — The per-role fee table with fee phases — the thing a delegate, a chair and an advisor each need to see their own price, and the only place early-bird windows appear — is collapsed behind a 11px "PRICING DETAILS ⌄" text button and closed by default. → _Default `pricingOpen` to true on desktop (there is room in the sticky rail), or inline each role's fee into the role picker rows where the decision is actually made._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Conference page — committee cards

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:2480`** — Committee cards render `{c.name}` alone at 15.5px, so "Disarmament and International Security Committee" wraps to three lines while `c.abbreviation` ("DISEC") is used only for the monogram medallion. The AllocationCard (:76) and the roster modal (:2694) do the same. `committeeDisplayName` from src/lib/presetNames.ts exists for exactly this and has zero call sites under src/app/conferences/. → _Render `committeeDisplayName(c.name, c.abbreviation)` as the primary 17px label with `c.name` as an 11px NEU.muted second line beneath it (only when the two differ) — the pattern CommitteesSessionsCard.tsx:101-108 already uses correctly._  `[rule-violation]`

## Conference page — committee card CTA

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:2629`** — The button reads "VIEW MEMBERS" (or "VIEW ROLES" for crisis) but the modal it opens is a list of *countries* with TAKEN/OPEN seat chips — no person's name appears anywhere. "Members" promises people. → _Label it "VIEW COUNTRIES" / "VIEW ROLES", or better "12 of 40 SEATS OPEN →" so the button carries the number the student is scanning for._  `[copy]`

## Conference page — committee slider

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:2406`** — `justifyContent: 'safe center'` on the overflowing flex row. The `safe` keyword is not supported in older Safari (and several mobile WebKit versions), where it degrades to plain `center` — which in an overflowing flex container clips the leading cards and makes them unreachable by scrolling. The comment above the line assumes the safe fallback works everywhere. → _Use `justifyContent: 'flex-start'` plus `margin-inline: auto` on the track when it fits, or add `@supports (justify-content: safe center)` with a `flex-start` fallback. Failing that, keep `flex-start` — a left-aligned rail is never broken._  `[responsive]`

## Conference page — committee slider (mobile)

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:2361`** — The prev/next arrows are `hidden md:flex`, and the track sets `scrollbarWidth:'none'` + `msOverflowStyle:'none'`. On a phone, a conference with 12 committees shows one 298px card with no arrows, no scrollbar, no dots and no peek beyond the container padding — nothing indicates the other eleven exist. → _Either show a dot/counter row ("1 / 12") below the track on mobile, or reduce the card width to ~270px so the next card peeks by ~30px at 375px, which is the standard swipe affordance._  `[responsive]`

## Conference page — committee sort bar

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:2318`** — Three identically-styled pills sit in one glass tray, but DIFFICULTY and AVAILABILITY sort while GA / CRISIS filters (`sortedCommittees = sortedCommittees.filter(...)`, :2277). Nothing visually separates the sort from the filter, and the filter's resting label "GA / CRISIS" reads as the current state rather than an action. → _Give the type control a distinct treatment (a segmented GA | Crisis | All control, or move it left of a hairline divider), and label its resting state "ALL TYPES"._  `[consistency]`

## Conference page — sort arrows

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:362`** — `SortButton` renders `ArrowDown` for `dir === 'asc'` and `ArrowUp` for `'desc'` — inverted from the universal convention. "AVAILABILITY ↓" currently means fewest-open-seats-first. → _Swap the two branches, or drop arrows entirely and put the meaning in the label ("MOST AVAILABLE" / "LEAST AVAILABLE", "EASIEST FIRST" / "HARDEST FIRST") — that removes the ambiguity completely._  `[polish]`

## Conference page — roster modal

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:2718`** — The country list is `maxHeight: '54vh'` in a `max-w-md` dialog with no search. A GA committee with 60 countries becomes a ~360px scroll window on desktop and ~180px in landscape on a phone, scanned one 44px row at a time to find whether a specific country is open. → _Add a country search input in the modal header (the same typeahead the apply flow's country chips could reuse), and a "Show only open" toggle. Bump the list to `min(70vh, 520px)`._  `[responsive]`

## Conference page — committee card

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:2482`** — The committee `<h3>` carries `minHeight: '2.6em'`, so every short name ("DISEC", "UNSC") leaves ~24px of forced empty space above the meta row. It is a fixed reservation to keep cards aligned, but in a horizontal slider where cards are not vertically compared it just adds air. → _Drop `minHeight` and instead pin the meta/topics/dais block to the bottom with the `mt-auto` wrapper already present at :2531 — alignment is preserved without the gap._  `[density]`

## Conference page — avatars

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:1612`** — Secretariat avatars (:1612) and dais chair avatars (:2541) are raw `<img src={m.avatar_url}>` with no `onError`. A broken or expired avatar URL leaves a 72px / 52px empty `#EDE7D8` circle with a hairline outline, while the no-avatar path right beside it renders a proper forest disc with the person's initial. → _Track a failed-src set (the pattern in LogoDisc.tsx:65) and fall back to the same initials disc the null branch already renders._  `[missing-state]`

## Conference page — hero logo (mobile)

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:1196`** — The 150px conference logo is `hidden sm:block`. On a phone the conference's own mark never appears anywhere on its page — the hero shows a stock banner and text only, and the next logo instance is inside the ORGANISED BY card far below. → _Render a 56px `LogoDisc` inline before the acronym eyebrow below `sm` instead of hiding it entirely._  `[responsive]`

## Conference page — difficulty pills

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:212`** — `DIFFICULTY_STYLES.expert` is `{ bg: 'rgba(139,32,32,0.1)', color: '#8B2020' }` — the app's danger red. An "Expert" committee reads as an error/warning badge rather than a prestige tier. `intermediate` is `#8A6614` on `rgba(238,217,138,0.35)` over the #FAF8F3 card, which lands around 3.4:1 — under AA for 10px text. → _Move expert to the deep-gold family (`#B6871F` on `rgba(182,135,31,0.12)`) so the four tiers escalate green → gold → amber → deep gold, and darken the intermediate text to `#6E4F0C`._  `[contrast]`

## Conference page — reviews tab

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:1737`** — With zero reviews and a viewer who did not attend, the whole tab is one card saying "No reviews yet / Reviews appear once delegates attend an edition of this conference." — a dead end with no next action, reached from a tab that gave no hint it would be empty. There is also no rating distribution, no sort and no filter once reviews exist. → _Add a secondary link out of the empty state ("Back to overview" / "Explore other conferences"), show the review count on the Reviews tab itself so the emptiness is predicted, and add a 5→1 star distribution bar under the average once `reviewCount > 0`._  `[missing-state]`

## Apply — wizard progress

- **`src/components/wizard.tsx:55`** — Progress is a row of 8px dots with a 26px active pill and an `aria-label="Step 3 of 6"` on the container — no visible number, no step names. `stepLabels` is computed in ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:1005 (Society / Invoicing / Preferences / Experience / Questions / Overview) and then **never used**. The applicant cannot tell how much effort remains or what is coming. → _Render "STEP 3 OF 6 · PREFERENCES" as a 10px uppercase NEU.muted line under the dots, fed by the already-computed `stepLabels[step-1]`. One line, zero new data._  `[hierarchy]`

## Apply — preference ranking

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:2515`** — The ranking panel's only instruction is "Drag to reorder your preferences." and `RankedRow` reorders exclusively via native HTML5 drag (`draggable` + onDragStart/Drop, :576-580). HTML5 drag-and-drop does not fire on touch devices, so on a phone — where a large share of applicants will do this — the ranking cannot be reordered at all. The ArrowUp/ArrowDown keyboard fallback (:595-597) is on a `role="button"` grip with `focus:outline-none` and is not advertised anywhere. → _Add visible ▲/▼ buttons to each RankedRow beside the existing remove ×, wired to the `onMove` prop that already exists and is already passed in (:2529). That is the non-drag fallback the flow is missing, and it fixes touch, keyboard and discoverability in one change. Update the hint to "Drag, or use the arrows, to reorder."_  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:591`** — The 6-dot grip is `role="button" tabIndex={0}` with `className="… focus:outline-none"` and no replacement focus style, and its hit area is only `px-1 py-2` (~14×22px) — well under the 44px touch minimum. → _Give it a visible focus ring (`box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px #B6871F` on `:focus-visible`), pad to at least 32×36, and set `aria-keyshortcuts="ArrowUp ArrowDown"` plus a `title` so the shortcut is discoverable._  `[polish]`

## Apply — preferences picker

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:2586`** — The committee picker is a nested scroll region: `maxHeight: '58vh'; overflowY: 'auto'; overscrollBehavior: 'contain'`. On a phone that is a ~380px window inside a page that also scrolls, with overscroll chaining deliberately disabled — the classic scroll-trap. The ranking panel sits above it, so the applicant is scrolling two axes of the same list. → _Below `md`, drop the fixed height and let the picker flow in the page (the scroll-anchor logic at :841-849 already prevents the jump the nested box was added to solve). Keep the 58vh box for desktop only._  `[responsive]`

## Apply — binary choice cards

- **`src/components/wizard.tsx:263`** — `TwoTabPick` hardcodes `gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr))'` with `minHeight: 400` and a 248px image per card. At 375px each card is ~160px wide and 400px tall with a 25px label and a 15px sub — a very tall, very narrow pair. There is no single-column breakpoint. It backs the Independent/Delegation choice, the pledge yes/no, and three steps of the creation wizard. → _Add `@media (max-width: 520px) { grid-template-columns: 1fr; min-height: 240px; }` (image height ~140px) so the two cards stack as wide, short tiles on phones._  `[responsive]`

## Apply — card grids

- **`src/components/wizard.tsx:452`** — `CardSelect` takes a fixed `columns` number with no breakpoints (`repeat(${columns}, minmax(0,1fr))`). It is called with `columns={3}` for the 193-country picker and `columns={4}` for delegate ranges — at 375px that is four ~80px-wide cards each `minHeight: 156` holding a 48px emoji and a truncated label. → _Make `columns` a max: `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(112px, 1fr))` capped at the requested count, or accept `{ base, sm, md }` and drop to 2 columns below 520px._  `[responsive]`

## Apply — keyboard focus

- **`src/components/wizard.tsx:215`** — `cardBaseStyle` sets `outline: 'none'` on every wizard card. `TwoTabPick` adds `className="… focus-visible:ring-2"` with no ring-colour utility (Tailwind v4 defaults the ring to `currentColor`, which on these cards is near-invisible), and `CardSelect` adds no focus class at all. Keyboard users navigating an 11-step creation wizard have no focus indicator on the primary controls. → _Replace `outline:'none'` with a `:focus-visible` box-shadow in the same shadow stack, e.g. `0 0 0 3px rgba(182,135,31,0.55), ${CARD_FLOAT}`, applied in `cardBaseStyle` so both consumers inherit it._  `[polish]`

## Apply — draft persistence

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:1077`** — State is snapshotted to localStorage only around the buy-credits round trip (`resumeKey`, restored at :1077-1090). Any other interruption — refresh, browser back, a dead tab, following the "Add now →" link to /account/cv from the roles modal — loses the society, all eight ranked preferences and every custom-question answer. → _Reuse the existing snapshot shape: write it on every `setStep` and restore it on mount whenever `savedAt` is within the same 2-hour window, showing a small "Draft restored" chip. The serialisation code already exists at the goBuyCredits call site._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Apply — invoicing step

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:2374`** — One screen asks two structurally identical yes/no questions in two completely different visual languages: "Paying for delegation spots?" gets a pair of 400px-tall TwoTabPick image cards, while "Paying for advisor tickets?" directly below gets two 40px outlined text pills. The second question reads as a minor setting rather than a second financial commitment. → _Use one language for both — either two compact segmented controls (and keep the step short), or split into two steps. If the visual weight difference is deliberate priority, make the advisor question a disclosure that opens only when relevant._  `[consistency]`

## Apply — society typeahead

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:2218`** — The society suggestion list is `className="absolute left-0 right-0 …"` with `zIndex: 20` inside the wizard column — not portaled, so it is clippable by any ancestor overflow and cannot flip when it would run off the bottom of the viewport. AGENTS.md names ConferenceRosterPicker's typeahead as the reference pattern for exactly this control. → _Portal it at fixed coordinates from the input's `getBoundingClientRect()`, exactly as `CreditInfoTip` in this same file already does (:232-258)._  `[rule-violation]`
- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:2228`** — Suggestions are plain `<button>`s selected via `onMouseDown` with a 150ms `setTimeout` blur close (:2207). There is no `role="listbox"`/`option`, no `aria-activedescendant`, and no ArrowUp/ArrowDown/Enter handling — you cannot pick a society with the keyboard, only with the mouse. → _Add arrow-key navigation with a highlighted index and Enter-to-select, and set the ARIA combobox roles. The chat DM picker in this codebase already supports Enter-to-select and can be copied._  `[polish]`

## Apply — unknown society

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:2281`** — A plain delegate who types a society that does not exist gets a grey paragraph inside the dropdown: "This delegation has not been created. Please ask your Head Delegate or Faculty Advisor to create it." It is a dead end — there is no button to switch to Independent from there, even though that is the obvious escape and the toggle is 400px above. → _Append a "Apply independently instead →" button under that message that calls `setIsIndependent(true)` and closes the dropdown._  `[copy]`

## Apply — overview

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:3108`** — The step is titled "Overview / Review your application before submitting" and then the application recap is collapsed behind a "Your application ⌄" toggle (`recapOpen` defaults false), while the credit-balance card, the plan pill, the trial notice and two credit-purchase photo cards are all expanded below it. The screen leads with billing and hides the thing it asked you to review. → _Default `recapOpen` to true. If vertical length is the concern, collapse the credit/upgrade block instead — most applicants have a credit and never need it._  `[hierarchy]`

## Apply — credits

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:3241`** — When gated, the same screen carries: the cost card with balance + plan pill, a red "Out of credits? Buy more or upgrade your subscription!" note, and then a "NEED MORE CREDITS?" section with two 118px photo cards each containing its own buy/upgrade CTA plus a third "Yearly billing available" link. That is four routes to the same purchase stacked above the Submit button. → _Keep the two photo cards (they are the good surface) and delete the red note's link — turn it into a plain sentence pointing down ("You need one more credit to submit — add one below.")._  `[density]`

## Apply — upgrade cards

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:655`** — `UpgradePhotoCard` puts its entire explanation ("Each credit gives one delegate fee-free access to one conference. Unused credits never expire.") behind `hovered` — on touch, `hoverText` is never rendered and the card shows only an eyebrow, a title and a price. The paid decision is being asked with the explanation hidden. → _Below `md` (or under `@media (hover: none)`), render `hoverText` permanently as a 12px line in the footer slot instead of the hover overlay._  `[missing-state]`

## Apply — withdraw

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:3408`** — "Withdraw application" — an irreversible destructive action — is a 12px muted underlined text link, centred, with no icon, that only turns red on hover. It reads as a footnote. → _Keep it secondary but make it legible as destructive at rest: 13px, `#8B2020` at 70% opacity with a small RotateCcw/X icon, in a bordered ghost pill. The two-step confirm it opens is already good._  `[polish]`

## Apply — questions step

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx:3005`** — `renderStepQuestions` paginates by Section (`splitIntoSections`), so one wizard dot can hide 1 or 6 screens. The dot row does not move between question pages, so an applicant on page 2 of 5 sees the same progress indicator as on page 1 and cannot tell how much is left. → _Render a secondary sub-progress line inside the step ("Section 2 of 5 · Motivation"), or expand the wizard dots to include each section page._  `[missing-state]`

## Confirmation

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/confirmation/page.tsx:212`** — The PAYMENT PORTAL button always renders, even when `timing === 'after_acceptance'` and the paragraph six lines above says "If accepted, payment will become available in your conference view." The applicant is offered a payment button that leads to a page with nothing to pay. → _Render the second button only when `timing === 'after_application'` (or when `timing` is absent/'anytime'). Otherwise show a single full-width CONFERENCE PORTAL button._  `[copy]`
- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/confirmation/page.tsx:210`** — The same destination has three names across the flow: "CONFERENCE PORTAL →" here, "VIEW YOUR DASHBOARD" in the detail sidebar (ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:2002), and the tab is labelled "You" (:1425). "Portal" is jargon the applicant has not encountered anywhere else in the flow. → _Pick one name and use it in all three places. "Your dashboard" is the clearest of the three._  `[copy]`
- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/confirmation/page.tsx:93`** — The page never names the conference. Someone who applies to three conferences in one sitting gets three byte-identical confirmations, and the browser-back/history entries are indistinguishable. The slug is right there in `useParams`. → _Put the conference logo + acronym above the eyebrow ("APPLICATION SUBMITTED · TEIMUN 2026"). Both are one anon query away, or can be passed as a query param from the submit call._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/confirmation/page.tsx:230`** — Two ALL-CAPS letterspaced buttons are followed by a lowercase, non-capitalised "explore more conferences" link. The page mixes three casings (Sentence case headline, ALL CAPS buttons, all-lowercase link). → _Make the tertiary link sentence case ("Explore more conferences") to match the headline, or ALL CAPS to match the buttons — anything but a third convention._  `[consistency]`

## Confirmation — timeline

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/confirmation/page.tsx:132`** — The three-step timeline is a fixed 3-column `flex` inside a 440px-max card with `px-5`. At 375px each column is roughly 90px wide holding a 44px disc, a 12.5px label and a 10.5px two-word sub ("The team reads it over") — the subs wrap to three lines each. → _Stack the timeline vertically below 480px (disc on the left, label + sub on the right, connector as a vertical rule), which also lets the subs be full sentences._  `[responsive]`

## Confirmation / Public CV

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/confirmation/page.tsx:57`** — `@keyframes gvRise` staggered entrance animations run unconditionally (also PublicCVClient.tsx:50, 70, 108, 114). Everywhere else in the codebase motion is guarded — ConferenceCard ships PHOTO_REDUCED_MOTION_CSS and the apply flow has a `useReducedMotion` hook. → _Wrap both keyframe blocks in `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)`, or add a `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { animation: none !important; }` rule next to the keyframes._  `[polish]`

## Participant — locked content

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/participant/PayGate.tsx:27`** — `LockedCard` says "Settle your fee below and this section opens up right away." Payment no longer lives below it — ParticipantView's own header comment states "Payment itself lives on its own /pay page now, reached from the 'YOUR APPLICATION' card", which is the dark sidebar card (to the right on desktop, above on mobile). The card gives no link at all, so the copy points at nothing and the user has to hunt. → _Fix the copy ("Pay your registration fee to unlock this section.") and add a primary `<Link href={`/conferences/${slug}/pay`}>PAY NOW →</Link>` inside the card. `conferenceSlug` is already threaded into this file for `RejectedCard`._  `[copy]`

## Participant — allocation card

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/participant/AllocationCard.tsx:66`** — The committee emblem is a raw `<img src={committee.logo_url}>` with no error handling, while the null branch right below renders `MonogramMedallion`. A broken logo URL leaves a 104px blank on the single most important card in the participant view. → _Use `LogoDisc` (or track the failed src and fall through to `MonogramMedallion`) so the two paths converge on the same fallback._  `[consistency]`

## Participant — session code

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/participant/AllocationCard.tsx:138`** — The session-code well shows a mono string and a copy icon with no label. Nothing says it is the code for the live committee session, and the trailing `minWidth: 42` spacer reserved for "Copied" leaves the code visually off-centre until you press it. → _Add a 9px "SESSION CODE" eyebrow above the well (matching "YOUR COUNTRY" directly above), and render the copied confirmation as an absolutely-positioned toast rather than a reserved 42px column so the resting layout is centred._  `[copy]`

## Participant — no allocation yet

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/participant/AllocationCard.tsx:36`** — The empty state is a single line — "Your committee allocation will appear here." — with no indication of when that is, what depends on it, or what to do meanwhile. Compare the sibling `CommitteesSessionsCard`, which does show "Sessions open {date}". → _Add a second muted line with the expectation ("Allocations are usually released a few weeks before the conference — we'll email you.") and, when the conference has a release date, print it the same way `formatReleaseDate` already does._  `[missing-state]`

## Participant — role switcher

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/participant/ParticipantView.tsx:195`** — Each role pill carries a 6px status dot whose colour is the only signal of the application's state (green assigned, gold accepted, muted submitted, red rejected — STATUS_DOT at :44). There is no legend, no tooltip and no text, so status is conveyed by colour alone at 6px. → _Add `title={app.status}` at minimum, and ideally render the status word as a second line inside the active pill — the pill is 3.5×2 padding and has room._  `[contrast]`

## Public CV

- **`src/app/cv/[id]/PublicCVClient.tsx:10`** — `PublicProfile` declares (and `get_public_cv` returns) `bio`, `education_level` and `mun_experience_level`, and none of the three is rendered. The hero is avatar + name + nationality only, so a CV shared into an application review shows no self-description and no MUN rank — the two things a reader wants after the name. → _Render `bio` as a 14px line under the nationality row (clamped to ~3 lines), and put `mun_experience_level` in the hero as the same `LevelInsignia` + label chip the apply flow uses._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/app/cv/[id]/PublicCVClient.tsx:59`** — The header CTA and the footer CTA are both "Build your MUN CV" with the same arrow and both `href="/"` — the marketing landing page, not a CV builder. The label promises a builder and delivers the homepage, twice on one short page. → _Point both at `/account/cv` (Next will bounce a signed-out visitor through sign-in), and differentiate: keep the header one as a compact "Get yours", and let the footer card be the fuller pitch it already is._  `[copy]`
- **`src/app/cv/[id]/PublicCVClient.tsx:52`** — A page whose entire purpose is being pasted into chats and applications has no share affordance — no copy-link button, no "share" action — even though `ShareAchievementModal` already exists in the codebase (used by /account/cv). → _Add a small "Copy link" ghost button next to the header CTA, using the same copy-with-check-confirmation pattern as AllocationCard's session code._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/app/cv/[id]/PublicCVClient.tsx:52`** — This is the only public page in the conferences surface with neither `SiteNav` nor `FooterLegal` — it hand-rolls a 60px bar with a plain "GAVELLING" wordmark. Explore, the conference page, the roles board and the apply confirmation all use SiteNav + FooterLegal. → _Either use `SiteNav` (it already has an overlay/slim variant) or, if the stripped bar is deliberate for a shared artefact, add `FooterLegal tone="ivory"` so the legal links are not missing on a public page._  `[consistency]`

## Creation wizard — draft loss

- **`src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx:13`** — The file's own header states "Progress lives in component state only, a refresh restarts the wizard." It is an 11-step flow that includes a logo crop + upload and a banner upload. A refresh, an accidental browser-back, or following any link out of the flow discards every answer — the uploaded assets survive in storage under `conferenceIdRef` but become orphans nothing points at. → _Persist the answer object to localStorage under a per-user key on every `goTo`, restore on mount with a "Continue where you left off / Start over" prompt, and clear it on successful create. The apply flow's snapshot code is a working template._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Creation wizard — logo step

- **`src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx:970`** — Step 8 is commented MANDATORY, the Continue button is disabled without a logo, and clicking it sets the error "A logo is required to continue." — and then a "Do this later" skip link sits 12px below it that advances anyway (:975). The review row then reports "Logo — Skipped". The screen tells the user it is required and simultaneously offers the bypass. → _Decide: if skippable, drop the error string and enable Continue (make Continue itself the skip); if required, remove the SkipLink. The sub-copy "Every conference needs a logo" suggests you want it required._  `[copy]`

## Creation wizard — delegate count

- **`src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx:825`** — The four range cards read "Up to 50", "~100", "~250", "500+" but `onChange` does `setExpectedDelegates(k)` where `k` is the literal string "50"/"100"/"250"/"500". Picking "500+" stores exactly 500, which then appears on the public card and the detail stat tile as a precise "500". The labels promise a bracket and the system records a point value. → _Either store the midpoint and label the cards as brackets you are honest about ("About 250"), or relabel the cards to the exact values they store ("50", "100", "250", "500") and keep the range language only in the `sub` line._  `[copy]`
- **`src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx:842`** — The primary Continue button is disabled until a number exists, while the actual way past the step — the muted underlined "Do this later" link — sits below it. A greyed-out primary with a live escape hatch underneath reads as a broken screen rather than an optional question. → _When the step is genuinely optional, make the primary button the skip: enable it and label it "Skip for now" when empty, "Continue" once filled (the pattern `WizardFooter` in the apply flow already implements with its `primary` flag)._  `[hierarchy]`

## Creation wizard — contact email

- **`src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx:1122`** — Organizer contact email is required for creation (`readyToCreate` at :571 and the button's `!contactEmail.trim()` guard) but is never asked in any of the ten question steps — it appears for the first time as a bare input on the review screen, below eleven review rows, with no required marker. If the profile has no email prefilled, the user reaches "Ready to create it?" with a silently disabled CREATE button. → _Either give it its own step (it belongs with step 1's name/acronym), or move it above the review rows with a "Required" tag and inline validation._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Creation wizard — create button

- **`src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx:1165`** — CREATE CONFERENCE is disabled by `readyToCreate` — a compound of nine conditions — with no message naming which one failed. NeuButton's disabled style is `background: rgba(27,56,40,0.14)` with muted text and no shadow, which reads as broken rather than blocked. The user has to guess which of eleven rows is incomplete. → _Keep the button enabled and, on click, scroll to and highlight the first incomplete ReviewRow with an inline message; or render a list of what is still missing above the button._  `[missing-state]`

## Creation wizard — currency

- **`src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx:875`** — The currency picker is a native `<select>` — the only native control in the entire wizard — styled with `bigInputStyle`, so it renders OS chrome (a system arrow, a system dropdown, a platform font for the option list) inside a fully custom neumorphic system. It also uses `<option disabled>──────────</option>` as a group separator instead of `<optgroup>`. → _Replace with the same portaled listbox pattern used elsewhere (or at minimum add a custom chevron + `appearance: none`), and use `<optgroup label="Common">` / `<optgroup label="All currencies">` for the split._  `[consistency]`

## Creation wizard — banner presets

- **`src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx:1012`** — `gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(5, minmax(0, 1fr))'` is fixed at five columns. At 375px each preset tile is roughly 58px wide by 78px tall — a strip of unreadable slivers — and the hover `scale(1.05)` + gold glow that carries all the affordance never fires on touch. → _Use `repeat(auto-fill, minmax(96px, 1fr))` so it collapses to 3 then 2 columns, and give the selected state a visible border on touch (it already has the gold check, which is good)._  `[responsive]`

## Creation wizard — dates step

- **`src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx:750`** — The two DatePickers are in a hard `grid grid-cols-2 gap-4` inside a padded NeuInset. At 375px that leaves each date field about 140px wide, and the shared DatePicker's trigger has to fit a formatted date plus its calendar icon. → _Use `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2` so the two dates stack on phones._  `[responsive]`

## Creation wizard

- **`src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx:138`** — `TertiaryPick` uses `transition: all 220ms ${EASE}` (:138) and `ReviewRow` uses `transition: all 220ms ${EASE}` (:1215). `transition: all` is banned by the house style and here animates `box-shadow`, `transform`, `border`, `background-color` and anything else that changes. → _Enumerate: `transition: border-color 220ms EASE, background-color 220ms EASE, box-shadow 220ms EASE, color 220ms EASE` for TertiaryPick and `box-shadow 220ms EASE, transform 220ms EASE` for ReviewRow._  `[polish]`

## Creation wizard — country step

- **`src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx:717`** — Picking one country from 193 is rendered as a searchable `CardSelect` — a scrolling 3-column grid (`maxHeight: 360`) of 156px-tall cards each holding a 42px flag. It is a lot of scrolling and a lot of pixels for a single-value choice the user already knows the answer to, and the visitor's country is not pre-selected even though the explore page already does a `/api/geo` lookup. → _Use the `ConferenceRosterPicker`-style typeahead (type two letters, hit Enter) with the grid as a secondary "browse all" disclosure, and pre-select the geo-detected country as a suggestion chip._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Creation wizard — exit

- **`src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx:605`** — There is no cancel/exit affordance anywhere in the wizard except the SiteNav logo — and leaving discards everything without warning (see the draft-loss finding). The wizard also never shows the URL the conference will live at, even on the review screen, though `generateSlug(fullName)` could preview it from step 1. → _Add a muted "Save and exit" / "Cancel" link under the step content, and show "gavelling.com/conferences/{slug}" as a muted line on the review screen so the organiser can sanity-check the public URL before committing._  `[missing-state]`

## Roles — coming soon

- **`src/app/conferences/roles/ConferencesRolesClient.tsx:997`** — `JOB_BOARD_ENABLED` is false so `ComingSoonScreen` always renders, but the early return sits *after* the effects: every visit still runs three network calls (all `job_postings` with joined conferences + committees, the viewer's `job_applications`, and a `mun_cv_entries` count) whose results are never displayed. → _Guard the `fetchAll` effect with `if (!JOB_BOARD_ENABLED) return;` — one line, and the flag stays a single switch._  `[wasted-effort]`
- **`src/app/conferences/roles/ConferencesRolesClient.tsx:727`** — The coming-soon card ends on three decorative glyph chips and offers no next action at all — no email capture, no "browse conferences instead", no link out.
