Every visitor who reaches it is a warm lead for chairing/staffing and leaves with nothing. → _Add a "Notify me when it opens" email input (or, if there is no capture endpoint yet, a secondary "Explore conferences →" link to /conferences/explore) below the glyph chips._  `[missing-state]`

## Conferences landing — dead code

- **`src/components/ConferenceFocusCards.tsx:34`** — `ConferenceFocusCards` is never rendered anywhere — only its `FocusCard` type is imported, by `src/app/conferences/ConferencesClient.tsx`, which is itself unreachable (`src/app/conferences/page.tsx` does `permanentRedirect('/')` and `/` renders StagefrontClient). ~730 lines of unreferenced conference-card UI that will drift out of sync with the real card and mislead anyone auditing this surface. → _Delete both files, or move `FocusCard` into a types module and delete the components. If the focus-cards composition is being kept as a design reference, move it under conferences/landing-lab/ with the other variants._  `[consistency]`

## Conferences landing — focus cards

- **`src/components/ConferenceFocusCards.tsx:80`** — If the component is ever revived: the conference name, date and city are inside an overlay with `opacity: hovered === card.id ? 1 : 0`, so on any touch device every card is an unlabelled photo. Separately, `index < 3` puts the overlay at the bottom and `index >= 3` at the top — an arbitrary split that makes the fourth card's caption jump to the opposite edge of an otherwise identical row. → _Always render the name at ~55% opacity and reveal the date/city on hover, and drop the `index < 3` branch so every card captions in the same place._  `[responsive]`

## Blog — related guides

- **`src/components/RelatedGuides.tsx:30`** — Each related-guide row uses `backgroundColor: '#EDE7D8'` — the exact page background token (NEU.base) — with a 1px `#DDD4C0` border. On an ivory page the cards read as flat outlines rather than surfaces, unlike every other card in the system which sits on `#F0EBDD`/`#FAF8F3` above the base. It also hardcodes hexes rather than importing NEU and has no hover state at all on what are the page's only navigation links. → _Switch the row background to `NEU.surface` and add a hover (border-left widening to 4px, or `NEU.outSm` shadow) so the rows read as clickable._  `[consistency]`

## Participant / detail — duplicate primitives

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/participant/shared.tsx:14`** — `SectionCard` is defined byte-identically in two places (here and ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:400) — same 20px radius, `#FAF8F3`, `#DDD4C0`, `0 1px 3px rgba(27,56,40,0.04)`. The comment says the duplication avoids an import cycle, which is fair, but the two will silently drift and the two halves of the same page will stop matching. → _Move `SectionCard` into a leaf module (e.g. `src/components/SectionCard.tsx`) that neither file imports the other from — that breaks the cycle without duplicating the style._  `[consistency]`

## Conference page — sidebar accents

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:1859`** — In roughly 500px of sticky sidebar there are five separate gold accents competing: the `#EED98A` eyebrow, the gold radial glow behind the card, the solid gold APPLY NOW button, the gold-tinted role rows (`rgba(238,217,138,0.08)` + `0.22` borders), and then the gold-ringed 150px pricing medallion in the card below. Nothing is clearly the single most important thing to press. → _Reserve solid `#EED98A` for the one primary action (APPLY NOW). Make the eyebrows `rgba(238,217,138,0.6)`, drop the medallion's `0 0 0 8px rgba(238,217,138,0.12)` outer ring, and let the role rows be neutral until hovered._  `[hierarchy]`

> **Method / limits:** METHOD — This audit is entirely from reading source in the working tree (branch feature/conferences-auth, uncommitted changes included). No dev server was started and no browser tooling was used, per the brief. Every line number was read directly from the file at the time of the audit; class strings and hex values are quoted verbatim from source.

FILES READ IN FULL OR IN SUBSTANTIAL PART: AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, src/components/neu.tsx, src/components/wizard.tsx, src/components/LogoDisc.tsx, src/components/PersonalConferenceCard.tsx, src/components/ConferenceFocusCards.tsx, src/components/DecorativeBleed.tsx, src/components/Skeleton.tsx, src/components/RelatedGuides.tsx, src/components/FilterPopover.tsx (partial), src/components/DraftNotice.tsx, src/app/conferences/ConferenceCard.tsx, src/app/conferences/explore/page.tsx + ConferencesExploreClient.tsx, src/app/conferences/[slug]/ConferenceDetailClient.tsx (~2000 of 3113 lines, the whole render tree), src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/ConferenceApplyClient.tsx (~1800 of 3761 lines, all step renderers + shared components), src/app/conferences/[slug]/apply/confirmation/page.tsx, src/app/conferences/[slug]/reviews/page.tsx, src/app/conferences/[slug]/role/page.tsx, src/app/conferences/[slug]/participant/{ParticipantView,AllocationCard,PayGate,ApplyPointer,shared,CommitteesSessionsCard}.tsx, src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx, src/app/conferences/roles/ConferencesRolesClient.tsx (partial), src/app/conferences/map/MapClient.tsx (partial), src/app/cv/[id]/{page,PublicCVClient}.tsx, src/app/conferences/StagefrontClient.tsx, src/app/conferences/page.tsx.

COVERAGE LIMITS — I did not do a line-by-line pass on: src/app/conferences/[slug]/pay/page.tsx (2341 lines, not in the stated scope list), src/app/conferences/map/MapClient.tsx beyond the render tree, src/app/conferences/landing-lab/** (design lab, and StagefrontClient which backs `/` was flagged as out of scope), src/app/conferences/[slug]/papers/[paperId]/page.tsx, RequestsPanel.tsx / DelegationPanel.tsx / PledgeInvoicingCard.tsx / AidRequestModal.tsx / DelegationCreditsCard.tsx / StudyGuideCard.tsx / DelegationPlacard.tsx / ChairParticipant.tsx beyond skims, and CustomQuestionsField / QuestionBuilder / ConferenceRosterPicker / PositionPaperRoster / PositionPaperButtons / ShareAchievementModal / LogoCropModal (all of which have their primary call sites on the organiser side owned by Christian, or in /account). There are almost certainly more findings in those files at the same density.

AGENTS.md UI RULE SWEEP RESULTS — (1) Native date inputs: CLEAN. `grep -rn 'type="date"'` across src/app/conferences, src/app/cv and src/components returns only the comment inside DatePicker.tsx itself; the creation wizard correctly uses the shared DatePicker. No `datetime-local`/`month`/`time` either. (2) Portal rule: two violations found in my scope — the explore RegionControl listbox and the apply-flow society typeahead. `CreditInfoTip` in the apply flow and `FilterPopover` (used by RequestsPanel) both do it correctly. (3) Hover-not-click hints: CLEAN in my scope — `CreditInfoTip` is the only "i" affordance and it is hover+focus with a 200ms close delay, portaled. `MinAgeChip` uses a native `title`, which the rule permits. (4) Acronym rule: violated across the conferences surface — `committeeDisplayName` from src/lib/presetNames.ts has ZERO call sites under src/app/conferences/. Only `CommitteesSessionsCard` happens to implement the pattern by hand. (5) `transition: all` / `transition-all`: four instances, all reported.

INCIDENTAL NON-UI OBSERVATIONS (not findings, flagged per the brief):
• src/app/conferences/new/page.tsx:287-291 hardcodes the Supabase project URL and publishable key inline in `getAuthedClient()` instead of using the shared `getAuthedClient(session.access_token)` from src/lib/supabase-auth like every other file in the area.
• src/app/conferences/ConferenceCard.tsx:287 and :457, ConferenceDetailClient.tsx:1305 and ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:538 all call `conf.expected_delegates.toLocaleString()` unguarded, but `expected_delegates` is nullable in the DB (the creation wizard writes `null` when the step is skipped, new/page.tsx:467). That is a crash, not a style issue.
• The explore page's geo fallback (ConferencesExploreClient.tsx:727) calls the third-party `https://ipapi.co/json/` from the client when `/api/geo` misses — a public page shipping the visitor's IP to an external service.
• `ConferenceDetailClient` computes `getRoleWindowStatus` off a `const now = new Date()` captured at render (:1054). A tab left open across a deadline never re-evaluates.
• src/app/conferences/ConferencesClient.tsx (585 lines) and src/components/ConferenceFocusCards.tsx are both unreachable dead code (reported as a consistency finding, but worth a separate cleanup pass).
• src/app/conferences/[slug]/reviews/page.tsx generates the identical metadata title "Conference Reviews" for every conference; the file's own comment acknowledges the name is not resolved. Same for the apply route.

BIGGEST WINS IF PETER ONLY DOES A FEW — (a) surface `applications_close_at` on the conference page and both explore cards; (b) add ▲/▼ buttons to the preference RankedRow so the apply flow works on a phone at all; (c) show step name + "3 of 6" in WizardShell using the already-computed `stepLabels`; (d) persist the creation-wizard draft to localStorage; (e) make the explore grid card a real `<Link>`; (f) fix or delete the dead "APPLICATIONS OPEN" filter; (g) fix the LockedCard's "below" copy and give it a PAY NOW link.


---

# Chair session view  
_109 findings_


## Whole app (hits every chair surface)

- **`src/app/layout.tsx:102`** — The entire design system's typography is dead. `globals.css:51` sets `body { font-family: 'Outfit', system-ui, sans-serif }` and 26 places in the chair tree set `fontFamily: "'Outfit', sans-serif"`, but layout.tsx only loads Playfair Display and Noto Sans Arabic from Google Fonts. There is no `next/font`, no `@font-face`, no self-hosted file (`public/fonts` does not exist). Outfit therefore never resolves and every screen falls through to `system-ui`. The same is true for `'Poppins'` (4 usages) and `'DM Mono', monospace` (5 usages, plus 50 `font-mono` classes) — all silently rendering as system-ui / the OS monospace. → _Load Outfit (400/500/600/700/800/900) the way Playfair is loaded, or better via `next/font/google` with `display: 'swap'`. Then either load DM Mono too or replace every `'DM Mono', monospace` and `font-mono` with a single decided mono face. Delete the three `'Poppins'` references — they were never going to work and Outfit is the house face._  `[consistency]`

## GSL main column — timer

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3220`** — Below quorum the Start button is a dead control. `handleToggleTimer` opens with `if (belowQuorum) return;` (:2250) but the button is only `disabled` for `gslRequireNextSpeaker && isLastGSLSpeaker` (:3222). A chair below quorum clicks the big green Start, gets no timer, no toast, no disabled styling, and no explanation. The only quorum copy on screen is in the bottom bar and it talks about *adding speakers*, not starting the clock. → _Add `|| belowQuorum` to the `disabled` prop, give the disabled state the same muted `#DDD4C0` / `#9A8A78` treatment already used for the last-speaker case, and put the reason in the button's `title` plus a single inline line under the timer: "Below quorum — {n} of {m} present, {needed} required to start."_  `[missing-state]`

## GSL main column — action row

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3246`** — The +TIME button renders its label at `fontSize: '8px'`, built by splitting the translation `'ADD\nTIME'` on `\n` into two stacked 8px lines inside a 52px-wide box. 8px is below any legibility floor, and the two-line hack means every locale must contain a literal newline in the right place (`AGREGAR\nTIEMPO`, `AJOUTER\nTEMPS`, `إضافة\nوقت`). Same button appears in the moderated caucus row (:1075). → _Make it a single-line 11–12px label ("+TIME"), or an icon+number chip. Drop the `\n` from the four translations and stop splitting on it._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3215`** — The five primary in-session controls (Restart ↺ / Start / Next / +TIME / Right to Reply) are packed into `w-full max-w-sm flex-wrap` — a 384px box, minus `px-4`, ≈352px of content. Restart (~44px) + gaps (32px) + +TIME (52px) + RTR (~90px) leaves ~134px for Start and Next combined, both of which carry `px-6`/`px-4` padding. They wrap onto two or three rows. Meanwhile the surrounding `<main>` is 700–900 design px wide, so ~400px of the row's own column sits empty on each side. → _Drop `max-w-sm` and let the row use the column width (`max-w-2xl`, matching the progress bar above it). Give Start visual dominance (it is the one control the chair reaches for under pressure) and demote +TIME/RTR/Restart into a single secondary cluster._  `[density]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3216`** — Five buttons in one row, five different heights and type scales: Restart is `px-3 py-3 text-sm` (~44px), Start is `py-3 px-6 text-base` (~46px), Next is `py-3 px-4` at an inline `fontSize:'14px'`, +TIME is `px-2 py-2` at 8px (~36px), RTR is `px-3 py-3 text-xs` (~40px). Radii are all `rounded-xl` but the fills span `#DDD4C0`, `#2A5A3C`, `#EDE7D8` and `#B8844A/15` — four different surface treatments for controls of the same rank. → _Pick one control height (44px) and two fills — solid forest for the primary action, ivory-with-border for everything secondary. Normalise all five to the same font size._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3238`** — `Next` uses `disabled:opacity-40` on a `bg-[#DDD4C0]` button sitting on the `#EDE7D8` page. 40% of #DDD4C0 over #EDE7D8 is very nearly the page colour — the button reads as a rendering glitch rather than an unavailable control, and nothing says why (the GSL is empty). → _Give disabled a real state: keep the fill at full opacity, drop the text to `NEU.muted #9A8A78`, add `cursor: not-allowed`, and set `title` to "No one else on the speakers list"._  `[missing-state]`

## GSL main column — no-speaker state

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3276`** — "Call first speaker" is hard-disabled at `committee.speakersList.length < 2` regardless of the `gslRequireNextSpeaker` setting, while the Start button honours the setting. A chair who turned the setting OFF still cannot call their only speaker, and the warning shown (`gsl_one_delegate_warning`) appears only when the list has exactly 1 — so at length 0 the button is disabled with no message at all. → _Gate it on `gslRequireNextSpeaker ? speakersList.length < 2 : speakersList.length < 1`, and show a one-line reason under the button in both the 0 and 1 cases._  `[copy]`

## GSL main column — header label

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3160`** — The main column is labelled with a bare three-letter acronym "GSL" at `text-lg font-black tracking-widest` in `'Poppins'` (a font that is never loaded). A first-time chair has no expansion anywhere on screen. By contrast the caucus views print the full motion name plus the topic in their left rail. → _Render "GSL" with "General Speakers List" beneath it at 10px muted — the same acronym-plus-expansion pattern the house UI RULE already mandates for committee names. Switch the font to Outfit._  `[copy]`

## GSL main column — timer colour

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3203`** — There are four different low-time colour ramps in one screen. GSL speaker timer: `<=10 → #B8844A`, else `#1C1410`. GSL progress bar: `>20% → #B6871F`, else `#B8844A` (never red). Moderated caucus total: `<=30 → text-red-500`. RTR and CoW timers: `<=5 → red-500, <=10 → #B6871F, else #B8844A`. So the GSL bar never signals urgency at all while two other clocks jump to Tailwind's `red-500 #EF4444`, which is not in the palette (`#8B2020` is). → _Define one urgency ramp in tokens (e.g. normal `NEU.ink` → warning `NEU.deepGold #B6871F` → critical `#8B2020`) at fixed fractions of the set duration, and apply it to all four clocks and all four progress bars._  `[consistency]`

## Moderated caucus — speaker progress bar

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:1048`** — Dead ternary branch: `caucusProgress > 50 ? 'bg-[#B6871F]' : caucusProgress > 20 ? 'bg-[#B6871F]' : 'bg-red-500'` — the first two branches are the same colour, so the bar is really 2-state with a redundant threshold. Reads as an unfinished intention. → _Collapse to the shared urgency ramp above; if three states are wanted, make the >50 branch a distinct colour._  `[polish]`

## Moderated + unmoderated caucus — extend popover

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:838`** — `{m % 1 === 0 ? `${m}m` : `${m}m`}` — identical in both branches, twice (also at :1152). The intent was clearly to render half-minute suggestions differently (the generator produces values like 7.5 from `caucus.totalTime / 120`), so a 7.5-minute suggestion renders as "7.5m" with no formatting and sits in a row of "5m" and "10m". → _Either delete the ternary or format the fractional case properly ("7m 30s"), and round the derived half-duration suggestion to a whole minute so the row reads as three clean presets._  `[polish]`

## Whole chair session

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2620`** — The chair session does not use the neumorphic design system at all. `neu.tsx` exports `NEU`, `NEU.out/outSm/in/inSm`, `NEU_GRADIENTS`, `NeuCard`, `NeuButton`, `NeuPill`, `NeuProgress` — the chair page imports none of them. Every surface is a flat `border border-[#DDD4C0]` rectangle with hardcoded hexes, while the applications benchmark and the new chat are extruded/pressed NEU surfaces. Two visual languages in one product. → _At minimum import `NEU` and replace the hardcoded `#EDE7D8 / #F0EBDD / #1C1410 / #1B3828 / #EED98A / #B6871F / #B8844A / #3D7A52 / #9A8A78` literals with tokens, so a palette change is one edit. Then migrate the repeated card/button/pill/progress shapes onto `NeuCard` / `NeuButton` / `NeuPill` / `NeuProgress`._  `[rule-violation]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2628`** — Radius chaos. Across the chair tree: 105 `rounded-xl`, 66 `rounded-full`, 62 `rounded-lg`, 20 `rounded-2xl`, 12 `rounded-sm`, 9 `rounded-3xl`, 6 `rounded-md`, plus ~15 distinct inline pixel radii (`borderRadius: 22, 20, 18, 16, 14, 13, 12, 10, 8, 5, 4, 3, 2, 1, 0`). The pill shape alone is spelled both `999` (32 places) and `9999` (6 places). → _Adopt three radii: 999 for pills, 16 for cards/inputs, 8 for chips/badges. `globals.css` already defines a `--radius-*` scale that nothing in this tree uses — either use it or delete it._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:729`** — Tailwind's default palette leaks into the forest/ivory system in ~25 places: `text-red-500` ×4 and `bg-red-500` ×3 in the chair page, `text-red-500` ×5 / `text-red-400` ×3 / `bg-red-950/40` / `border-red-800` in DocumentsModal, `border-red-700` ×2 and `border-orange-800` ×2 in MotionsModal, and `bg-amber-900/20 border-amber-700/40 text-amber-300` in the caucus sidebar notice. The house red is `#8B2020` and the house ambers are `#B8844A` / `#B6871F`. → _Replace every Tailwind-palette colour with the house equivalent (`#8B2020` for danger, `#B8844A`/`#B6871F` for warning). Add `red-*`/`amber-*`/`orange-*` to a lint rule for this directory if possible._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3223`** — `focus:outline-none` appears 34 times in the chair page alone (and throughout every panel) with zero `focus-visible:` replacements anywhere in `src/components` outside the shadcn `ui/` folder. Every button, input and typeahead in the chair console is unfocusable-looking for keyboard users, on a screen a chair may well drive with Tab/Enter while looking at the room rather than the laptop. → _Replace every `focus:outline-none` with `focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#B6871F] focus-visible:ring-offset-2` (or a `box-shadow` equivalent for the inline-styled buttons). This is a single find-and-replace plus one shared ring token._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2680`** — Almost nothing in the chair console has press feedback. `NeuButton` in the design system implements `scale(0.96)` on pointer-down and the codebase uses `active:scale-90` / `active:scale-[0.98]` in a handful of places (RollCallPanel's Begin Session and observer toggle, CowDelegationBoard tiles, VotingRulesPanel), but the header tabs, Start/Pause/Next, End Caucus, Approve/Deny, and every modal button have no `:active` state at all. Under pressure the chair cannot tell whether a click registered. → _Add `active:scale-[0.97]` (or the NeuButton pressed transform) to every button in the session tree — it is the single cheapest tactile improvement available here._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2178`** — `present` (:2178) and `presentCount` (:2185) are byte-identical expressions computed seven lines apart, and only `presentCount` feeds the quorum maths while `present` is unused after its declaration. Small, but it is the kind of drift that produces two disagreeing counts on screen later. → _Delete `present` and use `presentCount` (and pass it into the sidebar so the panel's own `present` — which counts observers — stops disagreeing with the quorum maths)._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2135`** — There is one loading state for everything: `GavelLoader`, a full-screen animated gavel with "LOADING…" in `font-mono tracking-widest`. There are no skeletons anywhere — the sidebar roster, the queue, the scoreboard, the documents list and the chat all pop in fully formed after a blank/blocked screen. On a slow conference wifi the chair stares at a spinning gavel with no sense of progress. → _Keep GavelLoader for the initial route load, but give the sidebar roster and the three modals skeleton rows so partial data appears as it arrives._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2167`** — The not-found state is a bare centred `t('session_not_found')` with a "Create committee" link — it never shows the code that failed, so a chair who mistyped one character cannot see what they typed, and the only offered action (create a new committee) is almost never what they want. → _Show the attempted code prominently, offer "Try another code" (back to /join) as the primary action, and demote Create to a secondary link._  `[copy]`

## Moderated caucus — add-speaker bar

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:474`** — The "Queue full: N speakers fit in remaining time" message is `text-sm text-amber-400` (#FBBF24) on `bg-[#FAF8F3]`. That is a 1.6:1 contrast ratio — effectively invisible on the ivory panel. It is also the only feedback the chair gets that the caucus queue is full, and it replaces the input entirely (`pointer-events-none`), so the chair sees an input that vanished and a message they cannot read. → _Use `#8A5A2E` or `#B6871F` on `#FAF8F3` (≥4.5:1) at `text-sm font-semibold`, and keep the input visible-but-disabled rather than swapping it out, so the chair understands what happened._  `[contrast]`

## Sidebar — caucus max-speakers notice

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2966`** — The "Maximum speakers reached" strip is the only element in the whole session that uses Tailwind's amber scale (`bg-amber-900/20 border-amber-700/40 text-amber-300`), and it is hardcoded English inside an otherwise fully translated screen. It also fires for 6 seconds and then vanishes, so a chair who looks away misses it entirely. → _Restyle with `rgba(238,217,138,0.14)` fill / `rgba(238,217,138,0.35)` border / `#EED98A` text to match the forest sidebar, add a translation key, and keep the notice sticky while the queue is actually full rather than on a 6s timer._  `[consistency]`

## Header

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2628`** — The four header tabs are `text-[18px] font-bold` (900 when active) inside a 44px-tall bar (`h-11`), which is the largest interface type on the screen after the timer — larger than the current speaker's supporting text. The controls they compete with (session code 12px, scoreboard icon 22px, settings icon 22px) are an order of magnitude quieter, so the eye lands on "ROLL CALL / MOTIONS / DOCUMENTS / CHAT" before the timer. → _Bring the tabs to 14px/700 (active 800) and give the header 48px so the type has room. The timer and the speaker's flag should be the two loudest things on the screen._  `[hierarchy]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2630`** — All four tab hovers are implemented by mutating `e.currentTarget.style.color/backgroundColor/transform` in `onMouseEnter`/`onMouseLeave`, on a component that re-renders every second while the timer runs. Any re-render mid-hover resets the inline style, so the hover state flickers off. The buttons also carry `transition-all duration-200` (11 `transition-all` instances in this file, 8 in RollCallPanel), which animates layout properties too. → _Move hover to CSS (`hover:` classes or a `:hover` rule) and replace `transition-all` with the explicit list — `transition: color 200ms, background-color 200ms, transform 200ms` — matching the `EASE` curve exported from neu.tsx._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2695`** — Two icon-only header buttons sit side by side; the scoreboard one has `title="Scoreboard"` (:2685), the settings gear has no `title`, no `aria-label` and no visible label. Hovering the gear tells the chair nothing. Neither has a focus ring. → _Give both `title` + `aria-label`, or better, a 9px uppercase caption under each icon — this is a room-management console, not a consumer app, and Settings hides the chair code and the whole voting rule set._  `[consistency]`

## Header — session code

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2680`** — On copy the button label swaps from the 6-character code to a single `✓`, so the chip collapses from ~70px to ~22px and the entire header row reflows sideways for 2 seconds. The same pattern in `SettingsPanel.CodeCopyButton` keeps the width (it shows "✓ Copied"). → _Render `✓ {code}` or keep the code and add a check glyph beside it, so the width never changes. Or set a `min-width` on the chip._  `[polish]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2680`** — The session code — the thing 30 delegates need in the first minute — is a `text-xs font-mono` chip roughly 22px tall, quieter than every tab beside it. To make it readable across a room the chair has to open Settings → Access, where the very same code is rendered as a full-width forest button at `text-xl tracking-widest`. → _Either promote the header chip (14px, `tracking-widest`, forest fill) or add a one-click "project the code" fullscreen state. Two very different treatments of the same value in two places is the underlying inconsistency._  `[hierarchy]`

## Header — unread badges

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2643`** — Motions/Documents/Chat badges are `w-4 h-4` (16px) `bg-[#1B3828]` circles with 10px white text, absolutely positioned `top-1 right-1` — they sit on top of the tab's own gold underline indicator and clip against the 44px bar. The chat panel's own unread badge (`ChatConversationList.tsx:126`) is a gold-gradient pill with forest text and `minWidth:18`. Two unread visual languages, both visible in the same session. → _Use one badge component. The gold-gradient pill from the chat list reads better on both surfaces; move it to the header too, sized 18px, and give the tab enough right padding that it never overlaps the underline._  `[consistency]`

## Sidebar — masthead stats

- **`src/components/RollCallPanel.tsx:553`** — The three `MajorityPie` donuts carry no labels at all — just three bare numbers (present count, ceil(2/3), floor(1/2)+1) in three colours (`#2A5A3C`, `#B6871F`, `#8A7A6A`). A chair cannot tell that the middle one is a two-thirds threshold. Worse, the arcs are decorative: `arcFill` is hardcoded 1 / 2⁄3 / 0.5, so the rings never move and encode nothing. → _Add a 8–9px uppercase caption under each number ("PRESENT", "2/3", "MAJORITY"), or replace the three static donuts with one line of text: "12 present · 8 for ⅔ · 7 for majority". The 96px of SVG currently buys nothing._  `[hierarchy]`
- **`src/components/RollCallPanel.tsx:403`** — The present count is shown with no denominator. `total` is computed on the very next line (`:404`) but is used only in the Begin Session label (`:711`). During a live session the chair sees "12" and has to count the roster to know whether that is 12 of 14 or 12 of 40. There is also no absent count anywhere. → _Render "12 / 20" with the total in `NEU.muted`. Note the two totals in play disagree — RollCallPanel counts observers, the quorum maths at `chair/page.tsx:2186` excludes them — so decide one definition and use it in both places._  `[missing-state]`

## Sidebar — delegate list

- **`src/components/RollCallPanel.tsx:702`** — One field does two very different jobs: `AddCountryInput` is both the roster filter (`onQueryChange={setSearch}`) and the add-a-delegate box, and Enter always commits an *add*. A chair mid-session who types "ger" to find Germany, then presses Enter out of habit, adds a delegation. The dropdown even offers "Add custom" for free text. → _Split them. A read-only filter field at the top of the list (with a clear ✕), and a separate "+ Add delegation" affordance in the footer that opens the country picker. If they must stay merged, require an explicit click on a dropdown row to add and make Enter jump to the first match instead._  `[wasted-effort]`
- **`src/components/RollCallPanel.tsx:622`** — Searching does not filter — non-matching rows stay in the list at `opacity-20` (`:623`). In a 190-seat GA the chair scrolls through 190 near-invisible rows to reach one match; `scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'})` jumps to the first hit but everything around it is ghosted. → _Actually filter the list while a query is active, and show a "showing 3 of 190" line plus a clear button. Keep the grey-out only for a live *status* filter, where seeing the excluded rows is the point._  `[density]`

## Sidebar — delegate row

- **`src/components/RollCallPanel.tsx:684`** — The observer (Megaphone) toggle is a 15px lucide icon in a `p-1` wrapper — about a 23×23px hit area, well under the 44px minimum, and it sits immediately left of the 90×30 status slider. Its only affordance is colour (`rgba(238,217,138,0.9)` when on vs `rgba(237,231,216,0.4)` when off) — no background, no border, no pressed state beyond `active:scale-90`. → _Grow the hit area to 32×32 minimum with `p-2`, and give the "on" state a real chip (gold tint fill + hairline) so observer status is legible without hovering for the tooltip._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/RollCallPanel.tsx:688`** — The "ABSENT" tag is 10px `font-mono` at `rgba(237,231,216,0.35)` on the `#1B3828` forest sidebar — a computed contrast ratio of about 2.6:1, well below the 4.5:1 AA floor for text that small. On the same row, absent delegates' names are already dimmed to `opacity-35`, so the label the chair most needs to scan is the least readable thing in the panel. → _Raise to `rgba(237,231,216,0.7)` (≈5.6:1) or give absent rows a muted-red left border instead of relying on a barely-visible label._  `[contrast]`
- **`src/components/RollCallPanel.tsx:638`** — Row backgrounds carry four states (`rgba(238,217,138,0.12)` current, `rgba(61,122,82,0.22)` present, `rgba(182,135,31,0.18)` present-voting, transparent absent) plus a fifth for non-matching search (transparent + `opacity-20`), and each has a separate imperative `onMouseEnter`/`onMouseLeave` hover colour written inline (`:650`). Border width also changes between states (`border-2` for the current speaker vs `border` for everyone else), so rows shift by 1px when the speaker changes. → _Use a constant 1px border and express "current" with a 3px gold left bar or an inset shadow, so nothing reflows. Move the hover colours into CSS custom properties per state instead of five inline handlers._  `[consistency]`

## Sidebar — committee badge

- **`src/components/CommitteeIdentityBadge.tsx:152`** — The topic line is 10.5px at `rgba(238,217,138,0.55)` on `#1B3828` — ≈3.9:1, failing AA for small text. The full-name secondary line (`:140`) is 10.5px at `rgba(237,231,216,0.52)` — ≈4.0:1, also failing. Both are also `line-clamp-2` / `truncate`, so a long topic is cut with no way to read the rest except a native `title`. → _Take both to at least 0.72 alpha (≈5.5:1) and 11px. For the topic, allow three lines before clamping — the topic is the one string a chair reads aloud._  `[contrast]`

## Sidebar

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2935`** — The sidebar is a hard `w-[22rem]` (352px). Inside FitToScreen the design canvas is always `aspect-ratio × 820` px wide — 1458 on a 16:9 display but only ~1230 on a 3:2 Surface and ~1093 on a 4:3 projector. On those the sidebar eats 29–32% of the canvas and the main column drops to ~740px, which is exactly where the `max-w-sm` action row and the absolutely-positioned caucus rail start colliding. → _Make the sidebar `clamp(280px, 24%, 352px)` and let the main column keep its share, or add a collapse control (see next finding)._  `[responsive]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:1298`** — `showRollCall` can only be set false by opening chat (`:2612`); there is no control anywhere that lets a chair collapse the sidebar. On a shared projector the chair cannot give the timer and the current speaker the full width, even during a moderated caucus where the sidebar duplicates the queue already shown at the top of the main column. → _Add a chevron on the sidebar's outer edge that collapses it to a 44px rail (flags only), persisted per committee in localStorage._  `[wasted-effort]`
- **`src/components/RollCallPanel.tsx:716`** — `FullListPopup` (a 60-line modal listing every queued speaker with remove buttons) is unreachable — `setShowFullList` is never called anywhere. Meanwhile the main column's queue strip caps at 7 flags and shows "+N more in queue" (`chair/page.tsx:376`) with no way to see the rest. The feature that solves the problem exists and is not wired up. → _Wire the "+N more in queue" text to `setShowFullList(true)` (it is already a natural affordance), or delete the dead component. Right now the chair has no route to speakers 8..n except switching the sidebar to Queue view._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Sidebar — A-Z / Queue toggle

- **`src/components/RollCallPanel.tsx:83`** — The view toggle is a 104×28px track with two 10px labels and a `rgba(255,255,255,0.22)` thumb — the selected half is barely distinguishable from the unselected half on the forest ground, and the whole control reads as decorative. It also has no `aria-pressed`/`role`, only a `title`. → _Give the active half a solid `#EDE7D8` fill with `#1B3828` text (the same segmented pattern `VotingRulesPanel.Seg` already uses), bump the labels to 11px, and add `role="radiogroup"`/`aria-checked`._  `[polish]`

## Sidebar — queue position bubble

- **`src/components/RollCallPanel.tsx:665`** — The current speaker's position bubble renders `★` while everyone else renders a number. A star does not communicate "has the floor" — AGENTS.md itself documents a 🎙 badge for this. There is also no colour difference: both the star and the numbers use `#EDE7D8` fill with `#1B3828` text. → _Use a 🎙 glyph (or a filled gold bubble) for the current speaker and keep numbers for the queue, so position 1 is unmistakably "speaking now" rather than "first in line"._  `[copy]`

## Pre-session roll call

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2916`** — Roll call renders as a `max-w-md` (448px) forest card with `maxHeight: 680px`, centred in a canvas 1100–1460px wide. For a 40-seat committee, let alone a 190-seat GA, the chair scrolls a 448px column with ~500px of empty ivory on each side — and this is the one screen where they are working through every single delegate at speed. → _Widen the card and switch to a 2- or 3-column grid of delegate rows above ~900px, or use the full-width sidebar+main layout the session itself uses. Keep the bulk buttons pinned._  `[density]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2917`** — The card's grain overlay is `absolute inset-0 z-[1]`, and the RollCallPanel that follows it is a static (unpositioned) sibling. Only the panel's header (`RollCallPanel:538`) and footer (`:701`) opt out with `relative z-10` — the delegate list itself renders *under* the grain layer. So the middle of the card is textured and the top and bottom are not. → _Move the grain to `z-0` behind everything, or add `relative z-10` to the list container so the whole card shares one texture._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/RollCallPanel.tsx:559`** — Clear All / All Present / All P+V only render when `showBulkActions` is true, which the chair page passes only in pre-session (`chair/page.tsx:2925`). After a caucus or a break, marking 30 delegates back to present mid-session is 30 individual slider taps. → _Show the bulk row whenever the status sliders are open (`showStatusSliders`), not only during pre-session. The GSL-removal guard is already keyed on phase, so nothing else has to change._  `[wasted-effort]`
- **`src/components/RollCallPanel.tsx:561`** — The three bulk buttons are 10px uppercase text in `px-2.5 py-1.5` chips — roughly 26px tall — and they are the highest-leverage controls on the roll-call screen. The Begin Session button below them is a full-width `py-3` block. The ranking is inverted relative to how often each is used. → _Bring the bulk chips to 32px with 11px labels and equal widths, and colour-code them to the status they set (the current red/green/gold tints are right, they are just too small to hit and read)._  `[hierarchy]`

## Moderated + unmoderated caucus — left rail

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:1010`** — The caucus motion name / purpose / "N delegates spoke" block is `absolute left-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 max-w-[160px]` (200px in the unmoderated view, :719) inside a column whose content — a 165×110 flag, a 5rem timer, and a `max-w-2xl` (672px) progress bar — is centred on the *whole* column. On a ~740px main column the 672px bar plus the 200px rail overlap by ~130px. The rail is `pointer-events-none`, so the overlap is silent. → _Put the rail in flow as a left column of a two-column grid (rail + centred stack) rather than absolutely positioned, so the centre content is centred in the remaining space._  `[responsive]`

## Unmoderated caucus

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:729`** — Four timer type sizes in one product: unmoderated countdown `text-9xl` (128px), GSL and moderated-caucus speaker clocks `fontSize: '5rem'` (80px), caucus total clock `text-lg` (18px), RTR / CoW overlays `text-5xl` (48px). The 128px unmoderated clock is the single largest thing in the app and it is the least urgent of the four (nobody is speaking). → _Standardise on two: 80px for the primary clock of whatever mode is running, 18–20px tabular for secondary clocks. The unmoderated timer should not out-shout the speaker timer._  `[consistency]`

## Unmoderated caucus — extend

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:824`** — The extend panel here is rendered *in flow* (`mt-4`), so opening it pushes the whole centred column upward and the 128px timer jumps. In the moderated caucus the equivalent panel is `absolute bottom-full right-0` (`:1139`) and does not move anything. Same control, two behaviours. → _Use the moderated version's absolute/portaled treatment in both, anchored to the Extend button._  `[consistency]`

## Unmoderated caucus — Consultation of the Whole

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:757`** — During a Consultation, the chair has five controls in one row — Start/Pause, Extend, CoW Timer, End Caucus — plus a separate floating CoW timer panel at `right: 2rem`, plus the delegation board. The CoW Timer button (`bg-[#B8844A]/15 border-[#B8844A]/30 text-[#B8844A]`) is the only amber-tinted control among four solid fills, which makes the least-important control the most visually distinct. → _Fold the CoW timer into the delegation board (tapping a flag already starts it), and drop the separate button. If it must stay, give it the same secondary ivory treatment as Extend._  `[hierarchy]`

## Consultation of the Whole — delegation board

- **`src/components/CowDelegationBoard.tsx:33`** — Flag tile captions render `{country}` — the raw stored name, not `getCountryDisplayName(country, language)` — even though the component imports that helper and uses it for the "Speaking:" line. So the board shows English names in a Spanish/French/Arabic session, and shows unabbreviated "United Kingdom of Great Britain…" truncated in a 64px cell. → _Wrap the caption in `getCountryDisplayName(...)` and apply the same `abbrevCountry` map the GSL queue uses (`chair/page.tsx:134`)._  `[copy]`
- **`src/components/CowDelegationBoard.tsx:62`** — The tile grid is `max-h-[280px] overflow-y-auto` with no scroll affordance and no count. In a 40+ delegation committee the chair sees three rows of flags and no indication that more exist below. There is also no empty state when every delegate is absent. → _Add a "N delegations · M have spoken" header line above the grid and a fade or scroll shadow at the bottom edge. Render "No delegations present" when the filtered list is empty._  `[missing-state]`

## Extra Time / Right of Reply / CoW timer overlays

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3382`** — Three separate floating panels are all hardcoded to `fixed; top: 50%; right: 2rem; transform: translateY(-50%)` — the Extra Time popover (:3382), the RTR popover (:3437) and the CoW timer (:767). They are not anchored to their triggers, so the chair clicks a button in the bottom action row and a panel appears in the middle of the right edge, roughly 400px away. The CoW timer and the RTR panel can be open simultaneously and land exactly on top of each other. → _Anchor each to its trigger via `getBoundingClientRect()` and render through `@/components/Portal` with edge flipping — the pattern the house UI RULE mandates and that `GavelChip`, `NewDmPicker` and `MotionsModal.ProposerInput` already implement in this same codebase._  `[consistency]`

## Right of Reply overlay

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3473`** — Granting a right of reply is four interactions: open the popover, type/select the country, pick a duration, press GRANT — and then the timer is still paused, so a fifth click on START is needed before the delegate can speak. The chair is doing this while a delegate stands waiting. → _Make GRANT start the clock (label it "Grant & start"), and pre-select the last speaker as the default target since a right of reply is almost always in response to whoever just spoke._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Add-speaker typeahead (GSL, caucus, RTR, roster)

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:204`** — Four typeahead dropdowns are plain `absolute bottom-full ... z-10` children, not portaled: `AddSpeakerInput` (:204), `RtrCountryInput` (:286), `CaucusAddSpeakerInput` (:488) and `RollCallPanel.AddCountryInput` (:155, z-30). The house UI RULE requires every floating layer to be rendered through `@/components/Portal` at fixed viewport coordinates with edge flipping. Three of the four open upward inside a bottom bar that is itself inside `overflow-hidden` flex columns, and the roster one sits inside the sidebar's `overflow-hidden` wrapper. → _Port all four onto the `MotionsModal.ProposerInput` implementation in this repo — it already does portal + `getBoundingClientRect` + capture-phase scroll reposition + flip. Delete the four bespoke absolute dropdowns._  `[rule-violation]`

## Add-speaker typeahead

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:217`** — Hardcoded English inside an otherwise translated screen: "already on list" (:217, :503), "currently speaking" (:503), "already spoke" (:517), "↑ First" / "↓ Last" (:523/:529), "Enter ↵" (:231, :532), "{n} speakers" (:398), "Last" (:431), "{n} delegate(s) spoke" (:1021), "Queue full: …" (:475), "Maximum speakers reached…" (:2967), "⚠️ Below quorum…" (:3315). A Spanish or Arabic chair gets a half-English console. → _Move all of these into `translations.ts`. The Arabic case is worse than cosmetic — "↵", "↑ First", "↓ Last" are directional glyphs in an RTL layout._  `[copy]`

## Bottom bar — speaker time

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3295`** — The custom speaker-time field is a bare `w-14` number input with no unit suffix, no placeholder and no label — the only clue is the `t('gsl_time')` word sitting four preset chips to its left. The presets beside it all read "45s / 60s / 75s / 90s", so a chair could reasonably type "1" meaning one minute and set a 1-second speaking time. → _Add an `s` suffix inside the field (the `NumberField` in SettingsPanel already does exactly this) and a `min={5}` clamp on commit._  `[copy]`

## Bottom bar — quorum warning

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3315`** — "⚠️ Below quorum: speakers cannot be added until {n} delegates are present" is inaccurate and incomplete. Speakers *can* still be added — clicking a delegate row in the sidebar calls `handleAddToSpeakersList` ungated (`:3017`); only the text input is gated. And the sentence never says how many are currently present, so the chair cannot tell how far off they are. → _Rewrite as "Below quorum — {present} of {total} present, {needed} required. The timer cannot start." and gate the sidebar row click the same way the input is gated so the two agree._  `[copy]`

## Motions modal — voting view

- **`src/components/MotionsModal.tsx:768`** — `const rest = order.slice(1, 5)` silently drops every queued motion beyond the fifth. In a busy committee six or seven placards go up at once; motions 6+ exist in the DB, count toward the header badge, and are completely invisible in the modal with no "+2 more" indication. → _Show a "+N more motions" row at the bottom of the right column that expands the list (the column already scrolls), or make the column scroll and drop the slice._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/components/MotionsModal.tsx:808`** — Every motion card is `draggable` with `cursor-grab` but has no drag handle, no grip glyph, and no drop indicator — the only hint is a forest info strip reading `t('motions_drag_hint')` at the top. Meanwhile the position badge that sits at the card's top-right corner is `pointer-events-none`, so the most badge-like thing on the card is not the handle. → _Add the six-dot grip the Settings motion-order rows already use (`SettingsPanel.tsx:307`) at the card's left edge, and render a 2px forest insertion line on `dragOver` the way `RollCallPanel:595` does._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/MotionsModal.tsx:968`** — Two purely decorative dotted-dot SVG dividers eat real space: a vertical one occupying a 20px column between the primary card and the queue at `opacity: 0.35`, and a horizontal one at 14px between every pair of queued cards. In a 4-card right column that is 42px of ornament in a modal fixed at `height: '88%'`. → _Replace both with a 1px `#DDD4C0` hairline (or nothing — the cards already have borders). The saved height goes to card content, which currently truncates._  `[density]`
- **`src/components/MotionsModal.tsx:937`** — The Reject button's hover is `hover:bg-red-950/40 hover:text-[#8B2020]` — Tailwind's red-950 (#450A0A) at 40% over `#DDD4C0` produces a muddy near-black wash behind dark red text, which is much closer to unreadable than to a warning. Every other destructive control in the session uses a solid `#8B2020`. → _Hover to `rgba(139,32,32,0.10)` fill with `#8B2020` text and a `rgba(139,32,32,0.4)` border — the same treatment the Waiting Room Deny button already uses (`chair/page.tsx:2802`)._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/components/MotionsModal.tsx:922`** — The required-votes chip on the primary card is computed by `requiredVotes()` (:103), which hardcodes "2/3 majority" for consultation/tour and "Simple majority" for everything else. It completely ignores `settings.substantiveThreshold`, which the chair can set to consensus or 2/3 in Settings → Voting. So a committee running consensus is shown "Simple majority · needs 8 of 15" — a label that actively lies. → _Either derive procedural-motion thresholds from settings (adding a `proceduralThreshold` if the two genuinely differ), or relabel the chip "Procedural motion — {fraction} of members present" so it is clearly a fixed rule of procedure and not the committee's configured threshold._  `[copy]`

## Motions modal — raise form

- **`src/components/MotionsModal.tsx:616`** — Form labels are `text-lg font-semibold` (18px) while the values they label are `text-sm` (14px) inputs, and the "Proposed by" label is coloured `#3D7A52` green while "Topic" is `#6A5A4A` brown — two label colours in the same form for no semantic reason. The heading above is `text-3xl font-black`, so the visual weight runs heading → labels → the actual data. → _Labels at 12–13px/700 in one colour (`NEU.muted` or `#6A5A4A`), inputs at 15–16px. Reserve the green for the proposer's resolved flag chip, not the label._  `[hierarchy]`
- **`src/components/MotionsModal.tsx:466`** — The Suspend Debate and End Debate buttons are a `flex-col` pair at half the height of the four motion-type tabs beside them, styled `bg-[#8B2020]/20 border-[#8B2020]/40 text-[#8B2020]`. They are the two most consequential actions in the modal and they read as an afterthought bolted onto the right edge of the type picker. → _Move them out of the type row into their own labelled group below the form ("Procedural") at full control height, so they are deliberate rather than adjacent-and-small._  `[consistency]`

## Motions modal — special vote screen

- **`src/components/MotionsModal.tsx:1362`** — "Does this motion pass?" is rendered with two `px-16 py-8` buttons (~128px tall each) reading YES / NO, and the only context is one line of 12px mono text: "END DEBATE · FRANCE". The chair is one click from permanently ending the committee (`endedAt` is irreversible per AGENTS.md) with no restatement of consequence and no confirmation step. → _For End Debate specifically, state the consequence under the question ("This closes the committee permanently. The session becomes read-only and is deleted in 1 hour.") and consider routing it through the existing `ConfirmModal` with `danger` + a type-to-confirm gate._  `[hierarchy]`

## Motions modal

- **`src/components/MotionsModal.tsx:1410`** — The modal is a fixed `height: '88%'` regardless of content. With no motions raised it opens on the raise form (a ~500px form) inside an ~720px shell, leaving a large empty void above the submit button; with the list view and one motion it is nearly all whitespace. Sibling modals disagree too: DocumentsModal is `max-w-xl max-h-[92%]`, ScoreboardPanel `max-w-3xl max-h-[92%]`, Motions `max-w-5xl` at a fixed 88%. → _Switch to `max-h-[92%]` and let height follow content, matching the other two. Standardise the three on one width scale as well — three different max-widths for three chair modals is arbitrary._  `[polish]`

## Documents modal — doc card

- **`src/components/DocumentsModal.tsx:720`** — Each document card carries an 88px-wide left strip containing a live `<iframe src={doc.fileUrl}>` at 120px height with `pointer-events: none` as a thumbnail. That is a full PDF render per card purely for decoration, and when there is no file it falls back to a 5.5rem (88px) 📋 emoji — a giant clipboard glyph dominating the row. → _Replace with a static generated thumbnail or a small file-type chip plus the doc code. If the live iframe stays, cap it to the first page and lazy-render only visible cards._  `[density]`
- **`src/components/DocumentsModal.tsx:818`** — When `requireDocApproval` is on, a card can stack three full-width buttons — Approve, Reject (side by side) and then Introduce below — plus a `📎 filename ▼` toggle and a `▼ Show content` toggle, all in one ~200px card. Five interactive elements, none visually ranked. → _Make Introduce the only full-width primary; put Approve/Reject as a compact segmented control in the title row beside the status badge; collapse the two content toggles into one "Preview" disclosure._  `[hierarchy]`

## Documents modal

- **`src/components/DocumentsModal.tsx:807`** — Hardcoded English amid translated copy: "▲ Hide content" / "▼ Show content" (:807), "Uploading…" (:606), "A document with this title already exists" (:614), `title="Delete"` (:761). Note the last one is a button that permanently deletes a submitted document with no confirmation at all. → _Translate all four, and route the delete through `ConfirmModal` (`danger: true`) — it exists in the repo and is already used on the conferences side._  `[copy]`

## Settings drawer

- **`src/components/SettingsPanel.tsx:1090`** — Roughly half the drawer is untranslated English while the other half goes through `t()`. Untranslated: "Committee display & permissions", "Sponsors label", "Lock delegate roll call", "Disable chat", "GSL", "Timer duration" + its note, "Submission limits" + its note, "Unlimited", "Maximum N per committee", the whole Points tab ("Score sources", "Quality factors", "Ranking blend", "Rating scale max", "Hide scores from delegates", "+ Add source", "+ Add factor", "Custom source", "New factor"), "Switch to custom veto members", `P5_FIXED_HINT` and `CUSTOM_MOTION_HINT`. The drawer's own language switcher is in its header, so a chair can switch to Spanish and watch half the panel stay English. → _Move every literal into `translations.ts`. Given the language toggle physically lives in this header, this panel is the worst possible place for partial translation._  `[copy]`
- **`src/components/SettingsPanel.tsx:871`** — Four tabs and roughly 30 settings with no search and no per-setting anchor. Mid-session, finding "can delegates change their own status" means opening the drawer, guessing between Access/Motions/Voting/Points, and scrolling. The default tab is Access, which contains the codes a chair needs at the *start*, not mid-session. → _Add a filter field above the tab strip that matches on setting label and note text, hiding non-matching rows. Cheap to add — every row already has a `label` string._  `[wasted-effort]`
- **`src/components/SettingsPanel.tsx:812`** — The app-wide language switcher lives inside the Settings drawer header, next to the close ✕, styled as a bare `Globe + EN` button on the forest bar. Nothing on the chair screen suggests language is changed there, and it is not a committee setting at all — it is a device preference sitting in a panel that writes to the DB. → _Move it to the header beside the scoreboard/settings icons, or give it its own labelled row in the Access tab so it does not read as part of the drawer's chrome._  `[consistency]`

## Settings → Access

- **`src/components/SettingsPanel.tsx:49`** — The chair code is `filter: blur(4px)` until hover, and the only click action is copy-to-clipboard. To read it aloud to a co-chair across the room the chair must keep the mouse hovering; on a projector-mirrored screen there is no way to hold it revealed. There is no reveal toggle and no keyboard path to unblur. → _Add a persistent eye toggle beside the field (click to reveal/hide) and keep copy on a separate button — the two actions are currently conflated onto one target._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Settings → Motions

- **`src/components/SettingsPanel.tsx:187`** — `RenameRow`'s only affordance is a pencil at `opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100`, and the "reset" chip is also hover-gated (`:197`). Nothing on the resting row indicates the name is editable, so the whole rename feature — a headline setting — is invisible until the pointer lands on it. Keyboard users never see either. → _Show the pencil at `opacity-40` at rest and 100% on hover/focus, and make the row `focus-visible` reveal both controls._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/SettingsPanel.tsx:291`** — The drag-to-reorder list sets `cursor: 'grab'` and dims the dragged row to `opacity: 0.5`, but renders no drop indicator — the chair drags a row and sees nothing showing where it will land until they release. `RollCallPanel:595` in the same product does render a 2px insertion line. → _Add the same 2px `#1B3828` insertion line on `dragEnter`, and set `cursor: grabbing` while dragging._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/SettingsPanel.tsx:324`** — The disruptiveness indicator is four 4×10px pips whose only explanation is a native `title="Disruptiveness: 3/4"`. "Disruptiveness" is MUN-scheduling jargon that also does not appear anywhere else in the chair UI, and the pips give no clue that dragging is what changes them. → _Add a one-line note under the section heading: "Drag to rank — the most disruptive motion is voted on first." and label the pip column with a 9px "RANK" caption._  `[copy]`

## Settings → Points

- **`src/components/SettingsPanel.tsx:1187`** — The Points tab uses three native `<input type="range">` sliders styled only with `accentColor: '#1B3828'` — the only native form controls in the entire chair experience, rendering in the OS's own shape and metrics next to hand-built neumorphic toggles. `SelectRow` (:585) is likewise the only native `<select>`. → _Either build (or reuse) a NEU-styled track+thumb — `NeuProgress` already has the inset-track / gradient-fill / white-ringed-thumb vocabulary — or accept native controls consistently everywhere. Currently it is one screen out of four._  `[consistency]`

## Settings — view-only banner

- **`src/components/SettingsPanel.tsx:875`** — The view-only notice is crimson (`rgba(139,32,32,0.10)` fill, `#8B2020` text, eye icon) — the danger colour. The `GavelChip` redesign in the same session deliberately abandoned exactly that treatment: its header comment states the old crimson pill "was coloured like an error, which made switching feel like something had gone wrong". The two surfaces now disagree about whether co-chairing is a problem. → _Restyle to the GavelChip's calm ivory/`NEU.outSm` treatment with a gavel glyph and the same copy, so "you are co-chairing" reads identically in both places._  `[consistency]`

## Scoreboard

- **`src/components/ScoreboardPanel.tsx:158`** — The entire panel is untranslated: "Scoreboard", "Export CSV", "Ranking", "Matrix", "← Back", "Manual adjustment", "+ Award", "− Deduct", "pts", "Reason (required)", "Apply", "No delegates yet", "No scored activity yet", and the ten CSV/table headers. It is reachable from the header of every session, in every locale. → _Route all of it through `t()`. The CSV header row can stay English (it is a data export) but should be a deliberate decision, not a side effect._  `[copy]`
- **`src/components/ScoreboardPanel.tsx:54`** — `getFeedbackForCommittee` is awaited in an effect with no loading state. On open, the ranking renders with `computeQualityScore` against an empty feedback array, so scores appear and then visibly re-sort a moment later when the fetch lands. There is also no error state if the fetch fails — scores just silently read as pure-objective. → _Render skeleton rows (or hold the panel on a spinner) until the feedback promise settles, and show a small "quality scores unavailable" note on failure so the numbers are not silently wrong._  `[missing-state]`

## Scoreboard — matrix tab

- **`src/components/ScoreboardPanel.tsx:254`** — Column headers are truncated to `'', 'GSL', 'Cauc', 'Time', 'Mot', 'RTR', 'WP', 'DR', '±', 'Total'` with no legend, no `title` attributes, and no units except a trailing `s` in the cells. "Cauc", "Mot" and "±" are unguessable, and "WP"/"DR" ignore the committee's renamed document labels that `docName()` exists to provide. → _Add `title` on each `<th>` with the full name, widen the headers where the table has room, and resolve WP/DR through `docName(committee, ...)` so a renamed committee is consistent._  `[copy]`

## Scoreboard — ranking tab

- **`src/components/ScoreboardPanel.tsx:183`** — Ranking rows are `backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF'` cards on a `#FAF8F3` panel — pure white, which neu.tsx explicitly rules out ("never white cards"). The same pure white appears on the ledger rows (:215), the manual-award inputs (:238), `SettingsPanel`'s NumberField (:647) and the whole `bg-white` set (7 in SettingsPanel, 3 in DocumentsModal, 2 in ScoreboardPanel, 1 in MotionsModal). → _Swap every `#FFFFFF` / `bg-white` for `NEU.surface #F0EBDD` (raised) or `NEU.base #EDE7D8` (inset). The ivory system's whole premise is that white never appears._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/components/ScoreboardPanel.tsx:188`** — Rows end in a bare `▸` character at `text-xs` in `#9A8A78` as the only drill-in affordance, and the row has no hover state at all (`transition-colors` is declared but no hover class or handler is attached). Nothing tells the chair the row is clickable. → _Add a hover background (`#EDE7D8`) and swap the text arrow for a lucide `ChevronRight` that nudges 2px on hover — the pattern `NeuChecklistRow` already implements in neu.tsx._  `[polish]`

## Scoreboard — manual adjustment

- **`src/components/ScoreboardPanel.tsx:232`** — Awarding or deducting points is buried three levels deep: open Scoreboard → Ranking tab → click a delegation → scroll past the entire ledger. It is the only write action in the panel and it sits below an arbitrarily long list. → _Pin the adjustment form to the bottom of the drill-in as a sticky bar, or surface a compact `± pts` control on each ranking row so the chair can award without drilling in._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Feedback dock (view-only co-chair)

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3332`** — `FeedbackLogPanel` — the per-speech notes-and-ratings dock that feeds the quality half of every delegate's score — renders only when `isViewOnly` is true. A single chair running a committee alone can never rate a speech, so `scoreBlend` above 0 silently produces nothing for them. AGENTS.md documents this as intentional, but from the chair's seat it reads as a missing feature with no explanation anywhere. → _If it must stay co-chair-only, say so where the chair looks for it: a line in Settings → Points ("Quality ratings are entered by co-chairs during the session"). If the restriction is only about screen space, offer it to a solo chair behind a toggle._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Feedback dock

- **`src/components/FeedbackLogPanel.tsx:304`** — Non-focused rows get `filter: blur(${blur}px)` up to 1.6px based on distance from focus, plus opacity down to 0.45. A blurred, half-transparent list of delegation names reads as a rendering fault or a stuck loading state, not as depth — and it makes the surrounding speech notes literally unreadable while the chair is trying to scan them. → _Drop the blur entirely. Distance recede via opacity (1 → 0.75 → 0.6) and a 2% scale is enough; blur on text is never the right tool._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/FeedbackLogPanel.tsx:254`** — The focused card's ring is `0 0 0 2px #B8844A` (amber). Everywhere else in the session, "this is the active/current thing" is `#EED98A` gold (current speaker row, gavel chip, motion badge) and `#B8844A` amber means "time is running out" (GSL timer at ≤10s, RTR warnings). Two meanings for one colour. → _Use `#EED98A` for focus/active and reserve `#B8844A` for time pressure only._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/components/FeedbackLogPanel.tsx:319`** — A fixed `GRID_COL = 280` px column is reserved on every row for the 2×2 factor sliders, even when `cfg.factors` is empty (the grid only renders when `factors.length > 0 && (isFocused || dist === 1)`, but the 280px spacer div always renders). A chair who disabled all quality factors loses 280px of every row to nothing. → _Render the spacer only when `factors.length > 0`, and let the pill take the full width otherwise._  `[density]`
- **`src/components/FeedbackLogPanel.tsx:237`** — Untranslated: "Comments appear here as delegates take the floor.", the textarea placeholder "Private note…", and the row tags "GSL" / "UNMOD" / "CAUCUS" (`tagFor`, :186). The tag also mixes a chair-renamed motion label with two hardcoded English fallbacks in the same column. → _Translate all three, and derive the fallback tags from the same motion-name settings the rest of the app uses so a renamed "Unmoderated Caucus" is not shown as "UNMOD"._  `[copy]`

## Chat (chair)

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2897`** — Opening chat renders `absolute inset-0 z-40` over the entire session area, so the chair loses sight of the running speaker timer, the queue and the current speaker — the three things they cannot afford to lose while a delegate is mid-speech. The old panel could at least be closed from inside; `ChatPanel` no longer renders its own close control (`onClose` is documented as "retained for call-site compatibility", :41), so the only exit is finding the Chat tab again in the header. → _Either dock chat as a right-hand column beside the session (the `showRollCall` mechanism already proves the layout can shed a column), or at minimum keep a compact speaker+timer strip pinned above the chat and give the panel its own ✕._  `[hierarchy]`

## Chat — new DM picker

- **`src/components/chat/NewDmPicker.tsx:201`** — The search field's placeholder is the literal string `"…"` and the empty-result state renders a single em dash `"—"` (:213). Both are untranslated non-words that tell the reader nothing. → _Placeholder: `t('chat_search_placeholder')` → "Search delegations and chairs". Empty: "No one matches \"{q}\""._  `[copy]`

## Chat — conversation list

- **`src/components/chat/ChatConversationList.tsx:44`** — The list is a hard `width: 280`. Inside FitToScreen's canvas (1093–1458 design px depending on aspect ratio) the chat overlay is list 280 + thread, which is fine — but `ChatPanel` also renders the list with `hidden sm:block` mobile logic (`ChatPanel.tsx:215`) that can never trigger, because `MobileGate` blocks every touch device from this page entirely. Two responsive systems, one of them dead. → _Drop the `sm:` breakpoint branches and the `showThread` mobile toggle from the chair path (or keep one implementation shared with the delegate view, which does need them) — right now `onBack` renders a `sm:hidden` back button that is permanently invisible._  `[responsive]`

## Chat — thread

- **`src/components/chat/ChatThread.tsx:61`** — `InfoHint` in the thread header renders its panel as a plain `absolute` span at `top: 22; insetInlineStart: -8; zIndex: 30` — not portaled. It sits inside the header of a `flex-1 flex-col min-w-0` column, and the messages area directly below is `overflow-y-auto`; near the right edge of the thread it will also run off the panel. Every other hover hint in this codebase (`SettingsPanel.HoverHint`, `MotionsModal.InfoHint`, `VotingRulesPanel.InfoDot`, `GavelChip`) is portaled with flipping. → _Port it onto the same Portal + `getBoundingClientRect` + flip pattern as its four siblings._  `[rule-violation]`

## Chat — message bubbles

- **`src/components/chat/ChatMessageGroup.tsx:98`** — A message in flight shows `opacity: 0.6` on the bubble plus the word "Sending…" in `NEU.amber` where the timestamp goes; a failed one gets a `1.5px solid #B4432F` border and a retry link. But there is no state for "sent and confirmed" beyond the timestamp reappearing, and `CHAT.danger #B4432F` is a fifth red in a product that already has `#8B2020` — the token file even notes "neu.tsx has no red". → _Reuse `#8B2020` for the failed state instead of minting `#B4432F`, and add a small ✓ beside the timestamp on confirm so the transition sending → sent is visible rather than inferred._  `[missing-state]`

## Tutorial overlay

- **`src/components/TutorialOverlay.tsx:734`** — `EndTutorialBtn` is `fixed top: 52, right: 16` and `GavelChip` is `fixed top: 3.75rem (60px), right: 0.85rem (13.6px)` — they overlap. Any chair in a 2-chair committee running the tutorial gets the gavel pill buried under the End Tutorial button. → _Offset the tutorial controls (e.g. `top: 100`) while the tutorial is open, or hide the GavelChip for the tutorial's duration._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/TutorialOverlay.tsx:679`** — The Gavin portrait is a hard `440 × 660` box at `bottom: 0; right: -20`. FitToScreen's design canvas is only 820px tall, so the otter occupies 80% of the screen height and its 440px width sits directly on top of the GSL bottom bar, the caucus add-speaker input and the right end of the action row — several of which are the exact targets the tutorial is pointing at (`speakers-bottom-bar` is step 3). → _Scale the portrait against the design box (e.g. `height: min(520, 62% of box.h)`) and flip it to the left side on steps whose spotlight target is in the bottom-right quadrant._  `[responsive]`
- **`src/components/TutorialOverlay.tsx:56`** — All tutorial copy is inlined in the component in four locales via a `pick()` helper, while the rest of the chair experience reads from `translations.ts`. That means a copy change here bypasses the whole translation workflow, and the memory note that `.claude/TRANSLATIONS.md` is the single source of truth is silently violated for 17 steps. → _Either move the strings into `translations.ts` under a `tutorial_*` prefix (they already partly are — `tutorial_end`, `tutorial_skip`, `tutorial_show_me` live there), or document this file as a deliberate exception in TRANSLATIONS.md. The current half-and-half is the worst of both._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/components/TutorialOverlay.tsx:762`** — On an action step the Next button reads "Skip", which sounds like skipping the tutorial rather than skipping this one step — the End Tutorial button 300px to the right is the actual quit. Two adjacent controls whose labels are easy to confuse under time pressure. → _Use "Skip step ›" on action steps and "Exit tutorial" for the other, so the scope of each is explicit._  `[copy]`

## Suspended / ended session views

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2856`** — The suspended screen leads with `text-6xl font-black` "SESSION SUSPENDED" and a `px-12 py-5 text-xl` Resume button, both centred in an otherwise empty ivory field. The ended screen (`SessionEndedContent`, :1198) leads with `text-5xl`. Neither shows the committee's current state — how many delegates are still connected, what the GSL looked like — so the chair has no context for the one decision on screen. → _Add a quiet summary line under the heading ("12 delegates present · 5 on the speakers list · suspended 4 min ago") so resuming is an informed action rather than a leap._  `[hierarchy]`

## Suspended session — take-over

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2867`** — When another chair holds the resume latch, this chair sees a permanently disabled `#DDD4C0` button plus "{name} is resuming…" — with no spinner, no elapsed indicator and nothing that suggests the take-over button will appear. It materialises silently after 12 seconds with no lead-in. → _Replace the static disabled button with a progress affordance ("Waiting for {name}… 8s") that counts toward the take-over, so the appearance of the take-over button is expected rather than magic._  `[missing-state]`

## Waiting Room banner

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2781`** — Join requests render as an unbounded `flex-wrap` row of chips inside a `shrink-0` banner. With ten simultaneous requests (normal at the start of a session) the banner grows to three or four rows and pushes the whole session down inside a fixed 820px canvas — the timer and action row lose that height with no scroll of their own. There is also no "approve all" for the common case. → _Cap the banner at one row with a "+N more" that opens a list, and add an "Admit all" button beside the count chip._  `[density]`

## GSL request banner

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2811`** — The Waiting Room banner (`#F3EEE2` ivory, forest chip) and the GSL-request banner (solid `#1B3828` forest, gold text) sit stacked directly on top of each other with opposite polarity, both full-width, both between the header and the session. Two banners with the same job — "someone needs your approval" — rendered as visual opposites. → _Unify them into one "Requests" strip with a segment per type, or give both the same ivory ground and distinguish by an icon and a small type label._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2826`** — The delegation name is rendered as the raw `m.proposedBy` string, not `getCountryDisplayName(m.proposedBy, language)` — even though the very same banner uses that helper in the Waiting Room block above (`:2796`) and `getCountryByName` for the flag two lines earlier. So the GSL request shows "Republic of Korea" while every other surface shows the localised/canonical name. → _Wrap it in `getCountryDisplayName(...)` for consistency with the Waiting Room and the queue._  `[copy]`

## Caucus loading screens

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2062`** — Entering a moderated caucus blocks the chair for a fixed `setTimeout(..., 3500)` and an unmoderated caucus for 3000ms, showing a stats card and a spinner while nothing is actually loading (the state is already applied optimistically). That is 3.5 seconds of a live committee where the chair cannot add the first speaker. → _Keep the card as a 900ms transition or make it dismissible on click/Escape. If it is meant as a projector "now entering caucus" slide, say so and give it a Start button rather than an indeterminate spinner._  `[wasted-effort]`
- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:3053`** — The Tour de Table card shows three stats (delegates / per-speaker / total), the moderated card shows three different ones (total / per-speaker / max speakers), and the unmoderated card shows two (total time / proposed by). Three variants of the same component with three different stat sets and no shared layout — and "proposed by" appears only in one of the three. → _One card, one stat row: Total time · Per speaker · Speakers · Proposed by, with cells omitted rather than reshuffled when they do not apply._  `[consistency]`

## Moderated caucus — bottom bar

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:1169`** — "End Caucus" is a solid `#8B2020` `px-8 py-3` block sitting in the total-timer row directly beside the extend control, and a second End Caucus appears inside `CaucusAddSpeakerInput` for Tour de Table (`:544`). It is the loudest control in the bar and the most destructive — one misclick discards the caucus queue. → _Demote to an outline treatment (`#8B2020` text on transparent with a `#8B2020`/40 border) and confirm on click when the caucus has more than 60 seconds remaining. Remove one of the two End buttons._  `[hierarchy]`

## GavelChip

- **`src/components/GavelChip.tsx:216`** — The chip is `fixed top: 3.75rem; right: 0.85rem` inside FitToScreen, so it floats over the main column at roughly y=60px — exactly where `DraggableSpeakersQueue` renders its 60×45 flag row (`chair/page.tsx:330`, `pt-2` under a 44px header). On a narrow canvas with 6–7 queued speakers the chip covers the last flag and its "Last" tag. → _Reserve the space: render the chip inside the header row (it already fits at the far right beside the settings gear) rather than floating over the content._  `[responsive]`
- **`src/components/GavelChip.tsx:249`** — The offline state reads "{gavel_chair_offline} **{gavel_take_over}**" as one continuous sentence in a pill, with the actionable half bolded but not separately clickable — clicking the chip opens the popover, where the take-over is a different button. The label implies a one-tap action that is actually two. → _Make the chip label state the fact only ("{name} looks offline") and let the popover carry the verb, or make the chip itself the take-over button in that state._  `[copy]`

## Auth gates (conference sessions)

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2141`** — The two blocking screens — "Sign in to join this session" (:2141) and "You don't chair this committee" (:2159) — are hardcoded English with no `t()`, and are styled unlike anything else in the app: bare `text-2xl font-black` headings on plain `bg-[#EDE7D8]` with a solid `#1B3828` button, no logo, no grain, no card. A chair locked out of their own committee gets the least finished screen in the product. → _Translate both, wrap them in the same card treatment as the not-found state, add `SessionsHeaderLogo`, and give the denied screen an actionable next step ("Ask your organiser to add you as a chair of this committee" plus the committee code so they can quote it)._  `[copy]`

## Sidebar — caucus mode

- **`src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx:2971`** — During a moderated caucus the caucus queue appears twice simultaneously — as flag tiles at the top of the main column (`DraggableSpeakersQueue`, :1000) and as a numbered list in the sidebar (`RollCallPanel` fed `caucusRollCallCommittee`). Both are drag-reorderable, both have remove buttons, and they use different visual vocabularies (60×45 flags with hover-✕ vs 36px circles with position bubbles). → _Pick one home for the queue. The sidebar list is better for reordering and removal (it scrolls and shows everyone); the main column strip is better for "who is next" at a glance — so reduce the main strip to a read-only next-3 preview and keep all editing in the sidebar._  `[hierarchy]`

> **Method / limits:** METHOD — findings come entirely from reading source on the current working tree (branch feature/conferences-auth), no dev server and no browser. I read in full: src/app/chair/[code]/page.tsx (3550 lines), RollCallPanel, MotionsModal, DocumentsModal, SettingsPanel, ScoreboardPanel, FeedbackLogPanel, VotingRulesPanel, ChatPanel + src/components/chat/** (all 7 files), TutorialOverlay, GavelChip, CommitteeIdentityBadge, CowDelegationBoard, ConfirmModal, Toast, neu.tsx, FitToScreen, MobileGate, plus AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md. I verified colour/radius/palette claims with greps and computed the WCAG contrast ratios by hand from the actual hex/alpha values in the source.

COVERAGE LIMITS — (a) I did not render anything, so overlap claims (GavelChip vs the speakers queue, the caucus left rail vs the progress bar, the tutorial otter vs the bottom bar, EndTutorialBtn vs GavelChip) are derived from the fixed pixel geometry plus FitToScreen's 820px design height and are stated as risks at particular aspect ratios rather than confirmed screenshots. (b) I did not audit the /voting page even though VotingRulesPanel lives partly in my list — that component is only rendered from src/app/voting/[code]/page.tsx, never from the chair page, so I limited myself to its own internals. (c) I did not exercise the delegate/advisor sides. (d) MobileGate blocks every touch UA from this route, so I deliberately did not raise phone-layout findings; the responsive items I did raise are about the FitToScreen canvas narrowing on 3:2 and 4:3 laptop/projector aspect ratios, which is a real desktop case.

DEAD CODE FOUND (not UI findings, but worth a cleanup pass):
- src/components/SpeakersListPanel.tsx (200 lines) and src/components/CaucusPanel.tsx (126 lines) are imported by nothing. The only greps that look like hits are the unrelated `caucusPanelLocked` state variable. Both are safe to delete.
- RollCallPanel's FullListPopup (:189-248) is unreachable — setShowFullList is never called. I raised the missing "+N more in queue" affordance as the UI finding; the component itself is either the fix or the deletion.
- RollCallPanel's `onCycleStatus` prop is marked @deprecated in its own JSDoc ("Never called") yet the chair page still passes handleCycleStatus in four places (:2920, :2998, :3007, :3020), and handleCycleStatus (:1935) is a live 20-line handler that nothing invokes. Deleting the prop removes a whole dead write path.

INCIDENTAL NON-UI / FUNCTIONAL OBSERVATIONS (deliberately kept out of findings per the brief):
1. Quorum is enforced inconsistently. `handleToggleTimer` returns early on belowQuorum (:2250) and the AddSpeakerInput onAdd is neutered (:3320), but the sidebar row click passes `handleAddToSpeakersList` ungated (:3017), the GSL-request approve button is ungated (:2828), and handleNextSpeaker is ungated. So below quorum a chair can still seat speakers three ways out of four. I raised the dead Start button and the misleading warning copy as UI findings; the enforcement gap itself is a logic issue.
