  • /conferences/[slug]/papers/[paperId] (512)
  • /conferences/[slug]/role/[role] (10, thin wrapper)
  • /delegation/[societyId] (DelegationPortalClient.tsx, 755) — an entire user role
  • /invites/chair/[token] (198), /invites/delegation/[token] (125), /invites/organizer/[token] (263)
  • /auth/signup (342), /auth/forgot (119), /auth/reset (142), /auth/error (106), /auth/onboarding (448), /auth/callback (route)
    — /auth/signin got exactly one mention (raw error.message)
  • /account/profile (1211), /account/unlimited (986), /account/calendar (301), /account/conferences (11), /account/cv (one mention)
  • /conferences/landing-lab + VariantRecord (507) + VariantFirstGavel (363) + LandingLabClient (47)
  • src/app/error.tsx (97), src/app/global-error.tsx (85)
  • All six loading.tsx: account, my-conferences, conferences/explore, conferences/[slug], .../apply, .../pay
  • /manage/[slug]/financials/settings and /manage/[slug]/financial-aid — skimmed, no findings

PURE REDIRECTS (correctly nothing to audit): /conferences → '/', /conferences/organise → '/my-conferences', /account → '/account/profile', /account/points → '/account/unlimited'
NOT UI: /api/contact, /api/emails/queue-participant, /api/geo, /api/indexnow

═══════════════════════════════════════════════
(b) UNCOVERED COMPONENTS
═══════════════════════════════════════════════
src/components — never mentioned by anyone:
  • ResolutionsPanel.tsx (143) — AND it is dead code, imported by nothing (see finding)
  • ChatAvatar.tsx — the only chat/ file with no findings
  • LogoCropModal.tsx, CustomQuestionsField.tsx, QuestionBuilder.tsx
  • PositionPaperRoster.tsx, PositionPaperButtons.tsx
  • AuthProvider.tsx (non-visual, correctly skipped)

src/components — read but produced zero findings (may genuinely be clean, unverified):
  DecorativeBleed, DraftNotice, DefaultEmailPreviewModal, PersonalConferenceCard, LogoDisc

src/components — one passing mention only, never audited:
  CVEntryModal (1374 lines, "only its one absolute layer and modal shell were checked"),
  ConferenceRosterPicker (cited as the reference pattern, never opened), ShareAchievementModal

src/components/ui — 12 of 14 never opened. The 6 that are actually imported (button, dialog, input, label, textarea, spinner) were counted but only Input was inspected. dialog.tsx matters most: it is the one semantically-correct dialog in a product with 21 hand-rolled overlays.

App-local components never opened (co-located, so easy to miss):
  authUi.tsx (768), accountUi.tsx (671), CVTimeline.tsx (433),
  participant/: RequestsPanel (721), DelegationPanel (542), PositionPaperCard (431), ChairParticipant (348), PledgeInvoicingCard (246), AidRequestModal (221), DelegationCreditsCard (174), DelegationPlacard (101), StudyGuideCard (97), DelegateParticipant/AdvisorParticipant/ObserverParticipant/ApplyPointer,
  manage/: DelegationsView (1177), delegationShared (740), LiveModals (562), VouchersSection (553), IndependentsView (426), AidFormEditor (194), ApplicationFeeSection (245), AddonsSection (335), financials/shared (392)

═══════════════════════════════════════════════
(c) THIN OR ZERO DIMENSIONS
═══════════════════════════════════════════════
ZERO COVERAGE:
  1. RTL / Arabic — dir="rtl" IS set (LanguageContext:31) and the codebase is 104 inline left/right, 38 borderLeft vs 6 borderRight, 63 physical text-left/right, vs 7 insetInlineStart. Plus :lang(ar) is beaten by 2403 inline fontFamily declarations.
  2. Print / PDF — zero @media print, zero @page, on a product with invoices, placards, CVs and scoreboards.
  3. 404 / not-found — no file exists.
  4. Route-level loading.tsx — all six are bare spinners; conferences/[slug] shows spinner→skeleton→content.
  5. Skip links, landmarks, screen-reader structure — 0 skip links, 10 <main> against 117 <h1>.
  6. aria-live / status announcements — 4 total in an optimistic-update product.
  7. Dialog semantics — 5 role=dialog and 4 aria-modal against ~21 hand-rolled overlays.
  8. First-run vs returning, for every role except chair.

THIN (mentioned, never swept):
  9. Form-validation presentation — 26 aria-invalid, 2 aria-describedby, 62 required.
 10. Motion / reduced-motion — 4 guards total; the map video, the 2.3s homepage reveal, the 3.5s caucus screen and every neu hover lift are unguarded.
 11. Iconography — five parallel systems (lucide 97 files, Twemoji ×50, Fluent3D ×35, 104 raw emoji, text glyphs as controls).
 12. Empty states — dozens reported, no shared primitive, four visual languages.
 13. Projector / room legibility — 263 elements ≤10.5px through a fractional transform, never assessed against the actual deployment context.
 14. One-item / very-long-name states — reported as isolated bugs, never as a class.
 15. Copy-to-clipboard feedback — six buttons, four acknowledgements, reported four times separately.

VERIFIED FINE, DO NOT RE-AUDIT:
  • Dark seals / logos on forest — LogoDisc solves it deliberately (#FDFCF9 disc, 7% margin, fallbackTone='plain' for dark surfaces). The gap is adoption, not design.
  • Native date pickers — genuinely clean except the two already-found violations (settings datetime-local, committees/documents type="time"). No type="date" anywhere.
  • /auth/signin, signup, forgot, reset — the cleanest surfaces in the product (0 focus:outline-none, 0 transition-all, shared primitives, correct DatePicker usage). Use authUi.tsx as the model for the missing Field/Input primitives.

═══════════════════════════════════════════════
NON-UI OBSERVATIONS (per the brief)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════
  • MapClient.tsx:448 — `tooltipPos`/`hoveredDef` are computed and never consumed; dead render path, reported as the missing hover state.
  • AboutClient.tsx:93 — the ambassador insert error is ignored, so a failed write still shows the success tick. Applications can be silently lost. (Also flagged by the design-system auditor.)
  • CLAUDE.md:63 names MotionsPanel, which has never existed as a file.
  • layout.tsx:102 loads Playfair Display as ital@1 only and layout.tsx:103 loads five weights of Noto Sans Arabic that inline styles override — two webfont downloads on every page, neither rendering as intended.
  • layout.tsx preloads /Conferences.webp with fetchPriority="high" on every route, including /join, /create and /sessions which never paint it.

BIGGEST WINS FROM THIS CRITIQUE, IF ONLY A FEW HAPPEN — (a) ship not-found.tsx; (b) move fitScale() into Portal, which corrects nine popovers at once and finally makes the AGENTS.md popover rule true on session pages; (c) the skip-link + <main> pass, the cheapest accessibility gain available; (d) one global prefers-reduced-motion block; (e) delete ResolutionsPanel/SpeakersListPanel/CaucusPanel and fix CLAUDE.md:63; (f) dispatch /delegation/[societyId] and /conferences/[slug]/pay for real audits — they are the two largest completely-unexamined user surfaces in the product.


---

# Gap sweep: uncovered-components  
_89 findings_


## src/components/ui — the whole shadcn folder

- **`src/components/ui/alert.tsx:1`** — 9 of the 14 shadcn primitives are imported by nothing: alert, avatar, badge, carousel, field, input-group, progress, separator, spinner. Only input, textarea, label, dialog and button are used, and only by two files (AboutClient, ContactClient). They are a second, parallel design system sitting dormant in the repo, and every new contributor will reach for them before finding neu.tsx. → _Delete the 9 unimported files. Either commit to shadcn as the base layer and retheme its CSS variables to the forest/ivory palette, or delete the folder entirely and port AboutClient/ContactClient to the neu.tsx + authUi primitives. Do not leave both._  `[consistency]`

## About / Contact — shadcn primitives

- **`src/app/globals.css:104`** — Every shadcn token is default greyscale oklch: --popover: oklch(1 0 0) (pure white), --primary: oklch(0.205 0 0) (near-black), --muted-foreground: oklch(0.556 0 0) (neutral grey), --border/--input: oklch(0.922 0 0). So ui/dialog.tsx's `bg-popover text-popover-foreground` renders a pure-white, cold-grey-bordered modal inside a warm #EDE7D8 / #F0EBDD product. AboutClient patches it with inline `text-[#1C1410]` / `color:'#9A8A78'` on the title and description only — the card, border and ring stay grey. → _Map the shadcn variables onto the NEU tokens in :root (--popover: #F0EBDD, --primary: #1B3828, --primary-foreground: #EED98A, --muted-foreground: #9A8A78, --border/--input: #DDD4C0, --ring: #1B3828). One 8-line change retints every shadcn component instead of inline-patching them one at a time._  `[consistency]`

## About / Contact — shadcn Button

- **`src/components/ui/button.tsx:8`** — `transition-all` on the base button variant. It animates every animatable property including width, height, padding and the focus ring, so a button that grows on hover or gains a ring will visibly lerp its box. This is the one place in the product the rule is broken at the primitive level rather than on a single element. → _Replace `transition-all` with `transition-[background-color,color,box-shadow,transform] duration-200 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)]` to match EASE in neu.tsx._  `[polish]`

## About / Contact — shadcn Dialog

- **`src/components/ui/dialog.tsx:64`** — This is the only semantically-correct dialog in the product (Radix: focus trap, aria-modal, Escape, scroll lock, portal) and it is used on exactly one screen. Every other overlay — QuestionBuilder's two edit modals, LogoCropModal, PositionPaperCard's Replace confirm, ConferenceRosterPicker's paste review, ShareAchievementModal, LiveModals' ModalShell — is hand-rolled with `fixed inset-0 z-50` and re-implements a subset of that behaviour, differently each time. → _Promote one house `<Modal>` (either this one retinted, or LiveModals' ModalShell hardened with Escape + focus trap + aria-modal) and migrate the hand-rolled overlays onto it. At minimum, extract the shared shell so backdrop opacity, radius, close-button position and Escape behaviour stop diverging per file._  `[consistency]`

## ResolutionsPanel (dead)

- **`src/components/ResolutionsPanel.tsx:47`** — This file is the last survivor of the old dark theme and it is visually broken. `bg-blue-600 ... text-[#1C1410]` puts near-black ink (#1C1410) on a blue-600 button — roughly 2.6:1, unreadable. The same find-and-replace hit every button and every card title: `text-[#1C1410]` on `bg-[#141929]` (line 107), on `bg-blue-900` disabled (line 80). The panel also uses navy #0d1120 / #141929 / #1e2540 surfaces and Tailwind text-yellow-400 / text-blue-400 / text-emerald-400 status colours that exist nowhere else in the product. → _Delete the file — it is imported by nothing (verified). Same for SpeakersListPanel.tsx and CaucusPanel.tsx. Then fix CLAUDE.md:63, which lists a MotionsPanel that has never existed._  `[rule-violation]`

## Chat — avatars

- **`src/components/chat/ChatAvatar.tsx:57`** — Three avatar kinds, three different visual languages: 'everyone' is a raw 📢 emoji on a gold gradient, 'dais' a raw 🪑 emoji on forest, 'chair' Outfit initials in gold on forest, 'delegate' a Twemoji flag. The two emoji discs render in the OS emoji font, so they look different on macOS, Windows and Android and sit at a different optical weight than the crisp flag and the typographic initials beside them in the same list. → _Use lucide glyphs (Megaphone, Gavel) at the same stroke weight as the rest of the product for 'everyone' and 'dais', or route them through Emoji3D/NeuIconDisc from neu.tsx so all four kinds share one seat treatment._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/components/chat/ChatAvatar.tsx:113`** — A delegate whose country name does not resolve (`getCountryByName` miss — custom delegations, characters in a crisis committee, 'European Union') renders `<FlagImg code="" />`, i.e. an empty disc with nothing in it. There is no monogram fallback, so custom delegations show as blank circles in the conversation list. → _Fall back to the same `initials(name)` treatment the 'chair' branch already uses when no country code resolves, tinted with NEU_GRADIENTS.sage so it still reads as a delegate rather than a chair._  `[missing-state]`

## Position paper — shared action button

- **`src/components/PositionPaperButtons.tsx:42`** — The disabled state is `background: rgba(27,56,40,0.12)`, `color: NEU.muted`, `boxShadow: 'none'`. Removing the shadow entirely makes a disabled button look like a hole in the card rather than an unavailable control, and #9A8A78 on a 12% forest wash over ivory is roughly 2.9:1 — the label is barely readable while busy. → _Keep NEU.outSm on the disabled state and drop only the coloured glow, and darken the disabled label to #6E5F4E. A disabled button should read as 'the same button, not right now', not as a different component._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/PositionPaperButtons.tsx:60`** — Both the `<a>` and `<button>` branches carry `focus:outline-none` with no replacement ring. APPROVE and REJECT on the chair's paper roster are therefore completely invisible to keyboard users — a reviewer tabbing through a 30-country roster has no idea where they are. → _Add `focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2` with `outlineColor: NEU.forest` (the pattern authUi.tsx:332 already uses) to the shared `sharedStyle`. One fix covers every consumer._  `[contrast]`

## Chair / organiser — position paper roster

- **`src/components/PositionPaperRoster.tsx:154`** — A single row can carry six competing chips inside ~200px of right-hand gutter: unread count (forest badge), LATE (amber pill), status badge, APPROVE (green gradient pill), REJECT (red outline pill), chevron. On a country with an unread late submitted paper all six render, plus the flag and two lines of text on the left. The row is a wall of colour with no dominant element. → _Collapse APPROVE/REJECT into the paper detail page (the row already navigates there) or reveal them on row hover only, and merge LATE into the status badge as 'Submitted · late'. Keep at most unread + status + chevron in the resting row._  `[density]`
- **`src/components/PositionPaperRoster.tsx:48`** — STATUS_STYLES here and `ppStatusMap` in PositionPaperCard.tsx:30 are two copies of the same status palette that have already drifted: `reviewed` is #6E5F4E on the chair side and #9A8A78 on the delegate side. The same paper shows a different colour to the two people looking at it, and #9A8A78 on rgba(154,138,120,0.15) is about 2.6:1 at 9px/700 — illegible. → _Move the map into src/lib/positionPapers.ts (both files already import from it) and use #6E5F4E for `reviewed` everywhere. While there, raise the pill type from 9px to 10.5px._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/components/PositionPaperRoster.tsx:134`** — A country with no paper reads 'No submission' in muted grey with no action. The reviewer's most common next move — nudge that delegate — is not offered anywhere on the row, and there is no indication whether the deadline has passed for them. → _Make the no-paper right-hand slot read 'Not submitted · 3 days overdue' when a deadline exists, and add a quiet 'Remind' text action that queues the existing chase email._  `[copy]`

## Apply / Aid form — rendered custom questions

- **`src/components/CustomQuestionsField.tsx:6`** — The applicant-facing input recipe (borderRadius 12, padding '12px 16px', 1.5px #DDD4C0) does not match the builder's preview recipe (QuestionBuilder.tsx:17 — borderRadius 10, padding '10px 14px') or authUi's (rounded-xl px-4 py-3). So the organiser builds a form that looks measurably different from what the applicant fills in, and there are three near-identical input styles in the codebase. → _Extract a single `<TextInput>` / `inputStyle` into a shared module (authUi's is the most-used) and import it in CustomQuestionsField, QuestionBuilder, VouchersSection, AddonsSection and ApplicationFeeSection._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/components/CustomQuestionsField.tsx:94`** — Focus and blur mutate `style.borderColor` imperatively. The blur handler restores `hasError ? '#8B2020' : '#DDD4C0'`, which works, but there is no `aria-invalid`, no `aria-describedby` linking the field to its 'This question is required.' message, and the error only appears after a failed submit — never inline. A screen-reader user gets a submit that silently does nothing. → _Set `aria-invalid={hasError}` and `aria-describedby={`${q.id}-err`}` on every input, give the error `<p id={`${q.id}-err`} role="alert">`, and scroll the first missing field into view on failed submit._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/components/CustomQuestionsField.tsx:132`** — The `dropdown` question type renders a bare native `<select>` with the shared inputStyle but no appearance reset — so it shows the OS chevron and OS row styling, unlike CVEntryModal.tsx:318 which does reset appearance and paint its own chevron. Two different select looks in the same product. → _Reuse CVEntryModal's selectStyle (appearance:none plus the inline SVG chevron) here, and pull it into the shared input module alongside inputStyle._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/components/CustomQuestionsField.tsx:152`** — Radio and checkbox inputs are 16×16px native controls (`w-4 h-4`) with `accentColor: '#1B3828'`. The whole row is a `<label>` so the hit area is larger, but the control itself has no focus ring, no hover state, and at 16px it is well under the 24px minimum for a target that people tap on a phone while sitting in a committee room. → _Bump to `w-5 h-5`, add `padding: 6px 0` to the label row so the touch target reaches 44px, and give the label a hover wash (rgba(27,56,40,0.04)) so it reads as clickable._  `[polish]`

## Organiser — question builder

- **`src/components/QuestionBuilder.tsx:200`** — Opening 'Add Question' and choosing Dropdown/Multiple choice/Checkboxes immediately shows the red error 'At least 2 options are required.' before the organiser has typed anything — `options` initialises to ['',''] so `validOptions.length` is 0. The form scolds you for a state you have not had a chance to leave yet. → _Track a `touched` flag (or only show the error once `canSave` has been false on an attempted save) and render the two empty option rows without an error until then._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/components/QuestionBuilder.tsx:448`** — The card action says DELETE, but for any question that already existed it archives instead (handleDelete:562) — the card silently reappears in an 'ARCHIVED QUESTIONS' section further down the page with no confirmation, no toast and no explanation. The button text does not describe what happens. → _Label it ARCHIVE for existing questions and DELETE only for ones created in this sitting (`newIds.has(id)` already distinguishes them), and show a one-line inline note the first time: 'Archived — existing answers are kept.'_  `[copy]`
- **`src/components/QuestionBuilder.tsx:129`** — `transition-all` on the type-picker chips and on all three add-block buttons (lines 655, 664, 673). The add buttons also mutate borderColor and color through inline mouse handlers while `transition-all` animates every property, including the dashed border-width. → _Replace with `transition-colors` on all four, and move the hover recolour into a `hovered` state variable rather than mutating `e.currentTarget.style` — the imperative version breaks if the component re-renders mid-hover._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/QuestionBuilder.tsx:397`** — BlockCard is a flat `rgba(27,56,40,0.03)` wash with a 1px `rgba(27,56,40,0.1)` border at radius 12 — a fourth card treatment alongside NeuCard (extruded, 22px, no border), accountUi's GlassCard (blurred 0.82 ivory, 1.5px #D8CDB6, 20px) and participant SectionCard (#FAF8F3, 1px #DDD4C0, 20px). Sibling organiser screens therefore do not look like one product. → _Render BlockCard as `<NeuCard style={{ borderRadius: 16, padding: 16 }}>` so the builder matches the applications page benchmark, and pick ONE of GlassCard / SectionCard / NeuCard as the house card._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/components/QuestionBuilder.tsx:403`** — The drag affordance is a GripVertical glyph with `cursor: 'grab'`, but `draggable` sits on the wrapper div (line 616) — the entire card is draggable, including the EDIT / DUPLICATE / DELETE text buttons, while only the 15px grip looks draggable. There is also no drop indicator below the last item, no `cursor: grabbing` while dragging, and no keyboard reorder. → _Put `cursor: grab` on the whole card, dim the dragged card to 0.5 opacity via a `draggingIdx` state, render the drop line after the last index too, and add ↑/↓ keyboard move buttons (QuestionEditModal already has that exact control for options at line 162)._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/QuestionBuilder.tsx:383`** — A Title block gets a 4px gold accent bar AND a gold 'TITLE' Pill; a Section gets a 4px forest bar AND a forest 'SECTION' Pill. The same fact is encoded twice within 30px, and the accent bar is the strongest colour in a list where question cards — the actual content — have none. → _Drop the ACCENT bar entirely and keep the Pill, or drop the Pill and keep the bar. Give question cards the visual weight instead — they are what applicants see._  `[hierarchy]`
- **`src/components/QuestionBuilder.tsx:605`** — The empty state is one line of 14px grey text, 'No questions yet.', left-aligned above three dashed add buttons. It does not say what the form is for, that applicants see it, or that an empty form is valid (applicants just get the built-in fields). → _Replace with a small centred empty state: an icon, 'No custom questions yet', and 'Applicants will only see the standard fields. Add a question to ask for anything else.'_  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/components/QuestionBuilder.tsx:602`** — There is no way to preview the form as an applicant sees it. The builder's card list shows the label, a type pill and a required pill but not the placeholder, the help text, or the actual control — and the builder's input styling does not match the applicant's anyway. The organiser has to publish and open the apply page to check their own form. → _Add a 'Preview' toggle that renders `<CustomQuestionsField blocks={value} answers={{}} onChange={()=>{}} />` inline — the component already exists and takes exactly these props._  `[wasted-effort]`

## Organiser — logo crop

- **`src/components/LogoCropModal.tsx:303`** — The whole modal is a bare `fixed inset-0 z-50` with no Portal, while QuestionBuilder's modals in the same settings page ARE portaled. It is mounted from /manage/[slug]/settings, /conferences/new and ConferenceDetailClient — any of which could grow a transformed or overflow-hidden ancestor and clip it. It also has no Escape-to-close (backdrop click and the X are the only exits) and no focus trap. → _Wrap in `<Portal>`, add an Escape keydown listener that calls onCancel when not saving, and add `role="dialog" aria-modal="true"`._  `[rule-violation]`
- **`src/components/LogoCropModal.tsx:311`** — The preview disc is a hard-coded 280px (DISC) inside a modal of `width: min(400px, 92vw)` with `p-6` (24px each side). At 320px viewport that is 294 − 48 = 246px of content for a 280px disc — the circle overflows its own card. Even at 375px it is 297px, only 17px of slack. → _Make DISC responsive: `const disc = Math.min(280, Math.round(Math.min(400, vw * 0.92) - 56))`, or set the disc to `width: min(280px, 100%)` and derive SAFE/OUT from the measured element._  `[responsive]`
- **`src/components/LogoCropModal.tsx:348`** — While the image decodes, `natural` is null so the <img> renders at opacity 0 and the disc is an empty near-white circle with a dashed ring — indistinguishable from a fully transparent PNG that failed to load. There is no spinner and no onError handler, so a corrupt or non-image file leaves the organiser staring at an empty disc with an enabled-looking Cancel and a disabled SAVE LOGO. → _Show a small spinner inside the disc until `natural` is set, add `onError` on the <img> that surfaces 'That file could not be read as an image' with a Choose another file action._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/components/LogoCropModal.tsx:399`** — FIT and FILL are opaque labels; the only explanation is a native `title` tooltip that needs a 1-second hover to appear. Below them a separate line says 'Drag to position, keep your artwork inside the dashed ring', which is the third instruction competing for the same 60px of vertical space (chips, hint line, zoom slider). → _Rename to 'Fit whole logo' / 'Fill the circle' and delete the separate hint line — the grab cursor and the dashed ring already teach dragging._  `[copy]`
- **`src/components/LogoCropModal.tsx:459`** — 'SAVING...' uses three ASCII periods. The same product uses a typographic ellipsis elsewhere in the same flows ('Confirming your purchase…', 'Preview as an applicant...'  — which is also ASCII). Across the uncovered files the split is roughly even, so the inconsistency shows up on adjacent screens. → _Standardise on the single character … everywhere. It is a find-and-replace of '...' inside string literals across LogoCropModal:459, DefaultEmailPreviewModal:138, DelegationCreditsCard:165, PositionPaperCard:425._  `[copy]`

## Organiser — default email preview

- **`src/components/DefaultEmailPreviewModal.tsx:111`** — The 'Preview as an applicant' typeahead is an `absolute` div inside a modal whose body is a flex column with `maxHeight: '88vh'` and an `overflow-y-auto` iframe region. It is not portaled and its `zIndex: 10` is local to the modal — the AGENTS.md popover rule names exactly this case. With a short viewport or a long candidate list the dropdown is clipped by the modal's own bounds. → _Port it to the PaymentMenu pattern: measure the input's getBoundingClientRect, render the list through @/components/Portal at fixed coords, reposition on capture-phase scroll and resize, and flip upward near the bottom edge._  `[rule-violation]`
- **`src/components/DefaultEmailPreviewModal.tsx:161`** — The preview iframe is `width: 600, height: 520` fixed, inside a modal of `min(94vw, 680px)` and `maxHeight: 88vh`. On a 13" laptop (768px tall) 88vh is 676px, and the header + search row + subject + close button already consume ~250px — the 520px iframe is squeezed and the whole modal scrolls, so the organiser reads the email through a 400px porthole. → _Give the iframe `flex: 1; minHeight: 320px; width: 100%` and let it take the remaining height, and remove the redundant full-width CLOSE button at the bottom (there is already an X at top-right)._  `[responsive]`
- **`src/components/DefaultEmailPreviewModal.tsx:146`** — Four separate lines of instructional grey text stack above the preview: 'This is what sends today — draft a template from this list to override it.', the test-send result, 'Search and select an applicant above to resolve fields, or preview unresolved.', and the subject line. Three of them are muted 12px, so the actual subject — the most important line — has no more weight than the help text. → _Keep only the first sentence, drop the 'Search and select…' line (the input placeholder already says it), and give the subject 15px/700 ink so it reads as the email's subject._  `[density]`

## Achievement share modal

- **`src/components/ShareAchievementModal.tsx:111`** — The hero button says 'Share this'. On desktop, where navigator.share does not exist, it silently falls through to handleCopy — and the only acknowledgement is the small round Link2 button at the far right of the row below flipping to a check. The user pressed a 50px-tall gradient button and the feedback appeared on a different, unrelated control. → _Set a `sharedFeedback` state in handlePrimaryShare and swap the hero button's own label to '✓ Link copied' for 2s, or label the button 'Copy link' when canNativeShare is false so it says what it will do._  `[missing-state]`
- **`src/components/ShareAchievementModal.tsx:175`** — Every decorative animation loops forever: gvConfetti `infinite` on 16 pieces (line 256), gvShimmer `infinite` (239), gvGlow `infinite` (227), gvFloat `infinite` (272). A 'congratulations' burst that never stops turns into visual noise within five seconds and keeps the GPU busy for as long as the modal is open. There is no `prefers-reduced-motion` guard anywhere in the file. → _Run the confetti and shimmer once (`animation-iteration-count: 1`, `forwards`), keep only the slow glow/float, and wrap the whole KEYFRAMES block in `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)` with a static fallback._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/ShareAchievementModal.tsx:80`** — `metaLine` joins `entry.committee` and `entry.allocation` raw. A delegate who typed the spelled-out committee gets 'Disarmament and International Security Committee  ·  Germany' rendered at 13.5px across a 420px card — it wraps to two or three lines under the conference name. The house rule says a long committee name renders as its ACRONYM; CVTimeline already has a `committeeLabel()` helper for this and it is not used here. → _Import `committeeLabel` from CVTimeline (or `committeeDisplayName` from @/lib/presetNames) and pass entry.committee through it before building metaLine._  `[rule-violation]`
- **`src/components/ShareAchievementModal.tsx:293`** — The conference name is an h2 at 24px/900 with no truncation, clamp or hyphenation on a max-width 420px card. 'Asia Youth International Model United Nations 2027' wraps to four lines and pushes the award chip, the divider, the delegate name and the entire ivory action tray below the fold on a phone. → _Add `overflow-wrap: anywhere` plus a 2-line clamp (`display:-webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp:2; -webkit-box-orient:vertical; overflow:hidden`) and step the font down to 20px when the name exceeds ~28 characters._  `[responsive]`
- **`src/components/ShareAchievementModal.tsx:119`** — The clipboard fallback is `window.prompt('Copy your public CV link:', url)` — a raw OS dialog in the middle of the most designed screen in the product. It fires whenever navigator.clipboard is unavailable (any non-secure context) or throws. → _Replace with an inline read-only input inside the tray, pre-selected, plus the text 'Press ⌘C to copy'. Never surface window.prompt in a celebratory flow._  `[polish]`

## Personal conference card

- **`src/components/PersonalConferenceCard.tsx:289`** — manageHref, manageDelegationHref and resubmitHref each render their OWN `mt-3.5 pt-3` block with its own 1px top divider. A head delegate who is also an organiser gets two stacked dividers and two stacked single-button rows; add a rejected resubmission and it is three. Worse, the three buttons are three different visual languages inside one card: a neu extruded ivory pill, a gold gradient pill, and a red-tinted outline pill. → _Render one divider and one flex-wrap row containing all present actions. Pick one button treatment (the neu extruded pill) and differentiate only by icon and label; reserve the red tint for genuinely destructive actions._  `[hierarchy]`
- **`src/components/PersonalConferenceCard.tsx:207`** — The card hand-rolls exactly what NeuCard already does — NEU.surface, hover ? NEU.outHover : NEU.out, translateY(-2px), `box-shadow 260ms ${EASE}, transform 260ms ${EASE}` — but at borderRadius 20 instead of NeuCard's 22. This same lift recipe is re-implemented inline in at least five uncovered files (here, CVTimeline.tsx:210, PositionPaperButtons.tsx:47, ShareAchievementModal.tsx:369, ConferenceRosterPicker rows). → _Use `<NeuCard hover style={{ borderRadius: 20, padding: '15px 16px 16px', opacity: muted ? 0.82 : 1 }}>` and delete the local hovered state. If a card needs a non-22 radius often enough, add a `radius` prop to NeuCard._  `[consistency]`
- **`src/components/PersonalConferenceCard.tsx:239`** — The whole card lifts and the title turns forest on hover, but only the title text is a `<Link>` — roughly 150×22px of a ~400×160px card actually navigates. Users will click the logo, the date row or the role chips and nothing happens. The ArrowUpRight cue is also `opacity: hovered ? 1 : 0`, so on touch devices it never appears at all. → _Make the whole card the link (NeuCard already accepts `href`) with the action pills as `stopPropagation` children, and keep the arrow at a resting opacity of ~0.45 so touch users see the affordance._  `[polish]`
- **`src/components/PersonalConferenceCard.tsx:70`** — `formatDateRange` returns the bare string 'TBD' when either date is missing — untranslated jargon in a product that ships an i18n layer, and it renders next to a gold CalendarClock icon as if it were a real date. The same function mixes dash conventions: '12–14 Mar 2027' (no spaces) in the same-month branch, '28 Feb – 2 Mar 2027' (with spaces) otherwise. → _Return 'Dates to be announced' (via the translation layer) and hide the calendar icon in that case. Use spaced en dashes in all three branches._  `[copy]`
- **`src/components/PersonalConferenceCard.tsx:218`** — `muted` past conferences render at `opacity: 0.82` on the whole card. That drops the secondary line (NEU.muted #9A8A78 at 10.5px) to roughly 3.0:1 against #F0EBDD — below AA for small text — and dims the logo image too, which was already the weakest element. → _Do not dim the card. Signal 'past' with a small neutral Pill ('Completed') and desaturate only the countdown/accent colours, leaving text at full opacity._  `[contrast]`

## Personal conference card — skeleton

- **`src/components/PersonalConferenceCard.tsx:377`** — ConferenceCardSkeleton omits the countdown chip and every affordance row, so it is materially shorter than the real card — content jumps down when data lands. Its shimmer is also `1.4s ... infinite` with no reduced-motion guard, and it is emitted via `<style jsx>` so the same keyframes are re-declared once per instance in a grid of cards. → _Add a right-hand chip block and a bottom divider+pill row to the skeleton so the two heights match, move `cal-shimmer` into globals.css behind `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)`, and reuse the existing src/components/Skeleton.tsx primitive instead of a local shimmer._  `[missing-state]`

## /account shared primitives — Pill

- **`src/app/account/accountUi.tsx:26`** — PILL_TONES ships seven tones, two of which are cold: `sky` (#365A72 text, #4A7896 dot) and `plum` (#57406A / #8A6BA0). They are the only blue and purple in a forest/gold/amber/ivory system, and they land side by side with warm tones on the same conference card (ROLE_TONE maps delegate→sky, advisor→plum). AWARD_TIER_STYLE.silver adds a third cold neutral, #4C5563. → _Either re-derive sky and plum as warm-shifted variants (a teal-leaning green and a mauve-leaning amber), or drop them and distinguish delegate/advisor by icon rather than hue. Seven tones for a status chip is more than the product needs._  `[consistency]`

## /account shared primitives — Eyebrow

- **`src/app/account/accountUi.tsx:411`** — The default Eyebrow is 9px, weight 700, letterSpacing 0.14em, uppercase, in #B6871F. On #FAF8F3 that is about 3.4:1 — below the 4.5:1 AA floor for text under 18px — and 9px uppercase with wide tracking is at the edge of legibility on a laptop, let alone projected in a committee room. The same 9px gold eyebrow is copy-pasted inline (not via this component) in DelegationPlacard:62, StudyGuideCard:56, DelegationCreditsCard:107, PositionPaperCard:343 and RequestsPanel:411. → _Raise the default to 10.5px and darken to #8A6614 (≈5.1:1), then replace the five inline copies with `<Eyebrow>` so the change lands everywhere at once._  `[contrast]`

## /account shared primitives — PillToggle

- **`src/app/account/accountUi.tsx:479`** — The off state is `#DDD4C0` on a #FAF8F3 card — about 1.15:1, so an off toggle is nearly invisible and reads as 'not loaded' rather than 'off'. The control is 40×22 (md) or 32×18 (sm), far under a 44px touch target, has `focus:outline-none` with no ring, and animates `left` (`transition-[left]`) which is a layout property rather than `transform`. → _Darken the off track to rgba(27,56,40,0.22), animate the thumb with `transform: translateX()`, add a focus-visible ring, and wrap the control in a 44×44 padded button so the hit area is compliant without changing the visual size._  `[polish]`

## /account shared primitives — GlassCard

- **`src/app/account/accountUi.tsx:440`** — GlassCard (rgba(250,248,243,0.82) + backdrop-blur(14px) saturate(1.4) + 1.5px #D8CDB6 + a two-layer drop shadow, radius 20) is a card language of its own — and its `p-6 md:p-7` padding is wrong for its biggest consumer, CVTimeline, which fights it with `!p-5` / `!p-4` important overrides (CVTimeline.tsx:178). Counting NeuCard, SectionCard and QuestionBuilder's flat wash, that is four card treatments across the account/conference surfaces. → _Add a `padding` prop to GlassCard so callers stop using `!p-*`, and converge on one card: either GlassCard everywhere on /account, or NeuCard everywhere. Do not keep both alive in the same scroll._  `[consistency]`

## /account shared primitives — ExperienceInfo

- **`src/app/account/accountUi.tsx:239`** — This is the one correctly hover-driven 'i' explainer in the product (AGENTS.md rule satisfied), but its edge-flip math relies on `POP_H = 348`, a hardcoded guess at the popover height. Add or remove one EXPERIENCE_BAND and the flip decision is silently wrong. The trigger also uses `cursor: 'help'` while also being click-to-toggle — two different affordance signals on one 20px button. → _Measure the popover with a ref after the first paint (or `getBoundingClientRect` on the portaled node) instead of the constant, and drop the onClick toggle so hover/focus is the only interaction, matching the rule._  `[polish]`

## /account shared primitives — AwardChip

- **`src/app/account/accountUi.tsx:651`** — AwardChip is an inline-flex rounded pill with no max-width and no wrapping. A custom free-text honour ('Best Delegate in the Historical Security Council') renders as one unbreakable 13px/800 capsule that overflows the 420px share card and the CV entry card. → _Add `maxWidth: '100%'` plus `overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap` on the label span, and a `title` with the full text._  `[responsive]`

## Participant — shared payment chips

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/participant/shared.tsx:83`** — PAID and COVERED share the identical background rgba(61,122,82,0.13) and differ only by text colour #2A5A3C vs #2A6858 — six hue-degrees apart, indistinguishable at 10px. WAIVED and REFUNDED are byte-for-byte identical (same bg, same #6B5F52). Four semantically different payment outcomes render as two visual states, which defeats the point of a chip. → _Give COVERED a distinct gold-tinted treatment (it means 'someone else paid'), and give REFUNDED a rose tint. Or drop the colour distinction and put the difference in the label ('Paid by delegation', 'Refunded')._  `[consistency]`

## Participant — shared SectionCard

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/participant/shared.tsx:14`** — The file's own comment admits SectionCard is 'duplicated from ConferenceDetailClient's own SectionCard rather than imported' to dodge an import cycle. ## Participant — delegation placard

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/participant/DelegationPlacard.tsx:94`** — There is no loading state. On first paint the card renders the fallback 'Delegation' plus 'No faculty advisor listed', then swaps to the real society name and advisors when the fetch lands — so every delegate sees a false 'you have no advisor' flash. A failed fetch leaves that false statement permanently on screen with no error. → _Track a `loading` flag and render two shimmer bars for the name and advisor lines; on error show 'Could not load your delegation. ## Participant — study guide

- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/participant/StudyGuideCard.tsx:63`** — The empty branch fires for both `!committeeId` and `guides.length === 0`, so a delegate who has not been allocated to a committee yet is told "The study guide hasn't been released yet." — which is not why they cannot see one. The scheduled variant ('Releases Mar 4, 2027.') is also a bare fragment with a trailing period and no context. → _Split the branches: no committee → 'Your study guide appears here once you're allocated to a committee.'; scheduled → 'Your chairs will publish the study guide on 4 March.'; neither → 'Your chairs haven't uploaded a study guide yet.'_  `[copy]`
- **`src/app/conferences/[slug]/participant/StudyGuideCard.tsx:61`** — The loading state is a hand-rolled `w-5 h-5 rounded-full border-2 animate-spin` div. Three spinner implementations, none shared. → _Pick one (Skeleton for content-shaped loads, a single shared Spinner for actions) and replace the hand-rolled circles.
