- 19 search inputs exist and only the applications one has all four of a pressed pill, a Search glyph, an `aria-label` and a conditional clear ✕. Nine placeholders use `'...'` and seven use `'…'`.
- Nothing exists for a first-time delegate, advisor, head delegate or organiser — the 17-step chair tutorial is the only first-run affordance in the product, and it is on the one surface whose user is usually the experienced one. It is also stored per-device, so an experienced chair on a new laptop is asked again.
- Two fixtures would catch most of the layout failures at once: a one-item committee (the Motions modal leaves ~430px of void; the Scoreboard ranks nine identical scores 1–10; the up-next strip has no ordering) and a 190-delegation committee whose names include "United Kingdom of Great Britain and Northern Ireland", "Democratic Republic of the Congo" and "Republic of Korea" (ragged baselines, four legible characters in an 80px tile, 105px role cards holding "FACULTY ADVISOR"). Set one truncation rule (two lines, ellipsis, full value in a `title`) and one tie rule for ranked lists.

---

## Where UI would save the most time

- **A hand-off screen after `/create`.** Creation currently ends in a silent `router.push` with the 4-digit chair code generated and never shown (`create/page.tsx:493`). One screen — session code large with Copy and "Delegates enter this at gavelling.com", chair code beneath with "Co-chairs need this one too" — removes the single most common start-of-session scramble.
- **A session-code entry on `gavelling.com` and forgiving code parsing on `/join`.** Today a delegate handed a code lands on a conference directory, must find the SESSIONS pill, then wait out 2.3s of animation (`VariantStagefront.tsx:388`, `HomeClient.tsx:569`); and a pasted code with a trailing space or a dash fails with a flat "not found" (`join/page.tsx:218`). One field plus `replace(/\s+/g,'').toUpperCase()` and a dash split fixes the highest-volume flow in the product.
- **Promote the delegate floor card and render it in every phase.** "How close am I to speaking?" is a `text-xs` span in a card header and disappears entirely during a caucus (`delegate/[code]/page.tsx:1382`). It is the one reason 30 people open the app; make it a full-width card with the number at ~48px. Fixing the 390px tab bar in the same pass (`:1134`) stops four labels truncating to single letters.
- **Keyboard shortcuts and an undo on `/voting`.** 41 mouse clicks with no way back and no recovery from a mis-click (`voting/[code]/page.tsx:785`, `:1016`). Bind F / A / B / R and a Previous control, print the key on each button, and the roll-call vote goes from a two-minute exercise in precision to thirty seconds.
- **Prev/next plus keyboard in the applications review modal, and bulk actions on the invoice ledger.** 200 applications are 200 open-read-decide-close cycles today (`applications/page.tsx:3049`), and marking 60 delegation invoices paid is 60 clicks with 60 confirms (`invoices/page.tsx:796`) — while the bulk bar the ledger needs already exists one route away.
- **Cut the caucus loading screens and normalise the GSL control row.** 3.5 seconds of a live committee where the chair cannot add the first speaker (`chair/[code]/page.tsx:2062`), then five controls at five heights with an 8px label wrapping onto two rows (`:3215`). One 44px height, one primary, a 900ms transition.
- **Bulk roll-call buttons mid-session and a roster search that actually filters.** After a break, marking 30 delegates present is 30 slider taps because the bulk row renders only in pre-session (`RollCallPanel.tsx:559`), and searching a 190-seat GA leaves 190 ghosted rows in place rather than filtering (`:622`).
- **One shared focus ring plus a `not-found.tsx`.** 843 `focus:outline-none` with no replacement makes every surface unusable by keyboard, and the missing 404 is the most-hit unstyled screen in a product built on shareable links. Both are single-file changes with product-wide effect.

---

# Appendix — /conferences/map and /conferences/roles (deep dive)

# SALVAGED AUDIT — /conferences/map and /conferences/roles

Source: orphaned child agent of the stopped `conferences-public` auditor. Merge into the final workflow synthesis. Findings are from code reading.

## Two framing facts

- **The roles board is unreachable.** `src/app/conferences/roles/ConferencesRolesClient.tsx:32` — `JOB_BOARD_ENABLED = false` early-returns `<ComingSoonScreen/>` at `:997`. ~900 lines of board UI never render today.
- **The two pages use different design systems.** `MapClient.tsx` imports nothing from `@/components/neu` — no `NEU`, no `OUTFIT`, no `EASE`, no `NeuCard`. Every token is retyped as a literal. Roles is fully neumorphic. Side by side they read as two products.

## map/MapClient.tsx

- `:606` info card is `#FAF8F3` + `1px solid #DDD4C0` + single drop shadow + `borderRadius: 16`. Breaks surface colour (`NEU.surface` is `#F0EBDD`), the dual-shadow extrusion model, and `NeuCard`'s radius 22 — all at once.
- Hardcoded tokens throughout a 1007-line file: `#1B3828` at `:457,834,867,902,987`; `#EDE7D8` at `:481,792,866,928,964`; `#EED98A` at `:482,835`; `#B6871F` at `:641,761,806`; `#9A8A78` at `:745,772,818`. Zero token imports.
- `fontFamily: "'Outfit', sans-serif"` written as a string literal 15× (`:638,654,770,806,862,897,929,965`…) instead of importing `OUTFIT`.
- Four transition durations, all with browser-default `ease` instead of `EASE`: `:527,577,594,795`.
- **`:614 + :982` — the CTA and the back button physically overlap** on Europe and Africa. Card at `{bottom:48,left:48}`, back button at `{bottom:32,left:48}` and ~36px tall, so it covers the card's lower 20px.
- **`:754-777` — a giant gold `0` is the largest element on the card.** The 46px/900 hero numeral is `activeCount`, defined at `:143-150` as conferences in session *right now*. On an ordinary day that is 0, printed above a real spotlight conference. The zero-state at `:721` only fires when there is also no spotlight, so "0 + spotlight" is the normal case.
- `:648` eyebrow reads "On the board" — never says what the board is, and it is the first text on the card.
- `:809-816` spotlight renders `full_name` at 13px with `nowrap`/ellipsis/`maxWidth:190`, chopping long names mid-word — while `acronym` is fetched at `:130` and used only for the logo monogram. The roles card does acronym-first correctly; the map does the opposite.
- `:806` "Spotlight" label is 12px/500 against a 13px/700 value — label competes with the thing it labels.
- **`:544-556` desktop map has zero hover feedback** — polygons are `fill="transparent" stroke="transparent"`. `hoveredDef`/`tooltipPos` are computed at `:447-448` and never rendered, so a tooltip was removed and nothing replaced it.
- `:336-343` `handleWheel` never checks `e.deltaY`, so scrolling *up* or a horizontal trackpad swipe fires an unskippable 1700ms cloud transition.
- `:883-914` desktop hint says "TAP A CONTINENT TO EXPLORE" (hidden on mobile via `hidden md:block`) while the actual gesture is hover-then-scroll, and `:876` teaches "SCROLL UP TO RETURN". Entry and exit taught with two different mental models.
- **`:544-556` the map is keyboard-unreachable** — polygons have `onClick` but no `tabIndex`, `role`, `aria-label` or `onKeyDown`. The accessible button list at `:952-974` is `md:hidden`, i.e. hidden from the users who need it.
- `:453-455` `100vw`/`100vh` — on iOS Safari `100vh` excludes the toolbar so bottom-anchored controls (`:918,947,982`) sit under browser chrome. Use `100dvh`, and `100%` for width.
- `:609-610` card is `minWidth:280` at fixed `left/right:48` → 328px overflows a 320px viewport. `getContinentLayout` (`:219-233`) has no responsive variant at all.
- `:918/:982` back controls at `left:48` sit above a mobile picker at `padding:'0 16px'` — two different left gutters stacked on one phone screen.
- `:953-973` mobile continent buttons have only `cursor:pointer` — no active, focus or hover, and ~41px tall (under the 44px touch minimum). `NeuButton` ships `scale(0.96)` on press.
- `:700-720` skeleton is two 20px bars standing in for a 46px numeral + 34px logo + two text lines, so the card visibly jumps. The CTA at `:828` renders live during loading.
- `:304-311` `Promise.all().then()` with **no `.catch`** — any network failure pins the skeleton forever. No error state on the page.
- `:301,312` the fetch does not start until the 1700ms timeout fires; minimum time-to-data is 1.7s + round-trip.
- `:563-579` fullscreen autoplaying video + always-on cursor-trail canvas (`:360-444`) with **no `prefers-reduced-motion` guard**, and no `onError` on the video (a 404 gives 1700ms of frozen screen).
- `:499-513` `SiteNav` wrapped in `rgba(27,56,40,0.72)` with injected CSS at `:481-482` recolouring only `a`/`button`. The wordmark `<img>`, avatar disc and credits chip keep ivory treatment on a dark forest bar.
- `:481` `.map-nav-wrapper a { color:#EDE7D8 !important }` also kills the nav's gold active-link indicator, on this page only.
- `:476-483` raw `<style>` tag defining globally-named `@keyframes pulse-skeleton`; roles uses Tailwind `animate-pulse` for the same job. Two skeleton systems on sibling pages.
- `:672-686` flag cluster hard-capped at 4 (`:168`) with no "+N more" — 30 countries and 4 render identically.
- `:672-686` flags are 22×15 at `borderRadius:2`; roles uses `FlagImg` square with no radius. Two flag treatments.
- `:828-846` seven radii inside one 250-line card: CTA 10, hint pills 24, spotlight chip 12, card 16, icon seat 10, skeleton bars 6, flags 2.
- `:828-846` CTA has no hover/focus/active at all, while the adjacent spotlight chip (`:797-798`) mutates border and transform imperatively. Two neighbouring interactive elements, opposite feedback.
- `:721-749` zero-state says "Be the first to bring MUN to {continent}" but is not a link; the only action is the CTA below, which lands on an empty explore page. Dead-end invitation.
- `:582-597` continent `<img>` has no `onError` and no placeholder — a missing asset shows the card floating on bare forest.
- `map/page.tsx:8` promises "view committees, dates, and fees". The map shows a count, a month·year and flags — no committees, no fees. No `loading.tsx` for the route (explore has one), so the heavy assets load against a blank forest screen.

## roles/ConferencesRolesClient.tsx

- **`:378-430` nothing leads the card.** Location, dates, other-roles count and posted+deadline all render at `12px` in near-identical browns. The deadline — the only time-critical fact — matches "Posted 3d ago" unless `deadlineDays <= 14` (`:270`).
- `:411` prints `postedAgo` *before* the deadline — least decision-relevant fact gets left-most position.
- `:409 vs :410` the Clock icon takes `deadlineColor` while adjacent text stays `#5A4F42` and only the trailing fragment (`:415`) gets urgency colour. Red icon + brown text + red text reads as a glitch.
- **`:374 + :477-490` compensation shows no amount and no currency** — just `PAID`/`TRAVEL COVERED`/`VOLUNTEER`. The figure lives in `compensation_note`, one click away behind "ROLE DETAILS" (`:435-449`).
- `:152-154` `fmtDeadline` gives "12 Mar" — **no year, no timezone**. March 2027 is indistinguishable from March 2026.
- **`:267-270` there is no expired state.** Negative `deadlineDays` leaves both urgency flags false, so a passed deadline renders calm grey with APPLY (`:310-319`) fully enabled. `.eq('is_open', true)` at `:896` is the only gate.
- `:103-116` no remote/in-person field and no experience level on the card — a user cannot tell whether a role needs a flight.
- `:1086-1150` filter bar is one `flex-wrap` row of search + six pills + clear + count. On a 375px phone that wraps to ~4 rows of sticky chrome pinned at `top-3`, eating the top third of the viewport. No filter sheet or collapse; the only mobile concession is hiding two 1px dividers.
- `:1084 vs :1155` filter bar is `px-4 md:px-10`, content is `px-6 md:px-14` — the filter card never lines up with the cards it filters.
- `:1133-1142` "CLEAR" is the least prominent element (10px/800 muted), placed after all six pills, and hovers to `DANGER` red — destructive styling for a non-destructive reset.
- `:1131-1149` no "N filters applied" summary; once scrolled past the wrap point the active pill may be off-screen.
- **`focus:outline-none` with no replacement ring nine times** in this file (`:1095,328,351,437,578,618,640,1135,1205`) plus `neu.tsx:534` (`NeuPill`) and `:578` (`NeuButton`) — so every pill and CTA on the page is affected. Grep for `focus-visible|focus:ring|outlineOffset` returns zero.
- `:351-354,437-440,1135-1138,699-701` hover implemented by imperatively mutating `e.currentTarget.style.color` — no `:focus-visible` parity, and any re-render mid-hover resets it. `:437-440` has to re-derive the resting colour from `expanded` in the leave handler.
- `:322-323` `<NeuCard hover>` lifts the whole card, but the card is not clickable — only the logo, acronym and button navigate. The lift promises a target that is not there.
- **`:1225-1234 vs :1213-1221` the board silently switches layout** between three category columns and a full-width card grid depending on how many categories have results. In column mode you cannot compare two roles side by side.
- `:1074-1078,516,1144-1148` three count readouts in three type treatments and three shadow depths for the same semantic.
- `:981+:1075-1077` `pad()` zero-pads, so an empty board reads "**00** OPEN ROLES · **00** CONFERENCES HIRING" — looks broken.
- `:1077` label "WITH REWARDS" collapses `paid` OR `travel-covered` (`:936-939`) into a word that states neither.
- `:59 vs :1106 vs :373` one concept, three labels on one screen: filter pill CHAIRS, group header CHAIRS, card chip CHAIRING.
- `:63,854` "SECRETARIAT"/"VOLUNTEER" with no explanation anywhere — no informational hover affordance on a public board aimed at newcomers.
- **`:167-175` the card fabricates requirements.** With no semicolons it splits on `,\s*`, so "Experience chairing GA, ECOSOC, and crisis committees" becomes three green-ticked bullets under "LOOKING FOR" (`:463`). `.slice(0,6)` truncates silently.
- **`:363-367` direct AGENTS.md UI RULE violation** — `conference_committees.name` renders raw at 11px in `#8A7D6C` with `truncate`. Must be ACRONYM + full name beneath via `committeeDisplayName`. `presetNames` is not imported in this file.
- `:358-360` conversely the conference `full_name` is truncated at 11.5px muted while the acronym gets 13.5/800 — for anyone who does not know the acronym, the only identifying line is the ellipsised one.
- `:297-308 vs :309-319` signed-out users get the **gold** "SIGN IN TO APPLY"; signed-in users get the quieter forest "APPLY". The auth detour outranks the conversion. Gold also means `chairs` category (`:59`) and "WITH REWARDS" (`:1077`) — three unrelated meanings.
- `:285-296` applied state uses a pressed-in `NeuInset` (the system's disabled treatment) with no link to the application, and `REJECTED` shows permanently in red on a public card with no dismiss.
- `:44` `DANGER = '#8B2020'` is a fifth accent with no NEU token, used for both rejection and clear-filters hover.
- Off-token colours at `:37,43,185,364,384,394,402,410,438,454,469,481,534,606,707,817,1067,1145,1161` — nine browns/greens. Card body text alone uses four (`#4A4238,#5A4F42,#6B5D4B,#8A7D6C`) with no rule distinguishing them.
- **Fifteen distinct font sizes** — 9, 9.5, 10, 11, 11.5, 12, 12.5, 13, 13.5, 14, 14.5, 15, 16, 18, 19. Several a half-pixel apart. No type scale.
- **Eight border radii** — 8, 11, 12, 14, 22, 26, 28, 999. `:1085` overrides `NeuCard`'s 22 to 26 with no rationale.
- `:1156-1181` skeleton always renders 3 columns × 2 cards but mobile is one column; and the placeholder blocks use `#EDE7D8`/`#F0EBDD` — the exact colours of the surfaces beneath, so `animate-pulse` is effectively invisible.
- `:1182-1212` the two empty states are asymmetric (one gets a `NeuButton` CTA, the other a bare underlined text link styled unlike anything else) and neither states which filters are active.
- **`:920-932 + :896-897` there is no sort control** — postings arrive `created_at desc` then get re-bucketed, so "closing soonest" is unreachable on a deadline-driven board. No `.limit()` either.
- `:922-928` search covers role name, conference fields and `requirements` but not `description` or `compensation_note` — searching "crisis" silently misses roles.
- `:559-565` `ApplyModal` uses `Portal` correctly, but has no `role="dialog"`, no `aria-modal`, no Escape handler, no focus trap, no scroll lock. Backdrop click at `:563` discards a typed cover note with no confirmation.
- `:655-662 → neu.tsx:583-590` disabled submit is a flat shadowless grey slab on a page where everything has a dual shadow — reads as a rendering failure.
- `:961-975` submitting gives no success confirmation; the modal just closes.
- `:707-711` LinkedIn icon is a grey `<span>` with `cursor:default` — reads as a broken link, not "coming soon".
- `:686-692` footer logo is `/GavellingLogo.png` recoloured through six chained CSS filter functions to approximate `#1B3828` instead of shipping a forest asset; `onError` hides it, leaving an empty cell in a 3-column footer.
- `:196,225,510,1184 → neu.tsx:79-123` every category chip, filter pill and card badge pulls a PNG from `cdn.jsdelivr.net` — an external CDN round-trip per glyph on a public marketing page.
- `:774-840` the `ComingSoonScreen` that actually ships has **no call to action at all** — no notify-me, no link to explore, no "list your conference" (which the gated empty state at `:1193-1196` does offer). Dead end for users who arrived with intent.
- `:784-788` `<img src="/WIP.png">` has no `onError` fallback (unlike the footer logo at `:691`).

## Incidental (functional, not UI)

- `MapClient.tsx:304-311` no `.catch` on the fetch; `:301,322` timeouts never cleared on unmount; `:366-367` canvas sized once with no resize listener; `:369,392-397,434` `getTerrainAt` point-in-polygon runs on every mousemove and the result is never read; `:447-448` dead `hoveredDef`/`tooltipPos`; `:342,355` wheel listeners without `{passive:true}`; `:154-208` unbounded conference + application queries with no `.limit()`.
- `ConferencesRolesClient.tsx:881-916` the fetch effect runs on every visit despite `JOB_BOARD_ENABLED === false` (early return is at `:997`, after the hooks) — the coming-soon screen fires 1–3 discarded Supabase queries.
- `ConferencesRolesClient.tsx:961-975` `setApplying(true)` precedes the `if (!session) return`, leaving a permanently disabled "SUBMITTING…" button; the `insert` is unchecked so a failed write reports success.
- `ConferencesRolesClient.tsx:598-599` a null `acronym` renders a stray leading `" · "` in the modal subtitle.
- `ConferencesRolesClient.tsx:148-150` `daysUntil` parses date-only ISO as UTC midnight — "closes in N days" is off by one west of UTC.
